================================================
FILE PATH: ./tools/check-source-parser.js
================================================

#!/usr/bin/env node
'use strict';

import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import vm from 'node:vm';

let input = '';
for await (const chunk of process.stdin) input += chunk;
const files = JSON.parse(input || '[]');
const failures = [];

for (const file of files) {
  try {
    let source = await readFile(file, 'utf8');
    if (source.startsWith('#!')) source = source.replace(/^#![^\n]*(?:\n|$)/, '\n');
    new vm.SourceTextModule(source, { identifier: file });
  } catch (error) {
    failures.push({ file, message: error?.message || String(error), stack: error?.stack });
  }
}

process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({ checked: files.length, failures }));


================================================
FILE PATH: ./tools/check-package-consumer.js
================================================

#!/usr/bin/env node
'use strict';

import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';

const root = process.cwd();
const temporary = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'rawnode-logger-consumer-'));

function run(command, args, options = {}) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const child = spawn(command, args, { cwd: options.cwd || root, env: { ...process.env, ...(options.env || {}) }, stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'] });
    let stdout = ''; let stderr = '';
    child.stdout.on('data', (chunk) => { stdout += chunk; });
    child.stderr.on('data', (chunk) => { stderr += chunk; });
    child.once('error', reject);
    child.once('close', (code) => {
      if (code === 0) resolve({ stdout, stderr });
      else reject(new Error(`${command} ${args.join(' ')} failed (${code})\n${stdout}\n${stderr}`));
    });
  });
}

try {
  const packed = await run('npm', ['pack', '--ignore-scripts', '--json', '--pack-destination', temporary]);
  const metadata = JSON.parse(packed.stdout);
  const tarball = path.join(temporary, metadata[0].filename);
  const consumer = path.join(temporary, 'consumer');
  await writeFile(path.join(temporary, 'package.json'), JSON.stringify({ private: true, type: 'module' }));
  await run('npm', ['install', '--ignore-scripts', '--no-audit', '--no-fund', tarball], { cwd: temporary });
  await writeFile(path.join(temporary, 'consumer.mjs'), `
    import defaultLogger, { createLogger, MemoryTransport, LevelRegistry } from 'rawnode-logger';
    import { SafeSerializer } from 'rawnode-logger/serialization';
    import { Redactor } from 'rawnode-logger/security';
    import { ChannelManager } from 'rawnode-logger/channels';
    import { LoggerRecorder } from 'rawnode-logger/testing';
    import { runDoctor } from 'rawnode-logger/console';
    import legacy from 'rawnode-logger/legacy';
    if (!defaultLogger || !legacy) throw new Error('default exports missing');
    if (new LevelRegistry().definitions().length !== 14) throw new Error('level contract missing');
    if (!(new SafeSerializer()) || !(new Redactor())) throw new Error('subpath constructors missing');
    const memory = new MemoryTransport();
    const logger = createLogger({ transports: [memory] });
    logger.info('installed-consumer');
    await logger.close();
    if (memory.all()[0].message !== 'installed-consumer') throw new Error('delivery failed');
    const channels = new ChannelManager({ default: 'memory', channels: { memory: { driver: 'memory' } } });
    channels.channel().info('channel');
    await channels.close();
    const recorder = new LoggerRecorder();
    recorder.logger.info('recorded');
    await recorder.close();
    const doctor = await runDoctor();
    if (!doctor.ok) throw new Error('doctor failed');
    process.stdout.write('consumer-ok');
  `);
  const result = await run(process.execPath, [path.join(temporary, 'consumer.mjs')], { cwd: temporary });
  if (result.stdout !== 'consumer-ok') throw new Error(`Unexpected consumer output: ${result.stdout}`);
  process.stdout.write(`Installed package consumer: OK (${path.basename(tarball)})\n`);
} finally {
  await rm(temporary, { recursive: true, force: true });
}

#!/usr/bin/env node
'use strict';
import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
const pkg = JSON.parse(await readFile(new URL('../package.json', import.meta.url), 'utf8'));
const fields = ['dependencies', 'optionalDependencies', 'peerDependencies'];
const violations = fields.filter((field) => pkg[field] && Object.keys(pkg[field]).length);
if (violations.length) {
  process.stderr.write(`Zero-dependency contract violated by: ${violations.join(', ')}\n`);
  process.exitCode = 2;
} else process.stdout.write('Zero-dependency contract: OK\n');

================================================
FILE PATH: ./tools/build-manifest.js
================================================

#!/usr/bin/env node
'use strict';

import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { readdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';

const root = process.cwd();
const ignored = new Set(['node_modules', '.git', 'MANIFEST.json']);
async function walk(directory, files = []) {
  const entries = await readdir(directory, { withFileTypes: true });
  entries.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
  for (const entry of entries) {
    if (ignored.has(entry.name)) continue;
    const absolute = path.join(directory, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) await walk(absolute, files);
    else if (entry.isFile()) files.push(absolute);
  }
  return files;
}
const entries = [];
let totalBytes = 0;
for (const absolute of await walk(root)) {
  const data = await readFile(absolute);
  totalBytes += data.length;
  entries.push({
    path: path.relative(root, absolute).split(path.sep).join('/'),
    bytes: data.length,
    sha256: createHash('sha256').update(data).digest('hex'),
  });
}
const manifest = { schemaVersion: 1, generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(), files: entries.length, totalBytes, entries };
await writeFile(path.join(root, 'MANIFEST.json'), `${JSON.stringify(manifest, null, 2)}\n`);
process.stdout.write(`Manifest written: ${entries.length} files, ${totalBytes} bytes\n`);


================================================
FILE PATH: ./tools/check-exports.js
================================================

#!/usr/bin/env node
'use strict';

import { readFile, access } from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';

const root = process.cwd();
const pkg = JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(root, 'package.json'), 'utf8'));
const requiredLegacy = [
  'Logger','AdvancedLogger','defaultLogger','logger','Config','colors','ConsoleTransport','FileTransport',
  'ObservableFileTransport','ObservableFileTransportEnhanced','ObservableConsoleTransport','ObservableConsoleTransportEnhanced',
];
const requiredEnterprise = [
  'createLogger','LoggerRuntime','LoggerBuilder','LoggerFacade','NullLogger','LevelRegistry','SafeSerializer','Redactor',
  'EnterpriseConsoleTransport','EnterpriseFileTransport','AuditFileTransport','MemoryTransport','RedisStreamTransport',
  'TcpTransport','UdpTransport','HttpBatchTransport','ChannelManager','LoggerServiceProvider','InspectorServer',
  'createHttpLoggingMiddleware','LoggerRecorder','runDoctor','runCli',
];
const failures = [];

const rootModule = await import(pathToFileURL(path.join(root, 'index.js')).href);
for (const name of [...requiredLegacy, ...requiredEnterprise]) if (!(name in rootModule)) failures.push(`root export missing: ${name}`);
if (!rootModule.default) failures.push('root default export missing');

for (const [subpath, definition] of Object.entries(pkg.exports || {})) {
  if (subpath === './*' || subpath === './package.json') continue;
  const target = typeof definition === 'string' ? definition : definition?.import;
  if (!target) continue;
  const absolute = path.resolve(root, target);
  try { await access(absolute); await import(pathToFileURL(absolute).href); }
  catch (error) { failures.push(`${subpath} -> ${target}: ${error.message}`); }
  const types = typeof definition === 'object' ? definition.types : null;
  if (types) {
    try { await access(path.resolve(root, types)); }
    catch { failures.push(`${subpath} types missing: ${types}`); }
  }
}

if (failures.length) {
  failures.forEach((failure) => process.stderr.write(`${failure}\n`));
  process.exitCode = 1;
} else process.stdout.write(`Checked ${Object.keys(pkg.exports || {}).length} package export entries and ${requiredLegacy.length + requiredEnterprise.length} public symbols: OK\n`);


================================================
FILE PATH: ./tools/check-source.js
================================================

#!/usr/bin/env node
'use strict';

import { readdir, readFile, access } from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';

const root = process.cwd();
const ignored = new Set(['node_modules', '.git']);
const importPattern = /(?:import\s+(?:[^'";]*?\s+from\s+)?|export\s+[^'";]*?\s+from\s+|import\s*\()\s*['"]([^'"]+)['"]/g;

async function walk(directory, files = []) {
  for (const entry of await readdir(directory, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    if (ignored.has(entry.name)) continue;
    const absolute = path.join(directory, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) await walk(absolute, files);
    else if (entry.isFile() && (entry.name.endsWith('.js') || entry.name.endsWith('.mjs'))) files.push(absolute);
  }
  return files;
}

async function resolveRelative(importer, specifier) {
  if (!specifier.startsWith('.')) return true;
  const base = path.resolve(path.dirname(importer), specifier);
  const candidates = path.extname(base)
    ? [base]
    : [base, `${base}.js`, `${base}.mjs`, path.join(base, 'index.js')];
  for (const candidate of candidates) {
    try { await access(candidate); return true; } catch {}
  }
  return false;
}

function parseAll(files) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const parser = path.join(root, 'tools/check-source-parser.js');
    const child = spawn(process.execPath, ['--experimental-vm-modules', parser], {
      stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
    });
    let stdout = '';
    let stderr = '';
    child.stdout.on('data', (chunk) => { stdout += chunk; });
    child.stderr.on('data', (chunk) => { stderr += chunk; });
    child.once('error', reject);
    child.once('close', (code) => {
      if (code !== 0) return reject(new Error(stderr || `Source parser exited with ${code}`));
      try { resolve(JSON.parse(stdout)); }
      catch (error) { reject(new Error(`Could not parse source-check output: ${error.message}\n${stdout}\n${stderr}`)); }
    });
    child.stdin.end(JSON.stringify(files));
  });
}

const files = (await walk(root)).sort();
const parsed = await parseAll(files);
const failures = parsed.failures.map((failure) => ({ type: 'syntax', ...failure }));

for (const file of files) {
  const source = await readFile(file, 'utf8');
  importPattern.lastIndex = 0;
  for (let match; (match = importPattern.exec(source));) {
    if (!(await resolveRelative(file, match[1]))) {
      failures.push({ type: 'import', file, specifier: match[1], message: 'Relative import target does not exist.' });
    }
  }
}

if (failures.length) {
  for (const failure of failures) {
    process.stderr.write(`${failure.type}: ${path.relative(root, failure.file)}${failure.specifier ? ` -> ${failure.specifier}` : ''}\n${failure.message || failure.stack || ''}\n`);
  }
  process.exitCode = 1;
} else {
  process.stdout.write(`Checked ${files.length} JavaScript modules and relative imports: OK\n`);
}

#!/usr/bin/env node
'use strict';
import { readdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';

async function walk(directory, files = []) {
  for (const entry of await readdir(directory, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const absolute = path.join(directory, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) await walk(absolute, files);
    else if (entry.isFile() && entry.name.endsWith('.test.js')) files.push(absolute);
  }
  return files;
}
const files = (await walk(path.resolve('tests'))).sort();
const child = spawn(process.execPath, ['--test', ...files], { stdio: 'inherit' });
child.once('close', (code) => { process.exitCode = code ?? 1; });

FILE PATH: ./logger.js

'use strict';

import Config from './src/submodules/config/index.js';
import AdvancedLogger from './src/submodules/advanced-logger/index.js';
import ObservableConsoleTransportEnhanced from './src/submodules/observable-console-transport-enhanced/index.js';

const { getLevelConfig } = Config;

const enhancedConsole = new ObservableConsoleTransportEnhanced({
  prefix: '[APP] ',
  suffix: ' [END]',
  formatter: (message, entry) => {
    const config = getLevelConfig(entry?.level);
    return config
      ? `${config.formatted(message)} [${entry?.timestamp || new Date().toISOString()}]`
      : `${message} [${entry?.timestamp || new Date().toISOString()}]`;
  },
});

const logger = new AdvancedLogger({
  level: 'debug',
  transports: [enhancedConsole],
});

export { enhancedConsole };
export default logger;

FILE PATH: ./config/logger.js

'use strict';

const bool = (name, fallback = false) => {
  const value = process.env[name];
  if (value == null || value === '') return fallback;
  return ['1', 'true', 'yes', 'y', 'on', 'enabled'].includes(String(value).trim().toLowerCase());
};

const int = (name, fallback) => {
  const value = Number(process.env[name]);
  return Number.isFinite(value) ? Math.trunc(value) : fallback;
};

export default {
  profile: process.env.LOG_PROFILE || (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? 'production' : 'v1'),
  level: process.env.LOG_LEVEL || 'info',
  channel: process.env.LOG_CHANNEL || 'stack',
  service: process.env.APP_NAME || 'rawnode',
  environment: process.env.APP_ENV || process.env.NODE_ENV || 'development',

  context: {
    enabled: process.env.LOG_CONTEXT !== 'false',
  },

  security: {
    redact: process.env.LOG_REDACT !== 'false',
    sanitize: process.env.LOG_SANITIZE !== 'false',
    replacement: process.env.LOG_REDACTION_REPLACEMENT || '[REDACTED]',
    paths: [],
  },

  queue: {
    maxItems: int('LOG_QUEUE_MAX_ITEMS', 20_000),
    maxBytes: int('LOG_QUEUE_MAX_BYTES', 64 * 1024 * 1024),
    overflow: process.env.LOG_QUEUE_OVERFLOW || 'drop-lowest',
    budgetMs: int('LOG_QUEUE_BUDGET_MS', 6),
    maxPerSlice: int('LOG_QUEUE_MAX_PER_SLICE', 1_000),
  },

  channels: {
    stack: {
      driver: 'stack',
      channels: ['console'],
      ignoreExceptions: false,
    },
    console: {
      driver: 'console',
      level: process.env.LOG_CONSOLE_LEVEL || undefined,
      colors: process.env.NO_COLOR ? false : true,
      structured: bool('LOG_CONSOLE_JSON', false),
    },
    daily: {
      driver: 'file',
      directory: process.env.LOG_DIRECTORY || 'storage/logs',
      filename: process.env.LOG_FILE || 'application.log',
      maxBytes: int('LOG_FILE_MAX_BYTES', 25 * 1024 * 1024),
      rotateIntervalMs: int('LOG_ROTATE_INTERVAL_MS', 24 * 60 * 60 * 1_000),
      compress: bool('LOG_COMPRESS', true),
      retention: {
        maxFiles: int('LOG_RETENTION_FILES', 14),
        maxAgeMs: int('LOG_RETENTION_AGE_MS', 14 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1_000),
        maxBytes: int('LOG_RETENTION_BYTES', 2 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024),
      },
    },
    audit: {
      driver: 'audit',
      directory: process.env.LOG_AUDIT_DIRECTORY || 'storage/logs/audit',
      filename: process.env.LOG_AUDIT_FILE || 'audit.ndjson',
      secret: process.env.LOG_AUDIT_SECRET || process.env.APP_KEY,
      keyId: process.env.LOG_AUDIT_KEY_ID || 'primary',
    },
  },

  processErrors: {
    enabled: bool('LOG_PROCESS_ERRORS', false),
    exitOnUncaught: bool('LOG_EXIT_ON_UNCAUGHT', false),
  },
};

'use strict';

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { LoggerRecorder, FakeClock } from '../../index.js';

test('LoggerRecorder and FakeClock provide deterministic first-party testing support', async () => {
  const clock = new FakeClock(Date.parse('2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z'));
  const recorder = new LoggerRecorder({ clock: clock.callback(), idFactory: () => 'record-id', level: 'important' });
  try {
    recorder.logger.info('hello');
    clock.tick(1000);
    recorder.logger.warn('careful');
    await recorder.flush();
    recorder.assertCount(2).assertLogged('info', 'hello').assertLogged('warn', /careful/).assertNotLogged('error');
    assert.equal(recorder.records()[0].id, 'record-id');
    assert.equal(recorder.records()[0].timeUnixMs, Date.parse('2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z'));
  } finally { await recorder.close(); }
});

FILE PATH: ./tests/unit/backpressure.test.js
================================================

'use strict';

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { createLogger } from '../../index.js';

test('bounded queues shed low-severity overflow while preserving critical records', async () => {
  const delivered = [];
  const slow = {
    name: 'slow',
    rawnodeEnterpriseTransport: true,
    async writeRecord(record) {
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 5));
      delivered.push(record);
    },
    async flush() {},
    async close() {},
  };
  const logger = createLogger({
    level: 'important',
    transports: [{
      transport: slow,
      options: {
        maxItems: 3,
        maxBytes: 1024 * 1024,
        maxOvercommitItems: 2,
        overflow: 'severity-aware',
      },
    }],
    pipelineQueue: { maxItems: 6, maxBytes: 1024 * 1024, overflow: 'severity-aware' },
  });
  try {
    for (let index = 0; index < 40; index++) logger.debug(`debug-${index}`);
    logger.critical('must-survive');
    await logger.flush();
    assert.ok(delivered.some((record) => record.message === 'must-survive'));
    const stats = logger.stats();
    const dropped = stats.pipeline.stats.dropped + stats.transports.reduce((sum, transport) => sum + transport.dropped, 0);
    assert.ok(dropped > 0);
  } finally { await logger.close(); }
});

test('transport failures are isolated and open a circuit breaker', async () => {
  let attempts = 0;
  const broken = {
    name: 'broken',
    rawnodeEnterpriseTransport: true,
    async writeRecord() { attempts++; throw Object.assign(new Error('offline'), { code: 'OFFLINE' }); },
    async flush() {},
    async close() {},
  };
  const logger = createLogger({
    level: 'debug',
    transports: [{ transport: broken, options: { failureThreshold: 2, circuitOpenMs: 10_000, maxRetries: 0 } }],
  });
  try {
    await assert.rejects(() => logger.logAsync('info', 'first'));
    await assert.rejects(() => logger.logAsync('info', 'second'));
    logger.info('third');
    await logger.flush();
    assert.equal(attempts, 2);
    assert.equal(logger.health().transports[0].circuitOpen, true);
    assert.ok(logger.diagnosticsSnapshot().some((entry) => /TRANSPORT/.test(entry.code)));
  } finally { await logger.close(); }
});


================================================
FILE PATH: ./tests/unit/runtime.test.js
================================================

'use strict';

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { createLogger, MemoryTransport } from '../../index.js';

async function withLogger(options, callback) {
  const memory = options.memory || new MemoryTransport({ maxItems: 10_000 });
  const logger = createLogger({ level: 'debug', transports: [memory], ...options });
  try { return await callback(logger, memory); }
  finally { await logger.close(); }
}

test('structured records include context, correlation, metadata, and child fields', async () => {
  await withLogger({ service: 'orders', environment: 'test' }, async (logger, memory) => {
    await logger.runWithContext({ requestId: 'req-1', traceId: 'trace-1' }, async () => {
      logger.child({ orderId: 'ord-1' }).info('created', { total: 42 });
    });
    await logger.flush();
    const record = memory.all()[0];
    assert.equal(record.service, 'orders');
    assert.equal(record.environment, 'test');
    assert.equal(record.requestId, 'req-1');
    assert.equal(record.traceId, 'trace-1');
    assert.equal(record.context.orderId, 'ord-1');
    assert.equal(record.data.total, 42);
    assert.equal(record.droppable, true);
    assert.equal(typeof record.id, 'string');
    assert.equal(typeof record.sequence, 'number');
    assert.match(logger.prometheus(), /rawnode_logger/);
  });
});

test('disabled lazy values are not evaluated', async () => {
  await withLogger({ level: 'info' }, async (logger, memory) => {
    let messageEvaluated = false;
    let dataEvaluated = false;
    logger.debug(() => { messageEvaluated = true; return 'expensive'; }, () => { dataEvaluated = true; return { expensive: true }; });
    await logger.flush();
    assert.equal(messageEvaluated, false);
    assert.equal(dataEvaluated, false);
    assert.equal(memory.all().length, 0);
  });
});

test('asynchronous processors preserve global record order', async () => {
  await withLogger({}, async (logger, memory) => {
    logger.use(async (record) => {
      if (record.message === 'one') await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 15));
      record.context.processed = true;
      return record;
    });
    logger.info('one');
    logger.child({ child: true }).info('two');
    logger.info('three');
    await logger.flush();
    assert.deepEqual(memory.all().map((record) => record.message), ['one', 'two', 'three']);
    assert.ok(memory.all().every((record) => record.context.processed));
  });
});

test('processor phases protect secrets and can enrich after redaction', async () => {
  await withLogger({}, async (logger, memory) => {
    let trustedSawSecret = false;
    let untrustedSawSecret = false;
    logger.processor((record) => {
      trustedSawSecret = record.data.password === 'super-secret';
      return record;
    }, { name: 'trusted', phase: 'before-redaction', trusted: true });
    logger.processor((record) => {
      untrustedSawSecret = record.data.password === 'super-secret';
      record.context.postProcessed = true;
      return record;
    }, { name: 'untrusted', phase: 'after-redaction', trusted: false });
    logger.info('login', { password: 'super-secret' });
    await logger.flush();
    assert.equal(trustedSawSecret, true);
    assert.equal(untrustedSawSecret, false);
    assert.equal(memory.all()[0].data.password, '[REDACTED]');
    assert.equal(memory.all()[0].context.postProcessed, true);
  });
});

test('custom levels are additive and retain delivery policy', async () => {
  await withLogger({}, async (logger, memory) => {
    logger.registerLevel({ name: 'audit', rank: 5.5, severity: 52, syslog: 5, droppable: false });
    logger.setLevel('debug');
    await logger.logAsync('audit', 'permission changed', { role: 'admin' });
    const record = memory.all()[0];
    assert.equal(record.level, 'audit');
    assert.equal(record.droppable, false);
    assert.equal(record.syslog, 5);
  });
});

test('timers, measure, once, at, when, sampling, and rate limiting are fluent', async () => {
  await withLogger({ random: () => 0.99 }, async (logger, memory) => {
    const timer = logger.time('operation');
    timer.stop('info', { ok: true });
    logger.once('boot').notice('booted');
    logger.once('boot').notice('booted again');
    logger.rateLimit('burst', { limit: 1, intervalMs: 1000 }).info('first');
    logger.rateLimit('burst', { limit: 1, intervalMs: 1000 }).info('second');
    logger.at('warn').log('fixed-level');
    logger.when(false).critical('disabled-critical');
    logger.sample(0).debug('sampled-out');
    await logger.measure('measured', async () => 42);
    await logger.flush();
    const messages = memory.all().map((record) => record.message);
    assert.equal(messages.filter((message) => message === 'booted').length, 1);
    assert.equal(messages.includes('booted again'), false);
    assert.equal(messages.filter((message) => message === 'first').length, 1);
    assert.equal(messages.includes('second'), false);
    assert.equal(messages.includes('fixed-level'), true);
    assert.equal(messages.includes('disabled-critical'), false);
    assert.equal(messages.includes('sampled-out'), false);
    assert.equal(messages.includes('operation'), true);
    assert.equal(messages.includes('measured'), true);
  });
});

test('request and job helpers propagate expected fields', async () => {
  await withLogger({}, async (logger, memory) => {
    logger.forRequest({
      id: 'req-7', method: 'POST', url: '/orders', headers: {},
      user: { id: 'user-3' }, tenantId: 'tenant-2', socket: { remoteAddress: '127.0.0.1' },
    }).info('request');
    logger.forJob({ id: 'job-1', queue: 'emails', attempt: 2, tenantId: 'tenant-2' }).info('job');
    await logger.flush();
    const [request, job] = memory.all();
    assert.equal(request.requestId, 'req-7');
    assert.equal(request.userId, 'user-3');
    assert.equal(request.context.method, 'POST');
    assert.equal(job.context.jobId, 'job-1');
    assert.equal(job.context.queue, 'emails');
  });
});

test('errors are normalized with causes and structured context', async () => {
  await withLogger({}, async (logger, memory) => {
    const cause = Object.assign(new Error('database offline'), { code: 'DB_DOWN' });
    const error = new Error('request failed', { cause });
    logger.error(error, { operation: 'checkout' });
    await logger.flush();
    const record = memory.all()[0];
    assert.equal(record.message, 'request failed');
    assert.equal(record.error.message, 'request failed');
    assert.equal(record.error.cause.message, 'database offline');
    assert.equal(record.error.cause.code, 'DB_DOWN');
    assert.equal(record.data.operation, 'checkout');
  });
});

test('plugin failures are isolated in production while valid hooks continue', async () => {
  await withLogger({}, async (logger, memory) => {
    logger.plugin({ name: 'broken', hooks: { beforeWrite() { throw new Error('plugin failed'); } } });
    logger.plugin({ name: 'good', priority: 20, hooks: { beforeWrite(record) { record.context.plugin = true; return record; } } });
    logger.info('plugin-test');
    await logger.flush();
    assert.equal(memory.all()[0].context.plugin, true);
    assert.ok(logger.diagnosticsSnapshot().some((entry) => entry.code === 'LOGGER_PLUGIN_ERROR'));
  });
});

test('metrics and health expose queue and transport state', async () => {
  await withLogger({}, async (logger) => {
    logger.info('health');
    await logger.flush();
    const stats = logger.stats();
    const health = logger.health();
    assert.ok(stats.counters.records_accepted_total >= 1);
    assert.equal(health.status, 'healthy');
    assert.equal(Array.isArray(health.transports), true);
    assert.equal(typeof stats.pipeline.queuedItems, 'number');
  });
});


test('metrics remains a callable severity while the registry remains observable', async () => {
  const transport = new MemoryTransport();
  const logger = createLogger({ level: 'important', transports: [transport] });
  try {
    assert.equal(typeof logger.metrics, 'function');
    assert.ok(logger.runtime.metricsRegistry);
    logger.metrics('queue depth', { value: 3 });
    await logger.flush();
    const [record] = transport.all();
    assert.equal(record.level, 'metrics');
    assert.equal(record.message, 'queue depth');
    assert.ok(logger.metricsSnapshot().counters.records_accepted >= 1);
  } finally { await logger.close(); }
});

  const transport = new MemoryTransport();
  const logger = createLogger({
    level: 'debug',
    transports: [transport],
    stateLimits: { maxOnceKeys: 2, maxRateBuckets: 2 },

  try {
    logger.once('a').info('a');
    logger.once('b').info('b');
    logger.once('c').info('c');
    logger.rateLimit('r1', { limit: 10, intervalMs: 60_000 }).info('r1');
    logger.rateLimit('r2', { limit: 10, intervalMs: 60_000 }).info('r2');
    logger.rateLimit('r3', { limit: 10, intervalMs: 60_000 }).info('r3');

    assert.ok(logger.runtime.sharedState.rateBuckets.size <= 2);

FILE PATH: ./tests/unit/queue.test.js

'use strict';

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { DeliveryQueue } from '../../index.js';

test('DeliveryQueue preserves order and cooperatively drains', async () => {
  const output = [];
  const queue = new DeliveryQueue({ maxItems: 10, maxBytes: 1024 * 1024, budgetMs: 1, maxPerSlice: 1 });
  for (let index = 0; index < 5; index++) {
    queue.enqueue(async () => {
      if (index === 0) await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 5));
      output.push(index);
    }, { bytes: 10, meta: { rank: 9, severity: 30, droppable: true } });
  }
  await queue.flush();
  assert.deepEqual(output, [0, 1, 2, 3, 4]);
  assert.equal(queue.snapshot().stats.completed, 5);
  await queue.close();
});

test('DeliveryQueue evicts low-priority records before high-priority records', async () => {
  const output = [];
  const queue = new DeliveryQueue({ maxItems: 2, maxBytes: 1024, overflow: 'severity-aware' });
  const gate = new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 20));
  queue.enqueue(async () => { await gate; output.push('active'); }, { bytes: 100, meta: { rank: 10, severity: 20, droppable: true } });
  queue.enqueue(async () => output.push('debug'), { bytes: 100, meta: { rank: 10, severity: 20, droppable: true } });
  const critical = queue.enqueue(async () => output.push('critical'), { bytes: 100, meta: { rank: 2, severity: 70, droppable: false } });
  await queue.flush();
  assert.equal((await critical.promise)?.dropped, undefined);
  assert.equal(output.includes('critical'), true);
  assert.ok(queue.snapshot().stats.evicted >= 1 || queue.snapshot().stats.overcommitted >= 1);
});

test('DeliveryQueue honors AbortSignal and flush timeout', async () => {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  controller.abort(new Error('cancelled'));
  const queue = new DeliveryQueue({ maxItems: 2, maxBytes: 1024, flushTimeoutMs: 5 });
  const aborted = queue.enqueue(async () => 1, { signal: controller.signal, bytes: 1 });
  assert.equal(aborted.accepted, false);
  const value = await aborted.promise;
  assert.equal(value.reason, 'aborted');
  await queue.close();
});

FILE PATH: ./tests/unit/transport-adapter.test.js

'use strict';

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { createLogger, MemoryTransport } from '../../index.js';

test('transport level and channel filters are enforced before delivery', async () => {
  const memory = new MemoryTransport();
  const logger = createLogger({
    level: 'important',
    transports: [{ transport: memory, options: { level: 'warn', channels: ['security'] } }],
  });
  try {
    logger.info('too-low', null, { channel: 'security' });
    logger.error('wrong-channel', null, { channel: 'application' });
    logger.error('delivered', null, { channel: 'security' });
    await logger.flush();
    assert.deepEqual(memory.all().map((record) => record.message), ['delivered']);
    assert.equal(logger.stats().transports[0].skipped, 2);
  } finally { await logger.close(); }
});

test('retry policy recovers transient failures and resets degraded state', async () => {
  let attempts = 0;
  const records = [];
  const transport = {
    name: 'recovering',
    rawnodeEnterpriseTransport: true,
    async writeRecord(record) {
      attempts++;
      if (attempts < 3) throw Object.assign(new Error('temporarily unavailable'), { code: 'EAGAIN' });
      records.push(record);
    },
    async flush() {},
    async close() {},
  };
  const logger = createLogger({
    transports: [{ transport, options: { maxRetries: 2, retryDelayMs: 1, retryMaxDelayMs: 2 } }],
  });
  try {
    await logger.logAsync('info', 'eventual');
    assert.equal(attempts, 3);
    assert.equal(records[0].message, 'eventual');
    const health = logger.health().transports[0];
    assert.equal(health.status, 'healthy');
    assert.equal(health.stats.retries, 2);
    assert.equal(health.stats.lastError, null);
  } finally { await logger.close(); }
});

test('transport ownership controls lifecycle without affecting flush', async () => {
  let externalClosed = 0;
  let ownedClosed = 0;
  let flushed = 0;
  const external = {
    name: 'external', rawnodeEnterpriseTransport: true,
    async writeRecord() {}, async flush() { flushed++; }, async close() { externalClosed++; },
  };
  const owned = {
    name: 'owned', rawnodeEnterpriseTransport: true,
    async writeRecord() {}, async flush() { flushed++; }, async close() { ownedClosed++; },
  };
  const logger = createLogger({
    transports: [
      { transport: external, options: { owned: false } },
      { transport: owned, options: { owned: true } },
    ],
  });
  logger.info('lifecycle');
  await logger.close();
  assert.ok(flushed >= 2);
  assert.equal(externalClosed, 0);
  assert.equal(ownedClosed, 1);
});

FILE PATH: ./tests/unit/channels-provider.test.js
================================================

'use strict';

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {
  ChannelManager,
  LoggerConfiguration,
  LoggerConfigurationError,
  LoggerServiceProvider,
  MemoryTransport,
  SwappableLogger,
  createLogger,
} from '../../index.js';

function storedRecords(channel) {
  const logger = channel?.logger || channel;
  const runtime = logger?.runtime || logger;
  return runtime.dispatcher.adapters[0].transport.all();
}

test('LoggerConfiguration produces immutable snapshots and actionable validation', () => {
  const configuration = new LoggerConfiguration({
    profile: 'production',
    level: 'debug',
    channel: 'memory',
    channels: { memory: { driver: 'memory' } },
  });
  assert.equal(configuration.get('profile'), 'production');
  assert.equal(configuration.get('channels.memory.driver'), 'memory');
  assert.equal(Object.isFrozen(configuration.value), true);
  const changed = configuration.with({ level: 'warn' });
  assert.equal(configuration.get('level'), 'debug');
  assert.equal(changed.get('level'), 'warn');

  assert.throws(() => new LoggerConfiguration({
    profile: 'not-real',
    channel: 'missing',
    channels: { console: { driver: 'console' } },
  }), LoggerConfigurationError);
});

test('ChannelManager resolves lazy channels, stacks, custom drivers, and cycle errors', async () => {
  const customMemory = new MemoryTransport();
  const manager = new ChannelManager({
    default: 'stack',
    base: { profile: 'production', level: 'debug' },
    channels: {
      memory: { driver: 'memory', maxItems: 20 },
      custom: { driver: 'custom' },
      stack: { driver: 'stack', channels: ['memory', 'custom'] },
      cycleA: { driver: 'stack', channels: ['cycleB'] },
      cycleB: { driver: 'stack', channels: ['cycleA'] },
    },
  });
  manager.extend('custom', () => customMemory);
  try {
    const stack = manager.channel();
    stack.info('stacked', { value: 1 });
    await stack.flush();
    assert.equal(storedRecords(manager.channel('memory'))[0].message, 'stacked');
    assert.equal(customMemory.all()[0].message, 'stacked');
    assert.throws(() => manager.channel('cycleA'), (error) => error.code === 'LOGGER_CHANNEL_CYCLE');
    assert.ok(manager.snapshot().drivers.includes('custom'));
    assert.equal(manager.health().status, 'healthy');
  } finally { await manager.close(); }
});

test('SwappableLogger keeps stable references while changing targets', async () => {
  const firstMemory = new MemoryTransport();
  const secondMemory = new MemoryTransport();
  const first = createLogger({ transports: [firstMemory] });
  const second = createLogger({ transports: [secondMemory] });
  const proxy = new SwappableLogger(first);
  try {
    proxy.info('first');
    await proxy.flush();
    assert.equal(firstMemory.all()[0].message, 'first');
    assert.equal(proxy.swap(second), first);
    proxy.info('second');
    await proxy.flush();
    assert.equal(secondMemory.all()[0].message, 'second');
  } finally {
    await Promise.allSettled([first.close(), second.close()]);
  }
});

test('LoggerServiceProvider binds a configuration-driven bootstrap-safe logger', async () => {
  const bindings = new Map();
  const app = {
    instance(key, value) { bindings.set(key, value); return value; },
    resolve(key) { return bindings.get(key); },
  };
  bindings.set('config', {
    get(key) {
      if (key === 'logger') return {
        profile: 'production',
        level: 'debug',
        channel: 'stack',
        service: 'provider-test',
        channels: {
          memory: { driver: 'memory', maxItems: 20 },
          stack: { driver: 'stack', channels: ['memory'] },
        },
      };
      if (key === 'app.name') return 'provider-test';
      return undefined;
    },
  });
  const provider = new LoggerServiceProvider(app);
  await provider.register();
  await provider.boot();
  try {
    const logger = app.resolve('logger');
    assert.ok(logger instanceof SwappableLogger);
    logger.info('provider-log');
    await logger.flush();
    const manager = app.resolve('logger.manager');
    assert.equal(storedRecords(manager.channel('memory'))[0].message, 'provider-log');
    assert.equal(app.resolve('logger.health').isHealthy(), true);
    assert.equal(app.resolve('logger.configuration').get('service'), 'provider-test');
  } finally { await provider.tearDown(); }
});

'use strict';

import test, { after } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {
  AdvancedLogger,
  Logger,
  Config,
  colors,
  defaultLogger,
  logger,
  ConsoleTransport,
  FileTransport,
  ObservableConsoleTransport,
  ObservableConsoleTransportEnhanced,
  ObservableFileTransport,
  ObservableFileTransportEnhanced,
  MemoryTransport,
  createLogger,
  createRuntimeLogger,
  NullLogger,
} from '../../index.js';

const levels = [
  'emergency', 'alert', 'critical', 'error', 'urgent', 'priority', 'warn',
  'notice', 'success', 'info', 'debug', 'metric', 'metrics', 'important',
];

const instances = [];
function track(instance) { instances.push(instance); return instance; }

after(async () => {
  await Promise.allSettled(instances.map((instance) => instance?.close?.()));
  await Promise.allSettled([logger?.close?.(), defaultLogger?.close?.()]);
});

test('v1 public exports and level methods remain available', async () => {
  assert.ok(defaultLogger);
  assert.ok(logger);
  assert.equal(typeof Config.getLevelConfig, 'function');
  assert.equal(typeof Config.setProfile, 'function');
  assert.equal(typeof colors.rgb, 'function');
  for (const Transport of [
    ConsoleTransport,
    FileTransport,
    ObservableConsoleTransport,
    ObservableConsoleTransportEnhanced,
    ObservableFileTransport,
    ObservableFileTransportEnhanced,
  ]) assert.equal(typeof Transport, 'function');

  const instance = track(new AdvancedLogger({ level: 'important', transports: [] }));
  for (const level of levels) assert.equal(typeof instance[level], 'function', level);
  assert.equal(instance.mode, 'json');
  assert.equal(instance.runtime.profile.name, 'v1');
});

test('legacy two-argument transports continue to receive level and formatted text', async () => {
  const captured = [];
  const transport = { write(level, formatted) { captured.push({ level, formatted }); } };
  const instance = track(new AdvancedLogger({ level: 'debug', mode: 'object', transports: [transport] }));
  instance.info('legacy transport', { value: 1 });
  await instance.flush();
  assert.equal(captured.length, 1);
  assert.equal(captured[0].level, 'info');
  assert.equal(typeof captured[0].formatted, 'string');
  assert.match(colors.strip ? colors.strip(captured[0].formatted) : captured[0].formatted, /legacy transport/);
});

test('one-argument custom transports preserve object and JSON modes', async () => {
  const objects = [];
  const json = [];
  const objectLogger = track(new AdvancedLogger({ level: 'debug', mode: 'object', transports: [{ write(value) { objects.push(value); } }] }));
  const jsonLogger = track(new AdvancedLogger({ level: 'debug', mode: 'json', transports: [{ write(value) { json.push(value); } }] }));
  objectLogger.info('object');
  jsonLogger.info('json');
  await Promise.all([objectLogger.flush(), jsonLogger.flush()]);
  assert.equal(typeof objects[0], 'object');
  assert.equal(typeof json[0], 'string');
  assert.equal(typeof JSON.parse(json[0]), 'object');
});

test('legacy middleware is ordered and log event payload remains compatibility-shaped', async () => {
  const captured = [];
  const events = [];
  const instance = track(new AdvancedLogger({ level: 'debug', mode: 'object', transports: [{ write(value) { captured.push(value); } }] }));
  instance.use(async (entry) => {
    if (String(entry.message).includes('first')) await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 10));
    entry.middleware = true;
    return entry;
  });
  instance.on('log', (entry) => events.push(entry));
  instance.info('first');
  instance.info('second');
  await instance.flush();
  assert.deepEqual(captured.map((entry) => colors.strip ? colors.strip(entry.message) : entry.message), ['first', 'second']);
  assert.ok(captured.every((entry) => entry.middleware === true));
  assert.equal(events.length, 2);
  assert.equal(events[0].middleware, true);
});

test('Logger.create fluent builder remains functional and additive', async () => {
  const captured = [];
  const instance = track(Logger.create()
    .level('debug')
    .mode('object')
    .transport({ write(value) { captured.push(value); } })
    .plugin({ name: 'test-plugin', hooks: {} })
    .done());
  instance.debug('builder works');
  await instance.flush();
  assert.equal(captured.length, 1);
});

test('new enterprise factory defaults to production while constructors remain v1', async () => {
  const memory = new MemoryTransport();
  const enterprise = track(createLogger({ level: 'debug', transports: [memory] }));
  const legacy = track(new AdvancedLogger({ level: 'debug', transports: [] }));
  assert.equal(enterprise.runtime.profile.name, 'production');
  assert.equal(enterprise.mode, 'object');
  assert.equal(enterprise.colors, false);
  assert.equal(legacy.runtime.profile.name, 'v1');
});

test('bare runtime factory is available for infrastructure code', async () => {
  const memory = new MemoryTransport();
  const runtime = track(createRuntimeLogger({ profile: 'production', level: 'debug', transports: [memory] }));
  await runtime.logAsync('info', 'runtime');
  assert.equal(memory.all()[0].message, 'runtime');
});

test('fire-and-forget methods preserve historical undefined returns', () => {
  const advanced = track(new AdvancedLogger({ level: 'debug', transports: [new MemoryTransport()] }));
  const simple = track(new Logger({ level: 'debug', transports: [new MemoryTransport()] }));
  assert.equal(advanced.info('advanced-return'), undefined);

  Config.setProfile('default');

  assert.equal(await disabled.logAsync('info', 'ignored'), null);

  assert.equal(disabled.time('x').stop(), null);
  await disabled.flush();
  await disabled.close();
  assert.equal(disabled.health().status, 'disabled');
});

  const { colors } = await import('../../index.js');

  assert.equal(colors.strip(styled), 'hello');

FILE PATH: ./tests/security/serializer-redaction.test.js
================================================

'use strict';

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { createLogger, MemoryTransport, SafeSerializer, Redactor } from '../../index.js';

test('safe serializer handles cycles, BigInt, maps, sets, errors, buffers, and hostile getters', () => {
  let getterInvoked = false;
  const value = {
    id: 1n,
    map: new Map([['a', 1]]),
    set: new Set([1, 2]),
    error: new Error('boom'),
    bytes: Buffer.from('hello'),
  };
  Object.defineProperty(value, 'secretGetter', { enumerable: true, get() { getterInvoked = true; throw new Error('must not run'); } });
  value.self = value;
  const serializer = new SafeSerializer({ getterPolicy: 'describe' });
  const normalized = serializer.normalize(value);
  assert.equal(getterInvoked, false);
  assert.equal(normalized.id, '1n');
  assert.match(normalized.self, /Circular/);
  assert.equal(normalized.map.type, 'Map');
  assert.equal(normalized.set.type, 'Set');
  assert.equal(normalized.error.message, 'boom');
  assert.equal(normalized.bytes.type, 'Buffer');
  assert.equal(normalized.secretGetter, '[Getter]');
  assert.doesNotThrow(() => JSON.parse(serializer.stringify(value)));
});

test('safe serializer enforces depth, key, array, string, and record bounds', () => {
  const serializer = new SafeSerializer({ maxDepth: 2, maxKeys: 5, maxArrayItems: 2, maxStringBytes: 8, maxRecordBytes: 256 });
  const normalized = serializer.normalize({
    text: 'this is a much longer string',
    values: [1, 2, 3, 4],
    deep: { one: { two: { three: true } } },
    extra1: 1,
    extra2: 2,
    extra3: 3,
  });
  assert.match(normalized.text, /truncated/);
  assert.match(normalized.values.at(-1), /more items/);
  assert.doesNotThrow(() => JSON.parse(serializer.stringify(normalized)));
});

test('redactor protects nested fields, bearer tokens, JWTs, URLs, controls, ANSI, and bidi characters', () => {
  const redactor = new Redactor({ enabled: true, sanitizeControls: true, stripAnsi: true });
  const output = redactor.redact({
    password: 'pw',
    nested: { authorization: 'Bearer abc.def.ghi' },
    message: '\u001b[31mhello\u001b[0m\nforged\u202E',
    url: 'https://user:pass@example.com/path?token=secret&safe=1',
    jwt: 'eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJzdWIiOiIxIn0.signature',
  });
  assert.equal(output.password, '[REDACTED]');
  assert.equal(output.nested.authorization, '[REDACTED]');
  assert.equal(output.message.includes('\u001b'), false);
  assert.equal(output.message.includes('\n'), false);
  assert.equal(output.message.includes('\\n'), true);
  assert.equal(output.message.includes('\u202E'), false);
  assert.match(output.url, /\[REDACTED\]/);
  assert.equal(output.jwt, '[REDACTED]');
});

test('production logging redacts secrets from records and formatted output', async () => {
  const memory = new MemoryTransport();
  const logger = createLogger({ level: 'debug', transports: [memory] });
  try {
    const circular = { password: 'super-secret', nested: { authorization: 'Bearer abc.def.ghi' }, count: 2n };
    circular.self = circular;
    logger.info('login\nforged-line', circular);
    await logger.flush();
    const record = memory.all()[0];
    assert.equal(record.data.password, '[REDACTED]');
    assert.equal(record.data.nested.authorization, '[REDACTED]');
    assert.equal(record.data.count, '2n');
    assert.match(record.data.self, /Circular/);
    assert.equal(record.message.includes('\n'), false);
    assert.equal(record.formatted.includes('super-secret'), false);
    assert.equal(JSON.stringify(record).includes('super-secret'), false);
  } finally { await logger.close(); }
});

test('redaction paths and allow-path exceptions are deterministic', () => {
  const redactor = new Redactor({
    enabled: true,
    paths: ['$.profile.email'],
    allowPaths: ['$.debug.token'],
    sanitizeControls: true,
  });
  const output = redactor.redact({ profile: { email: 'x@example.com' }, debug: { token: 'visible-in-debug' } });
  assert.equal(output.profile.email, '[REDACTED]');
  assert.equal(output.debug.token, 'visible-in-debug');
});

FILE PATH: ./tests/integration/file-transport.test.js

'use strict';

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { mkdtemp, readFile, readdir, stat, writeFile, symlink } from 'node:fs/promises';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { createLogger, EnterpriseFileTransport, AuditFileTransport } from '../../index.js';

test('file transport is lazy, asynchronous, rotatable, compressible, reopenable, and closeable', async () => {
  const directory = await mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'rawnode-logger-file-'));
  const transport = new EnterpriseFileTransport({
    logDirectory: directory,
    fileName: 'app.log',
    rotateInterval: 0,
    maxSize: 1024 * 1024,
    compressOnRotate: true,
  });
  assert.equal(transport.stream, null);
  const logger = createLogger({ level: 'debug', transports: [transport] });
  logger.info('first', { value: 1 });
  logger.error('second', { value: 2 });
  await logger.flush();
  const content = await readFile(path.join(directory, 'app.log'), 'utf8');
  assert.match(content, /first/);
  assert.match(content, /second/);
  const rotation = await transport.rotate();
  assert.ok(rotation.archive.endsWith('.gz'));
  assert.ok((await stat(rotation.archive)).size > 0);
  await transport.reopen();
  logger.info('after-reopen');
  await logger.flush();
  assert.match(await readFile(path.join(directory, 'app.log'), 'utf8'), /after-reopen/);
  await logger.close();
  const files = await readdir(directory);
  assert.ok(files.includes('app.log'));
  assert.ok(files.some((name) => name.endsWith('.gz')));
  assert.equal(transport.health().closed, true);
});

test('file retention limits archive count', async () => {
  const directory = await mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'rawnode-logger-retention-'));
  const transport = new EnterpriseFileTransport({
    logDirectory: directory,
    fileName: 'app.log',
    rotateInterval: 0,
    maxSize: 1024 * 1024,
    retention: { maxFiles: 2 },
  });
  const logger = createLogger({ level: 'debug', transports: [transport] });
  for (let index = 0; index < 4; index++) {
    logger.info(`rotation-${index}`);
    await logger.flush();
    await transport.rotate();
  }
  await logger.close();
  const files = (await readdir(directory)).filter((name) => name.startsWith('app-'));
  assert.ok(files.length <= 2, `expected <=2 archives, got ${files.length}`);
});

test('file transport refuses symbolic-link targets by default', async (t) => {
  if (process.platform === 'win32') return t.skip('symlink privileges vary on Windows');
  const directory = await mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'rawnode-logger-symlink-'));
  const target = path.join(directory, 'target.log');
  const link = path.join(directory, 'app.log');
  await writeFile(target, '');
  await symlink(target, link);
  const transport = new EnterpriseFileTransport({ logDirectory: directory, fileName: 'app.log', rotateInterval: 0 });
  await assert.rejects(() => transport.ready(), (error) => error.code === 'LOGGER_FILE_SYMLINK_DENIED');
  await transport.close().catch(() => {});
});

test('audit transport creates, resumes, verifies, and detects tampering in its HMAC chain', async () => {
  const directory = await mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'rawnode-logger-audit-'));
  const secret = 'audit-test-secret';
  const file = path.join(directory, 'audit.log');

  const firstTransport = new AuditFileTransport({ secret, logDirectory: directory, fileName: 'audit.log', rotateInterval: 0 });
  const firstLogger = createLogger({ level: 'debug', transports: [firstTransport] });
  firstLogger.notice('role assigned', { userId: 1, role: 'admin' });
  firstLogger.warn('policy changed', { policy: 'billing' });
  await firstLogger.close();

  const secondTransport = new AuditFileTransport({ secret, logDirectory: directory, fileName: 'audit.log', rotateInterval: 0 });
  const secondLogger = createLogger({ level: 'debug', transports: [secondTransport] });
  secondLogger.error('access denied', { userId: 2 });
  await secondLogger.close();

  const valid = await AuditFileTransport.verifyFile(file, { secret });
  assert.equal(valid.valid, true);
  assert.equal(valid.lines, 3);
  assert.equal(valid.sequence, 3);

  const content = await readFile(file, 'utf8');
  await writeFile(file, content.replace('role assigned', 'role removed'));
  const invalid = await AuditFileTransport.verifyFile(file, { secret });
  assert.equal(invalid.valid, false);
  assert.ok(invalid.failures.length >= 1);
});

FILE PATH: ./tests/integration/cli.test.js
================================================

'use strict';

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { mkdtemp, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';

const root = path.resolve(new URL('../..', import.meta.url).pathname);
const bin = path.join(root, 'bin/index.js');

function run(args, env = {}) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const child = spawn(process.execPath, [bin, ...args], {
      cwd: root,
      env: { ...process.env, ...env },
      stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
    });
    let stdout = '';
    let stderr = '';
    child.stdout.on('data', (chunk) => { stdout += chunk; });
    child.stderr.on('data', (chunk) => { stderr += chunk; });
    child.once('error', reject);
    child.once('close', (code) => resolve({ code, stdout, stderr }));
  });
}

test('CLI exposes version, doctor, health, levels, channels, and masked config', async () => {
  const version = await run(['version', '--json']);
  assert.equal(version.code, 0);
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(version.stdout).version, '1.1.0');

  const doctor = await run(['doctor', '--json']);
  assert.equal(doctor.code, 0, doctor.stderr || doctor.stdout);
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(doctor.stdout).ok, true);

  const health = await run(['health', '--json']);
  assert.equal(health.code, 0, health.stderr);
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(health.stdout).health.status, 'healthy');

  const levels = await run(['levels', '--json']);
  const definitions = JSON.parse(levels.stdout);
  assert.equal(definitions.length, 14);
  assert.equal(definitions[0].name, 'emergency');
  assert.equal(definitions.at(-1).name, 'important');

  const channels = await run(['channels', '--json']);
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(channels.stdout).default, 'stack');

  const config = await run(['config', '--json'], { LOG_AUDIT_SECRET: 'must-not-leak' });
  assert.equal(config.code, 0);
  assert.doesNotMatch(config.stdout, /must-not-leak/);
  assert.match(config.stdout, /\[REDACTED\]/);
});

test('CLI tail reads only the requested final records', async () => {
  const directory = await mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'rawnode-logger-cli-'));
  const file = path.join(directory, 'application.log');
  await writeFile(file, 'one\ntwo\nthree\nfour\n');
  const result = await run(['tail', file, '--lines', '2']);
  assert.equal(result.code, 0, result.stderr);
  assert.equal(result.stdout.trim(), 'three\nfour');
});


================================================
FILE PATH: ./tests/integration/inspector.test.js
================================================

'use strict';

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import net from 'node:net';
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { createLogger, InspectorTransport, InspectorServer } from '../../index.js';

function decodeFrame(buffer) {
  if (buffer.length < 2) return null;
  let length = buffer[1] & 0x7f;
  let offset = 2;
  if (length === 126) {
    if (buffer.length < 4) return null;
    length = buffer.readUInt16BE(2);
    offset = 4;
  } else if (length === 127) {
    if (buffer.length < 10) return null;
    length = Number(buffer.readBigUInt64BE(2));
    offset = 10;
  }
  if (buffer.length < offset + length) return null;
  return {
    value: JSON.parse(buffer.subarray(offset, offset + length).toString('utf8')),
    rest: buffer.subarray(offset + length),
  };
}

function connect(address, request) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const socket = net.createConnection({ host: address.address, port: address.port });
    let buffer = Buffer.alloc(0);
    const timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('inspector handshake timed out')), 1000);
    socket.once('error', reject);
    socket.on('data', (chunk) => {
      buffer = Buffer.concat([buffer, chunk]);
      const marker = buffer.indexOf('\r\n\r\n');
      if (marker === -1) return;
      clearTimeout(timer);
      const headers = buffer.subarray(0, marker + 4).toString('utf8');
      const rest = buffer.subarray(marker + 4);
      socket.removeAllListeners('data');
      resolve({ socket, headers, rest });
    });
    socket.once('connect', () => socket.write(request));
  });
}

test('inspector enforces path/token/origin and broadcasts redacted structured records', async () => {
  const transport = new InspectorTransport({ maxBuffer: 10 });
  const server = new InspectorServer(transport, {
    host: '127.0.0.1',
    port: 0,
    path: '/inspect',
    token: 'inspector-secret',
    origins: ['https://console.example'],
  });
  const address = await server.start();
  const key = randomBytes(16).toString('base64');
  const baseHeaders = [
    `Host: ${address.address}:${address.port}`,
    'Upgrade: websocket',
    'Connection: Upgrade',
    `Sec-WebSocket-Key: ${key}`,
    'Sec-WebSocket-Version: 13',
    'Origin: https://console.example',
  ];

  const denied = await connect(address, [`GET /inspect HTTP/1.1`, ...baseHeaders, '', ''].join('\r\n'));
  assert.match(denied.headers, /401 Unauthorized/);
  denied.socket.destroy();

  const accepted = await connect(address, [`GET /inspect?token=inspector-secret HTTP/1.1`, ...baseHeaders, '', ''].join('\r\n'));
  assert.match(accepted.headers, /101 Switching Protocols/);

  const logger = createLogger({ level: 'debug', transports: [{ transport, options: { owned: false } }] });
  try {
    let buffered = accepted.rest;
    const recordPromise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const deadline = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('inspector record timed out')), 1000);
      const consume = () => {
        const frame = decodeFrame(buffered);
        if (!frame) return false;
        buffered = frame.rest;
        if (frame.value.type === 'record') {
          clearTimeout(deadline);
          resolve(frame.value.record);
          return true;
        }
        return consume();
      };
      if (!consume()) accepted.socket.on('data', (chunk) => { buffered = Buffer.concat([buffered, chunk]); consume(); });
    });

    logger.info('inspected', { password: 'must-not-leak', ok: true });
    await logger.flush();
    const record = await recordPromise;
    assert.equal(record.message, 'inspected');
    assert.equal(record.data.password, '[REDACTED]');
    assert.equal(record.data.ok, true);
    assert.equal(transport.getBufferedLogs().length, 1);
  } finally {
    accepted.socket.destroy();
    await logger.close();
    await server.close();
    await transport.close();
  }
});

test('inspector history is bounded by both item count and bytes and returns defensive clones', async () => {
  const transport = new InspectorTransport({ maxBuffer: 10, maxBufferBytes: 1400 });
  const logger = createLogger({ transports: [{ transport, options: { owned: false } }] });
  try {
    for (let index = 0; index < 20; index++) logger.info(`record-${index}`, { payload: 'x'.repeat(80) });
    await logger.flush();
    const records = transport.getBufferedLogs();
    assert.ok(records.length < 20);
    assert.ok(transport.bufferBytes <= transport.maxBufferBytes);
    if (records.length) {
      const original = transport.getBufferedLogs({ clone: false })[0].message;
      records[0].message = 'mutated';
      assert.equal(transport.getBufferedLogs({ clone: false })[0].message, original);
    }
    assert.ok(transport.health().stats.dropped > 0);
  } finally {
    await logger.close();
    await transport.close();
  }
});

FILE PATH: ./tests/integration/process-lifecycle.test.js
================================================

'use strict';

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import path from 'node:path';
import { ProcessErrorHandler } from '../../index.js';

test('ProcessErrorHandler attachment is explicit, idempotent, and reversible', async () => {
  const before = {
    uncaughtException: process.listenerCount('uncaughtException'),
    unhandledRejection: process.listenerCount('unhandledRejection'),
    warning: process.listenerCount('warning'),
  };
  const calls = [];
  const logger = {
    async logAsync(level, message, data, options) { calls.push({ level, message, data, options }); },
    async flush() { calls.push({ flush: true }); },
  };
  const handler = new ProcessErrorHandler(logger, {
    uncaughtException: true,
    unhandledRejection: true,
    warning: true,
    flushTimeoutMs: 100,
  });
  handler.attach().attach();
  assert.equal(process.listenerCount('uncaughtException'), before.uncaughtException + 1);
  assert.equal(process.listenerCount('unhandledRejection'), before.unhandledRejection + 1);
  assert.equal(process.listenerCount('warning'), before.warning + 1);
  await handler._report('error', 'synthetic', new Error('boom'), { source: 'test' });
  assert.equal(calls[0].level, 'error');
  assert.equal(calls.at(-1).flush, true);
  handler.detach();
  assert.equal(process.listenerCount('uncaughtException'), before.uncaughtException);
  assert.equal(process.listenerCount('unhandledRejection'), before.unhandledRejection);
  assert.equal(process.listenerCount('warning'), before.warning);
});

test('import, log, flush, and close leave no refed logger handles', async () => {
  const root = path.resolve(new URL('../..', import.meta.url).pathname);
  const script = `
    import { createLogger, MemoryTransport } from ${JSON.stringify(path.join(root, 'index.js'))};
    const logger = createLogger({ transports: [new MemoryTransport()] });
    logger.info('open-handle-check');
    await logger.close();
    process.stdout.write('closed');
  `;
  const result = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const child = spawn(process.execPath, ['--input-type=module', '-e', script], { stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'] });
    let stdout = ''; let stderr = '';
    const timer = setTimeout(() => { child.kill('SIGKILL'); reject(new Error(`child did not exit: ${stderr}`)); }, 3000);
    child.stdout.on('data', (chunk) => { stdout += chunk; });
    child.stderr.on('data', (chunk) => { stderr += chunk; });
    child.once('error', reject);
    child.once('close', (code) => { clearTimeout(timer); resolve({ code, stdout, stderr }); });
  });
  assert.equal(result.code, 0, result.stderr);
  assert.equal(result.stdout, 'closed');
});

FILE PATH: ./tests/integration/framework-integrations.test.js
================================================

'use strict';

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';
import {
  createLogger,
  MemoryTransport,
  LoggerServiceProvider,
  createHttpLoggingMiddleware,
  EventLoggingBridge,
  ChannelManager,
} from '../../index.js';

class FakeApplication {
  constructor(configuration) {
    this.bindings = new Map();
    this.configuration = configuration;
    this.instance('config', {
      get: (key) => {
        if (key === 'logger') return this.configuration.logger;
        if (key === 'app.name') return 'integration-app';
        if (key === 'app.env') return 'test';
        return undefined;
      },
    });
  }
  instance(key, value) { this.bindings.set(key, value); return value; }
  resolve(key) { return this.bindings.get(key); }
}

test('LoggerServiceProvider builds stack channels, aliases bindings, and tears down cleanly', async () => {
  const app = new FakeApplication({
    logger: {
      profile: 'production',
      level: 'debug',
      channel: 'stack',
      channels: {
        stack: { driver: 'stack', channels: ['memory'] },
        memory: { driver: 'memory', maxItems: 100 },
      },
      processErrors: { enabled: false },
    },
  });
  const provider = new LoggerServiceProvider(app);
  const logger = await provider.register();
  await provider.boot();
  try {
    assert.equal(app.resolve('logger'), logger);
    assert.equal(app.resolve('log'), logger);
    assert.equal(app.resolve('Logger'), logger);
    const manager = app.resolve('logger.channels');
    assert.ok(manager instanceof ChannelManager);

    logger.info('provider-log', { tenantId: 'tenant-1' });
    await logger.flush();
    const memoryLogger = manager.channel('memory');
    await memoryLogger.flush();
    const transport = memoryLogger.runtime.dispatcher.adapters[0].transport;
    assert.equal(transport.all()[0].message, 'provider-log');
    assert.equal(transport.all()[0].service, 'integration-app');
  } finally {
    await provider.tearDown();
  }
  assert.equal(app.resolve('logger.channels').snapshot().closed, true);
});

test('HTTP logging middleware propagates request context and completion timing', async () => {
  const memory = new MemoryTransport();
  const logger = createLogger({ level: 'debug', transports: [memory] });
  const middleware = createHttpLoggingMiddleware(logger, { requestIdFactory: () => 'generated-request-id' });
  const req = {
    method: 'POST',
    url: '/orders',
    headers: { 'user-agent': 'rawnode-test' },
    socket: { remoteAddress: '127.0.0.1' },
    tenantId: 'tenant-7',
    user: { id: 'user-3' },
  };
  const res = new EventEmitter();
  res.statusCode = 201;
  res.headers = new Map();
  res.setHeader = (name, value) => res.headers.set(String(name).toLowerCase(), value);

  try {
    const result = await middleware(req, res, async () => {
      assert.equal(logger.getContext().requestId, 'generated-request-id');
      res.emit('finish');
      return 'done';
    });
    assert.equal(result, 'done');
    await logger.flush();
    assert.equal(res.headers.get('x-request-id'), 'generated-request-id');
    const [received, completed] = memory.all();
    assert.equal(received.message, 'HTTP request received');
    assert.equal(completed.message, 'HTTP request completed');
    assert.equal(received.requestId, 'generated-request-id');
    assert.equal(received.tenantId, 'tenant-7');
    assert.equal(received.userId, 'user-3');
    assert.equal(completed.data.statusCode, 201);
    assert.equal(typeof completed.data.durationMs, 'number');
  } finally { await logger.close(); }
});

test('EventLoggingBridge attaches, maps, and detaches without leaking listeners', async () => {
  const events = new EventEmitter();
  const memory = new MemoryTransport();
  const logger = createLogger({ level: 'debug', transports: [memory] });
  const bridge = new EventLoggingBridge(events, logger, { channel: 'events' });
  try {
    bridge.listen('order:paid', 'notice', (order) => ({ orderId: order.id, amount: order.amount }));
    events.emit('order:paid', { id: 'ord-1', amount: 42 });
    await logger.flush();
    assert.equal(memory.all()[0].channel, 'events');
    assert.equal(memory.all()[0].data.orderId, 'ord-1');
    bridge.close();
    assert.equal(events.listenerCount('order:paid'), 0);
  } finally { bridge.close(); await logger.close(); }
});


================================================
FILE PATH: ./tests/integration/network-transports.test.js
================================================

'use strict';

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import net from 'node:net';
import dgram from 'node:dgram';
import http from 'node:http';
import {
  createLogger,
  TcpTransport,
  UdpTransport,
  HttpBatchTransport,
  RedisStreamTransport,
} from '../../index.js';

async function listen(server, ...args) {
  await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    server.once('error', reject);
    server.listen(...args, resolve);
  });
  return server.address();
}

async function closeServer(server) {
  if (!server) return;
  await new Promise((resolve) => server.close(resolve));
}

test('TCP transport sends newline-delimited structured records with backpressure support', async () => {
  const received = [];
  const server = net.createServer((socket) => {
    let buffer = '';
    socket.setEncoding('utf8');
    socket.on('data', (chunk) => {
      buffer += chunk;
      let index;
      while ((index = buffer.indexOf('\n')) !== -1) {
        const line = buffer.slice(0, index);
        buffer = buffer.slice(index + 1);
        if (line) received.push(JSON.parse(line));
      }
    });
  });
  const address = await listen(server, 0, '127.0.0.1');
  const transport = new TcpTransport({ host: '127.0.0.1', port: address.port });
  const logger = createLogger({ level: 'debug', transports: [transport] });
  try {
    logger.info('tcp-one');
    logger.warn('tcp-two');
    await logger.flush();
    const deadline = Date.now() + 500;
    while (received.length < 2 && Date.now() < deadline) {
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 5));
    }
    assert.deepEqual(received.map((record) => record.message), ['tcp-one', 'tcp-two']);
  } finally {
    await logger.close();
    await closeServer(server);
  }
});

test('UDP transport sends bounded datagrams and truncates oversized records', async () => {
  const socket = dgram.createSocket('udp4');
  const messages = [];
  socket.on('message', (message) => messages.push(message));
  await new Promise((resolve) => socket.bind(0, '127.0.0.1', resolve));
  const address = socket.address();
  const transport = new UdpTransport({ host: '127.0.0.1', port: address.port, maxPacketBytes: 512, oversize: 'truncate' });
  const logger = createLogger({ level: 'debug', transports: [transport] });
  try {
    logger.info('udp-one');
    logger.warn('x'.repeat(4000));
    await logger.flush();
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 20));
    assert.equal(messages.length, 2);
    assert.ok(messages.every((message) => message.length <= 512));
    assert.equal(JSON.parse(messages[0]).message, 'udp-one');
    assert.equal(JSON.parse(messages[1]).truncated, true);
  } finally {
    await logger.close();
    await new Promise((resolve) => socket.close(resolve));
  }
});

test('HTTP batch transport batches records and retries transient responses', async () => {
  const bodies = [];
  let requests = 0;
  const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
    const chunks = [];
    req.on('data', (chunk) => chunks.push(chunk));
    req.on('end', () => {
      requests++;
      bodies.push(JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8')));
      if (requests === 1) { res.statusCode = 503; res.end('retry'); }
      else { res.statusCode = 202; res.end('accepted'); }
    });
  });
  const address = await listen(server, 0, '127.0.0.1');
  const transport = new HttpBatchTransport({
    url: `http://127.0.0.1:${address.port}/logs`,
    batchSize: 3,
    maxRetries: 1,
    retryDelayMs: 1,
  });
  const logger = createLogger({
    level: 'debug',
    transports: [{ transport, options: { batchSize: 3 } }],
  });
  try {
    logger.info('one');
    logger.info('two');
    logger.info('three');
    await logger.flush();
    assert.equal(requests, 2);
    assert.equal(bodies.at(-1).records.length, 3);
    assert.deepEqual(bodies.at(-1).records.map((record) => record.message), ['one', 'two', 'three']);
  } finally {
    await logger.close();
    await closeServer(server);
  }
});

test('Redis Streams transport uses native XADD-compatible client methods', async () => {
  const calls = [];
  const client = { async xadd(...args) { calls.push(args); return '1-0'; } };
  const transport = new RedisStreamTransport(client, { stream: 'rawnode:test:logs', maxLength: 1000 });
  const logger = createLogger({ level: 'debug', transports: [transport] });
  try {
    logger.info('redis-one', { tenantId: 'tenant-1' });
    await logger.flush();
    assert.equal(calls.length, 1);
    assert.equal(calls[0][0], 'rawnode:test:logs');
    assert.ok(calls[0].includes('MAXLEN'));
    assert.ok(calls[0].includes('payload'));
  } finally { await logger.close(); }
});

FILE PATH: ./benchmarks/enterprise/runner.js
================================================

'use strict';

import { performance, monitorEventLoopDelay } from 'node:perf_hooks';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { createLogger, MemoryTransport, NullTransport, EnterpriseFileTransport, SafeSerializer } from '../../index.js';

function numeric(value, fallback) {
  const number = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(number) && number > 0 ? Math.trunc(number) : fallback;
}

async function measure(name, iterations, operation, cleanup = null) {
  const startCpu = process.cpuUsage();
  const startHeap = process.memoryUsage().heapUsed;
  const started = performance.now();
  await operation();
  const elapsedMs = performance.now() - started;
  const cpu = process.cpuUsage(startCpu);
  const endHeap = process.memoryUsage().heapUsed;
  await cleanup?.();
  return {
    name,
    iterations,
    elapsedMs: Number(elapsedMs.toFixed(3)),
    operationsPerSecond: Math.round(iterations / Math.max(0.001, elapsedMs) * 1000),
    cpuMs: Number(((cpu.user + cpu.system) / 1000).toFixed(3)),
    heapDeltaBytes: endHeap - startHeap,
  };
}

export async function runBenchmarks(options = {}) {
  const iterations = numeric(options.iterations, 100_000);
  const acceptedIterations = Math.min(iterations, numeric(options.acceptedIterations, 25_000));
  const serializerIterations = Math.min(iterations, numeric(options.serializerIterations, 50_000));
  const results = [];
  const delay = monitorEventLoopDelay({ resolution: 10 });
  delay.enable();
  await new Promise((resolve) => setImmediate(resolve));

  const disabled = createLogger({ profile: 'production', level: 'info', transports: [new NullTransport()] });
  results.push(await measure('disabled-level-fast-path', iterations, async () => {
    for (let index = 0; index < iterations; index++) disabled.debug('disabled', { index });
  }, () => disabled.close()));

  const memory = new MemoryTransport({ maxItems: Math.max(acceptedIterations, 1000), clone: false });
  const accepted = createLogger({ profile: 'production', level: 'debug', transports: [memory] });
  results.push(await measure('accepted-structured-records', acceptedIterations, async () => {
    for (let index = 0; index < acceptedIterations; index++) accepted.info('accepted', { index, ok: true });
    await accepted.flush();
  }, () => accepted.close()));

  const serializer = new SafeSerializer();
  const object = { id: 42n, nested: { values: [1, 2, 3], token: 'secret' }, error: new Error('benchmark') };
  object.self = object;
  results.push(await measure('safe-serialization', serializerIterations, async () => {
    for (let index = 0; index < serializerIterations; index++) serializer.stringify(object);
  }));

  if (options.file === true) {
    const file = path.join(tmpdir(), `rawnode-logger-bench-${process.pid}-${Date.now()}.log`);
    const transport = new EnterpriseFileTransport({ logDirectory: path.dirname(file), fileName: path.basename(file), rotateInterval: 0, durability: 'none' });
    const logger = createLogger({ profile: 'production', level: 'info', transports: [transport] });
    const fileIterations = Math.min(acceptedIterations, 10_000);
    results.push(await measure('file-transport-delivery', fileIterations, async () => {
      for (let index = 0; index < fileIterations; index++) logger.info('file', { index });
      await logger.flush();
    }, async () => { await logger.close(); await rm(file, { force: true }); }));
  }

  await new Promise((resolve) => setImmediate(resolve));
  delay.disable();
  return {
    schemaVersion: 1,
    node: process.version,
    platform: process.platform,
    architecture: process.arch,
    iterations,
    results,
    eventLoopDelayMs: {
      min: Number((delay.min / 1e6).toFixed(3)),
      max: Number((delay.max / 1e6).toFixed(3)),
      mean: Number((delay.mean / 1e6).toFixed(3)),
      p99: Number((delay.percentile(99) / 1e6).toFixed(3)),
    },
  };
}

async function main() {
  const report = await runBenchmarks({
    iterations: process.env.LOG_BENCH_ITERATIONS,
    file: process.argv.includes('--file'),
  });
  if (process.argv.includes('--json')) process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(report, null, 2)}\n`);
  else {
    console.table(report.results);
    console.log('Event-loop delay:', report.eventLoopDelayMs);
  }
}

if (import.meta.url === new URL(`file://${process.argv[1]}`).href) {
  main().catch((error) => { process.stderr.write(`${error.stack || error}\n`); process.exitCode = 1; });
}

// benchmarks/pino.js
"use strict";

import fs from "fs";
import { now, cpuUsage } from "./utils.js";

export default async function benchPino(iterations = 200_000) {
    const stream = fs.createWriteStream("/tmp/pino.log", { flags: "w" });

    const startCPU = process.cpuUsage();
    const start = now();
    for (let i = 0; i < iterations; i++) {
        stream.write(JSON.stringify({ level: 30, msg: "Benchmark test message" }) + "\n");
    }
    stream.end();
    const end = now();
    const cpu = cpuUsage(startCPU);

    return {
        name: "Pino (simulated)",
        iterations,
        duration_ms: end - start,
        logs_per_sec: (iterations / (end - start)) * 1000,
        cpu_ms: cpu,
    };
}

        .level("debug")
        .transport(new ConsoleTransport())
        .done();

    const start = now();

        log.debug("Benchmark test message");
    }
    const end = now();
    const cpu = cpuUsage(startCPU);

    return {
        name: "RawNodeLogger",
        iterations,
        duration_ms: end - start,

// benchmarks/runner.js
"use strict";

import benchPino from "./pino.js";
import benchWinston from "./winston.js";
import benchBunyan from "./bunyan.js";
import benchLaravel from "./laravel.js";
import { measureEventLoopDelay, printResults } from "./utils.js";

async function runAll() {
    console.log("Running RawNode Logger Benchmarks...\n");

    const results = [];

    results.push(await benchRawNode());
    results.push(await benchPino());
    results.push(await benchWinston());
    results.push(await benchBunyan());
    results.push(await benchLaravel());

    printResults("Logger Throughput", results);

    const delay = await measureEventLoopDelay(500);
    printResults("Event Loop Delay (500ms sample)", [delay]);
}

runAll();

================================================
FILE PATH: ./benchmarks/utils.js
================================================

// benchmarks/utils.js
"use strict";

import { performance, PerformanceObserver } from "perf_hooks";
import process from "process";

export function now() {
    return performance.now();
}

export function measureEventLoopDelay(duration = 1000) {
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
        const start = now();
        let min = Infinity;
        let max = 0;

        function tick() {
            const h = now() - start;
            const delay = h - Math.floor(h);
            if (delay < min) min = delay;
            if (delay > max) max = delay;

            if (h >= duration) {
                resolve({ min, max });
            } else {
                setImmediate(tick);
            }
        }
        tick();
    });
}

export function cpuUsage(start) {
    const diff = process.cpuUsage(start);
    return (diff.user + diff.system) / 1000; // ms
}

export function printResults(title, results) {
    console.log(`\n===== ${title} =====`);
    console.table(results);
}

FILE PATH: ./src/compatibility/v1/LegacyRecordFactory.js
