'use strict';

import formatLog from '../../submodules/formatter/formatLog.js';
import buildLogEntry from '../../submodules/formatter/buildLogEntry.js';
import colors from '../../submodules/colors/index.js';

const endTimestamp = colors.rgb(184, 197, 209);
const plain = (record) => {
  let formatted = `[${record.timestamp}] [${String(record.level).toUpperCase()}] ${record.message}`;
  if (record.data != null) {
    let data;
    try { data = typeof record.data === 'string' ? record.data : JSON.stringify(record.data); }
    catch { data = String(record.data); }
    formatted += ` => ${data}`;
  }
  return { timestamp: record.timestamp, level: record.level, message: record.message, formatted, data: record.data };
};

export class LegacyRecordFactory {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.colors = options.colors !== false;
    this.serialize = options.serialize === true;
    this.style = options.style || 'advanced';
  }

  create(record) {
    if (!this.colors || this.style === 'simple') return plain(record);
    const data = record.data == null ? record.data : record.data;
    try {
      const formatter = formatLog({ timestamp: record.timestamp, level: record.level, message: record.message, data });
      const formatted = this.serialize ? JSON.stringify(formatter) : formatter;
      return buildLogEntry({
        timestamp: endTimestamp(record.timestamp),
        level: record.level,
        message: record.message,
        formatted,
        data: colors.green(data),
        color: colors.white,
      });
    } catch {
      return plain(record);
    }
  }
}

export default LegacyRecordFactory;

'use strict';

const BASE = Object.freeze({
  name: 'v1',
  strictLevels: false,
  legacyFormatting: true,
  legacyTransportPayload: 'json',
  isolateProcessorErrors: true,
  isolatePluginErrors: true,
  redact: false,
  sanitizeControls: false,
  stripAnsi: false,
  boundedQueues: true,
  queueOverflow: 'drop-oldest',
  awaitTransportDelivery: false,
  autoUnref: true,
  metrics: false,
  context: true,
  freezeRecords: false,
  lazyDefaultTransports: true,
});

export const COMPATIBILITY_PROFILES = Object.freeze({
  v1: BASE,
  strict: Object.freeze({
    ...BASE,
    name: 'strict',
    strictLevels: true,
    legacyFormatting: false,
    legacyTransportPayload: 'object',
    redact: true,
    sanitizeControls: true,
    stripAnsi: true,
    metrics: true,
    freezeRecords: true,
  }),
  production: Object.freeze({
    ...BASE,
    name: 'production',
    strictLevels: true,
    legacyFormatting: false,
    legacyTransportPayload: 'object',
    redact: true,
    sanitizeControls: true,
    stripAnsi: true,
    queueOverflow: 'severity-aware',
    metrics: true,
    freezeRecords: true,
  }),
  test: Object.freeze({
    ...BASE,
    name: 'test',
    strictLevels: true,
    legacyFormatting: false,
    legacyTransportPayload: 'object',
    redact: true,
    sanitizeControls: true,
    stripAnsi: true,
    metrics: true,
    freezeRecords: true,
    autoUnref: true,
  }),
  serverless: Object.freeze({
    ...BASE,
    name: 'serverless',
    strictLevels: true,
    legacyFormatting: false,
    legacyTransportPayload: 'object',
    redact: true,
    sanitizeControls: true,
    stripAnsi: true,
    queueOverflow: 'severity-aware',
    metrics: true,
    freezeRecords: true,
    lazyDefaultTransports: true,
  }),
});

export function resolveCompatibilityProfile(profile = 'v1', overrides = {}) {
  const supplied = profile && typeof profile === 'object' ? profile : null;
  const requestedName = typeof profile === 'string' ? profile : supplied?.name || 'v1';
  const preset = COMPATIBILITY_PROFILES[requestedName] || COMPATIBILITY_PROFILES.v1;
  return Object.freeze({
    ...preset,
    ...(supplied || {}),
    ...(overrides || {}),
    name: requestedName,
  });
}

export default resolveCompatibilityProfile;


================================================
FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/records/RecordFactory.js
================================================

'use strict';

import os from 'node:os';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { threadId, isMainThread } from 'node:worker_threads';

function valueOrCall(value) {
  return typeof value === 'function' ? value() : value;
}

function ownData(value, key) {
  try {
    const descriptor = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(value, key);
    return descriptor && 'value' in descriptor ? descriptor.value : undefined;
  } catch { return undefined; }
}

function messageText(value, serializer) {
  if (value === undefined) return '';
  if (typeof value === 'string') return value;
  if (value === null) return 'null';
  if (typeof value === 'object') return serializer.stringify(value);
  try { return String(value); } catch { return '[Unprintable]'; }
}

export function resolveMessageAndError(message, data, options = {}, serializer) {
  let resolvedMessage = valueOrCall(message);
  let resolvedData = valueOrCall(data);
  let error = options.error || null;

  if (resolvedMessage instanceof Error) {
    error = resolvedMessage;
    resolvedMessage = resolvedMessage.message || resolvedMessage.name;
  } else if (resolvedData instanceof Error) {
    error = resolvedData;
    resolvedData = null;
  } else if (resolvedMessage && typeof resolvedMessage === 'object' && !Array.isArray(resolvedMessage)) {
    const candidate = resolvedMessage;
    const candidateError = ownData(candidate, 'error') || ownData(candidate, 'err');
    const candidateMessage = ownData(candidate, 'message') ?? ownData(candidate, 'msg');
    if (candidateError instanceof Error) error = candidateError;
    if (candidateMessage !== undefined) {
      resolvedMessage = candidateMessage;
      if (resolvedData == null) {
        const normalized = serializer.normalize(candidate);
        if (normalized && typeof normalized === 'object') {
          delete normalized.message;
          delete normalized.msg;
          delete normalized.error;
          delete normalized.err;
          resolvedData = Object.keys(normalized).length ? normalized : null;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return {
    message: messageText(resolvedMessage, serializer),
    data: resolvedData,
    error,
  };
}

export class RecordFactory {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    if (!options.serializer) throw new TypeError('RecordFactory requires a serializer.');
    if (!options.errorNormalizer) throw new TypeError('RecordFactory requires an error normalizer.');
    this.serializer = options.serializer;
    this.errorNormalizer = options.errorNormalizer;
    this.clock = options.clock || (() => Date.now());
    this.hrtime = options.hrtime || (() => process.hrtime.bigint());
    this.idFactory = options.idFactory || randomUUID;
    this.hostname = options.hostname || os.hostname();
    this.service = options.service || process.env.APP_NAME || process.env.SERVICE_NAME || 'rawnode';
    this.environment = options.environment || process.env.APP_ENV || process.env.NODE_ENV || 'development';
    this.sequence = Number(options.sequence) || 0;
    this.schemaVersion = Number(options.schemaVersion) || 1;
  }

  create(input = {}) {
    const now = input.timeUnixMs == null ? Number(this.clock()) : Number(input.timeUnixMs);
    const sequence = ++this.sequence;
    const resolved = resolveMessageAndError(input.message, input.data, input, this.serializer);
    const context = this.serializer.normalize(input.context || {});
    const data = resolved.data == null ? null : this.serializer.normalize(resolved.data);
    const error = resolved.error ? this.errorNormalizer.normalize(resolved.error) : null;
    const levelDefinition = input.levelDefinition || {};

    return {
      schemaVersion: this.schemaVersion,
      id: this.idFactory(),
      sequence,
      timestamp: new Date(now).toISOString(),
      timeUnixMs: now,
      hrtimeNs: String(input.hrtimeNs == null ? this.hrtime() : input.hrtimeNs),
      level: input.level,
      levelValue: levelDefinition.severity ?? null,
      levelRank: levelDefinition.rank ?? null,
      category: levelDefinition.category || 'custom',
      syslog: levelDefinition.syslog ?? null,
      consoleMethod: levelDefinition.consoleMethod || 'log',
      droppable: levelDefinition.droppable !== false,
      message: resolved.message,
      data,
      context,
      error,
      channel: input.channel || 'app',
      service: input.service || this.service,
      environment: input.environment || this.environment,
      hostname: this.hostname,
      pid: process.pid,
      threadId: isMainThread ? 0 : threadId,
      requestId: input.requestId ?? context.requestId ?? context.request_id ?? null,
      correlationId: input.correlationId ?? context.correlationId ?? context.correlation_id ?? null,
      traceId: input.traceId ?? context.traceId ?? context.trace_id ?? null,
      spanId: input.spanId ?? context.spanId ?? context.span_id ?? null,
      tenantId: input.tenantId ?? context.tenantId ?? context.tenant_id ?? null,
      userId: input.userId ?? context.userId ?? context.user_id ?? null,
      tags: Array.isArray(input.tags) ? input.tags.map(String) : [],
      formatted: '',
    };
  }
}

export default RecordFactory;

FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/records/ErrorNormalizer.js

'use strict';

export class ErrorNormalizer {
  constructor(serializer, options = {}) {
    this.serializer = serializer;
    this.maxCauseDepth = Math.max(0, Number(options.maxCauseDepth) || 8);
    this.includeStack = options.includeStack !== false;
  }

  normalize(error, depth = 0) {
    if (error == null) return null;
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) {
      return {
        name: 'NonErrorThrown',
        message: typeof error === 'string' ? error : 'A non-Error value was thrown.',
        value: this.serializer.normalize(error),
      };
    }

    const output = {
      name: error.name || 'Error',
      message: error.message || '',
    };
    if (this.includeStack && error.stack) output.stack = error.stack;
    if (error.code !== undefined) output.code = this.serializer.normalize(error.code);
    if (error.status !== undefined) output.status = this.serializer.normalize(error.status);
    if (error.statusCode !== undefined) output.statusCode = this.serializer.normalize(error.statusCode);
    if (error.details !== undefined) output.details = this.serializer.normalize(error.details);
    if (error instanceof AggregateError) {
      output.errors = Array.from(error.errors || []).map((item) => (
        depth < this.maxCauseDepth ? this.normalize(item, depth + 1) : { name: 'MaxCauseDepth', message: 'Cause chain truncated.' }
      ));
    }
    if (error.cause !== undefined) {
      output.cause = depth < this.maxCauseDepth
        ? this.normalize(error.cause, depth + 1)
        : { name: 'MaxCauseDepth', message: 'Cause chain truncated.' };
    }
    return output;
  }
}

export default ErrorNormalizer;

'use strict';

export { default as ContextManager, globalContextManager, globalContextManager as defaultContextManager } from './ContextManager.js';


================================================
FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/context/ContextManager.js
================================================

'use strict';

import { AsyncLocalStorage, AsyncResource } from 'node:async_hooks';

const BLOCKED_KEYS = new Set(['__proto__', 'prototype', 'constructor']);
const EMPTY = Object.freeze(Object.create(null));

function copyContext(value) {
  const output = Object.create(null);
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') return output;
  for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(value)) {
    if (!BLOCKED_KEYS.has(key)) output[key] = item;
  }
  return output;
}

export class ContextManager {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.enabled = options.enabled !== false;
    this.storage = new AsyncLocalStorage();
    this.freeze = options.freeze !== false;
  }

  get() {
    if (!this.enabled) return EMPTY;
    return this.storage.getStore() || EMPTY;
  }

  current() { return this.get(); }

  merge(context = {}) {
    const merged = { ...this.get(), ...copyContext(context) };
    return this.freeze ? Object.freeze(merged) : merged;
  }

  run(context, callback, ...args) {
    if (typeof callback !== 'function') throw new TypeError('ContextManager.run requires a callback.');
    if (!this.enabled) return callback(...args);
    return this.storage.run(this.merge(context), callback, ...args);
  }

  enterWith(context) {
    if (!this.enabled) return this.get();
    const merged = this.merge(context);
    this.storage.enterWith(merged);
    return merged;
  }

  bind(callback, context = this.get()) {
    if (typeof callback !== 'function') throw new TypeError('ContextManager.bind requires a callback.');
    const resource = new AsyncResource('RawNodeLoggerContext');
    const manager = this;
    return function boundLoggerContext(...args) {
      return manager.run(context, () => resource.runInAsyncScope(callback, this, ...args));
    };
  }

  createRequestContext(req = {}, extra = {}) {
    const headers = req.headers || {};
    const user = req.user || req.auth || null;
    return this.merge({
      requestId: req.id || req.requestId || headers['x-request-id'] || headers['x-correlation-id'] || null,
      correlationId: req.correlationId || headers['x-correlation-id'] || null,
      traceId: req.traceId || headers['x-trace-id'] || null,
      spanId: req.spanId || headers['x-span-id'] || null,
      tenantId: req.tenantId || req.tenant?.id || headers['x-tenant-id'] || null,
      userId: user?.id || user?._id || user?.sub || null,
      method: req.method || null,
      path: (typeof req.path === 'function' ? req.path() : req.path) || req.url || null,
      route: req.route?.name || req.route?.path || req.routeName || null,
      protocol: req.protocol || (req.socket?.encrypted ? 'https' : 'http'),
      remoteAddress: req.ip || req.socket?.remoteAddress || null,
      ...extra,
    });
  }

  createJobContext(job = {}, extra = {}) {
    return this.merge({
      jobId: job.id || job.jobId || null,
      queue: job.queue || job.queueName || null,
      attempt: job.attempt || job.attempts || null,
      maxAttempts: job.maxAttempts || job.tries || null,
      tenantId: job.tenantId || job.tenant_id || null,
      traceId: job.traceId || job.trace_id || null,
      correlationId: job.correlationId || job.correlation_id || null,
      ...extra,
    });
  }

  disable() {
    this.enabled = false;
    this.storage.disable();
  }
}

export const globalContextManager = new ContextManager();
export default ContextManager;

'use strict';

import { performance } from 'node:perf_hooks';

function yieldToLoop() {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setImmediate(resolve));
}

function createQueueError(code, message, details = null) {
  const error = new Error(message);
  error.code = code;
  if (details) error.details = details;
  return error;
}

function finiteInteger(value, fallback, minimum = 0) {
  const number = Number(value);
  return Number.isSafeInteger(number) && number >= minimum ? number : fallback;
}

/**
 * Ordered, cooperative, byte-bounded async queue.
 *
 * Guarantees:
 * - tasks execute in enqueue order;
 * - both queued and active work count toward memory limits;
 * - lower-priority/droppable work is evicted before higher-severity work;
 * - non-droppable work is never silently discarded;
 * - emergency overcommit is itself bounded to prevent a failing sink from
 *   converting the logger into an unbounded memory queue.
 */
export class DeliveryQueue {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.name = options.name || 'logger-pipeline';
    this.maxItems = finiteInteger(options.maxItems, 10_000, 1);
    this.maxBytes = finiteInteger(options.maxBytes, 64 * 1024 * 1024, 1_024);
    this.maxOvercommitItems = finiteInteger(
      options.maxOvercommitItems,
      Math.max(16, Math.ceil(this.maxItems * 0.05)),
      0,
    );
    this.maxOvercommitBytes = finiteInteger(
      options.maxOvercommitBytes,
      Math.max(1024 * 1024, Math.ceil(this.maxBytes * 0.10)),
      0,
    );
    this.overflow = options.overflow || 'severity-aware';
    this.budgetMs = Math.max(1, Number(options.budgetMs) || 6);
    this.maxPerSlice = finiteInteger(options.maxPerSlice, 1_000, 1);
    this.flushTimeoutMs = finiteInteger(options.flushTimeoutMs, 30_000, 0);
    this.onDrop = typeof options.onDrop === 'function' ? options.onDrop : null;
    this.onError = typeof options.onError === 'function' ? options.onError : null;
    this.onEmergency = typeof options.onEmergency === 'function' ? options.onEmergency : null;

    this.queue = [];
    this.queuedBytes = 0;
    this.active = 0;
    this.activeBytes = 0;
    this.overcommitItems = 0;
    this.overcommitBytes = 0;
    this.draining = false;
    this.accepting = true;
    this.flushWaiters = new Set();
    this.stats = {
      accepted: 0,
      completed: 0,
      failed: 0,
      dropped: 0,
      evicted: 0,
      overcommitted: 0,
      emergencyFallbacks: 0,
      aborted: 0,
      highWaterItems: 0,
      highWaterBytes: 0,
      maxQueueLatencyMs: 0,
    };
  }

  enqueue(task, options = {}) {
    if (typeof task !== 'function') throw new TypeError('DeliveryQueue.enqueue requires a function.');
    const bytes = Math.max(0, Number(options.bytes) || 0);
    const meta = options.meta || Object.create(null);
    const signal = options.signal || meta.signal || null;
    let resolve;
    let reject;
    const promise = new Promise((res, rej) => { resolve = res; reject = rej; });
    // Fire-and-forget logging callers should never trigger process-level
    // unhandled-rejection warnings; explicit callers still receive the same promise.
    promise.catch(() => {});

    if (!this.accepting) {
      reject(createQueueError('LOGGER_QUEUE_CLOSED', `Queue [${this.name}] is closed.`));
      return { accepted: false, promise, reason: 'closed' };
    }
    if (signal?.aborted) {
      this.stats.aborted++;
      resolve({ dropped: true, reason: 'aborted', queue: this.name });
      return { accepted: false, promise, reason: 'aborted' };
    }

    const room = this._makeRoom(bytes, meta);
    if (!room.accepted) {
      const result = { dropped: true, reason: room.reason || 'overflow', queue: this.name };
      this.stats.dropped++;
      try { this.onDrop?.(result, meta); } catch {}
      if (this.overflow === 'throw' || this.overflow === 'reject') {
        reject(createQueueError('LOGGER_QUEUE_OVERFLOW', `Queue [${this.name}] is full.`, this.snapshot()));
      } else {
        resolve(result);
      }
      return { accepted: false, promise, reason: result.reason };
    }

    if (room.emergencyFallback) {
      this.stats.accepted++;
      this.stats.emergencyFallbacks++;
      Promise.resolve()
        .then(() => options.emergency?.(meta) ?? this.onEmergency?.(meta))
        .then((value) => resolve({ delivered: true, emergencyFallback: true, value }), reject);
      return { accepted: true, promise, emergencyFallback: true, overcommitted: false };
    }

    const overcommitted = room.overcommitted === true;
    if (overcommitted) {
      this.stats.overcommitted++;
      this.overcommitItems++;
      this.overcommitBytes += bytes;
    }

    const item = {
      task,
      bytes,
      meta,
      signal,
      resolve,
      reject,
      overcommitted,
      enqueuedAt: performance.now(),
    };
    this.queue.push(item);
    this.queuedBytes += bytes;
    this.stats.accepted++;
    this._captureHighWater();
    this._schedule();
    return { accepted: true, promise, overcommitted };
  }

  _captureHighWater() {
    this.stats.highWaterItems = Math.max(this.stats.highWaterItems, this.queue.length + this.active);
    this.stats.highWaterBytes = Math.max(this.stats.highWaterBytes, this.queuedBytes + this.activeBytes);
  }

  _fits(bytes) {
    return (this.queue.length + this.active) < this.maxItems
      && (this.queuedBytes + this.activeBytes + bytes) <= this.maxBytes;
  }

  _canOvercommit(bytes) {
    return this.overcommitItems < this.maxOvercommitItems
      && this.overcommitBytes + bytes <= this.maxOvercommitBytes;
  }

  _makeRoom(bytes, meta) {
    if (this._fits(bytes)) return { accepted: true };

    const canEvict = ['drop-oldest', 'drop-lowest', 'severity-aware'].includes(this.overflow);
    if (canEvict) {
      while (!this._fits(bytes) && this.queue.length) {
        const index = this._evictionIndex();
        if (index < 0) break;
        const [item] = this.queue.splice(index, 1);
        this.queuedBytes -= item.bytes;
        if (item.overcommitted) {
          this.overcommitItems = Math.max(0, this.overcommitItems - 1);
          this.overcommitBytes = Math.max(0, this.overcommitBytes - item.bytes);
        }
        this.stats.dropped++;
        this.stats.evicted++;
        const result = { dropped: true, reason: 'overflow-evicted', queue: this.name };
        item.resolve(result);
        try { this.onDrop?.(result, item.meta); } catch {}
      }
      if (this._fits(bytes)) return { accepted: true };
    }

    if (meta?.droppable === false) {
      if (this._canOvercommit(bytes)) return { accepted: true, overcommitted: true };
      // There is no safe in-memory space left. The queue does not silently lose
      // critical work; it invokes the synchronous/direct emergency path instead.
      return { accepted: true, emergencyFallback: true };
    }

    return { accepted: false, reason: 'overflow' };
  }

  _evictionIndex() {
    if (this.overflow === 'drop-oldest') {
      return this.queue.findIndex((item) => item?.meta?.droppable !== false);
    }

    // Larger rank / smaller normalized severity means lower importance. On a
    // tie, evict the oldest item to keep behavior deterministic.
    let selected = -1;
    let selectedRank = -Infinity;
    let selectedSeverity = Infinity;
    for (let index = 0; index < this.queue.length; index++) {
      const item = this.queue[index];
      if (item?.meta?.droppable === false) continue;
      const rank = Number.isFinite(Number(item?.meta?.rank)) ? Number(item.meta.rank) : Infinity;
      const severity = Number.isFinite(Number(item?.meta?.severity)) ? Number(item.meta.severity) : -Infinity;
      if (selected < 0 || rank > selectedRank || (rank === selectedRank && severity < selectedSeverity)) {
        selected = index;
        selectedRank = rank;
        selectedSeverity = severity;
      }
    }
    return selected;
  }

  _schedule() {
    if (this.draining || !this.queue.length) return;
    this.draining = true;
    queueMicrotask(() => this._drain().catch((error) => {
      this.draining = false;
      try { this.onError?.(error, { queue: this.name, phase: 'drain' }); } catch {}
      if (this.queue.length) this._schedule();
      else this._resolveFlushWaiters();
    }));
  }

  async _drain() {
    let sliceStart = performance.now();
    let processed = 0;
    try {
      while (this.queue.length) {
        const item = this.queue.shift();
        this.queuedBytes -= item.bytes;
        if (item.overcommitted) {
          this.overcommitItems = Math.max(0, this.overcommitItems - 1);
          this.overcommitBytes = Math.max(0, this.overcommitBytes - item.bytes);
        }

        if (item.signal?.aborted) {
          this.stats.aborted++;
          item.resolve({ dropped: true, reason: 'aborted', queue: this.name });
          continue;
        }

        this.active++;
        this.activeBytes += item.bytes;
        this.stats.maxQueueLatencyMs = Math.max(this.stats.maxQueueLatencyMs, performance.now() - item.enqueuedAt);
        try {
          const value = await item.task();
          this.stats.completed++;
          item.resolve(value);
        } catch (error) {
          this.stats.failed++;
          try { this.onError?.(error, item.meta); } catch {}
          item.reject(error);
        } finally {
          this.active--;
          this.activeBytes = Math.max(0, this.activeBytes - item.bytes);
        }

        processed++;
        if (processed >= this.maxPerSlice || performance.now() - sliceStart >= this.budgetMs) {
          processed = 0;
          sliceStart = performance.now();
          await yieldToLoop();
        }
      }
    } finally {
      this.draining = false;
      if (this.queue.length) this._schedule();
      else this._resolveFlushWaiters();
    }
  }

  _resolveFlushWaiters() {
    if (this.draining || this.active || this.queue.length) return;
    for (const waiter of this.flushWaiters) waiter.resolve();
    this.flushWaiters.clear();
  }

  async flush(options = {}) {
    if (!this.draining && !this.active && this.queue.length === 0) return;
    const timeoutMs = options.timeoutMs == null
      ? this.flushTimeoutMs
      : Math.max(0, Number(options.timeoutMs) || 0);
    let timer = null;
    await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const waiter = {
        resolve: () => {
          if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
          this.flushWaiters.delete(waiter);
          resolve();
        },
      };
      this.flushWaiters.add(waiter);
      if (timeoutMs > 0) {
        timer = setTimeout(() => {
          this.flushWaiters.delete(waiter);
          reject(createQueueError(
            'LOGGER_FLUSH_TIMEOUT',
            `Queue [${this.name}] flush timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms.`,
            this.snapshot(),
          ));
        }, timeoutMs);
       
      }
      this._schedule();
    });
  }

  async close(options = {}) {
    if (!this.accepting) {
      await this.flush(options);
      return;
    }
    this.accepting = false;
    await this.flush(options);
  }

  destroy(reason = 'destroyed') {
    this.accepting = false;
    while (this.queue.length) {
      const item = this.queue.shift();
      this.queuedBytes -= item.bytes;
      if (item.overcommitted) {
        this.overcommitItems = Math.max(0, this.overcommitItems - 1);
        this.overcommitBytes = Math.max(0, this.overcommitBytes - item.bytes);
      }
      this.stats.dropped++;
      item.resolve({ dropped: true, reason, queue: this.name });
    }
    this._resolveFlushWaiters();
  }

  snapshot() {
    return {
      name: this.name,
      accepting: this.accepting,
      draining: this.draining,
      active: this.active,
      activeBytes: this.activeBytes,
      queuedItems: this.queue.length,
      queuedBytes: this.queuedBytes,
      overcommitItems: this.overcommitItems,
      overcommitBytes: this.overcommitBytes,
      maxItems: this.maxItems,
      maxBytes: this.maxBytes,
      maxOvercommitItems: this.maxOvercommitItems,
      maxOvercommitBytes: this.maxOvercommitBytes,
      overflow: this.overflow,
      stats: { ...this.stats },
    };
  }
}

export default DeliveryQueue;

'use strict';

import TransportAdapter, { directEmergency } from './TransportAdapter.js';

function isDelivered(result) {
  if (result?.status === 'rejected') return false;
  const value = result?.status === 'fulfilled' ? result.value : result;
  return value?.delivered === true || value?.emergencyFallback === true || value === undefined;
}

export class TransportDispatcher {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.serializer = options.serializer;
    this.levelRegistry = options.levelRegistry;
    this.diagnostics = options.diagnostics;
    this.metrics = options.metrics;
    this.adapterOptions = { ...(options.adapter || {}) };
    this.emergencyWriter = typeof options.emergencyWriter === 'function' ? options.emergencyWriter : null;
    this.adapters = [];
    this.closed = false;
    for (const entry of options.transports || []) {
      if (entry?.transport) this.add(entry.transport, entry.options || {});
      else this.add(entry);
    }
  }

  add(transport, options = {}) {
    if (this.closed) throw new Error('Cannot add a transport to a closed dispatcher.');
    if (!transport) throw new TypeError('TransportDispatcher.add requires a transport.');
    const existing = this.adapters.find((adapter) => adapter === transport || adapter.transport === transport);
    if (existing) return existing;
    if (!transport.serializer) {
      try { transport.serializer = this.serializer; } catch {}
    }
    const adapter = transport instanceof TransportAdapter ? transport : new TransportAdapter(transport, {
      serializer: this.serializer,
      levelRegistry: this.levelRegistry,
      diagnostics: this.diagnostics,
      metrics: this.metrics,
      emergencyWriter: this.emergencyWriter,
      ...this.adapterOptions,
      ...(options || {}),
    });
    this.adapters.push(adapter);
    return adapter;
  }

  remove(nameOrTransport, options = {}) {
    const index = this.adapters.findIndex((adapter) => (
      adapter === nameOrTransport
      || adapter.name === nameOrTransport
      || adapter.transport === nameOrTransport
    ));
    if (index === -1) return null;
    const [adapter] = this.adapters.splice(index, 1);
    if (options.close === true) {
      adapter.close(options).catch((error) => this.diagnostics?.report?.(
        'LOGGER_TRANSPORT_CLOSE_ERROR',
        error,
        { transport: adapter.name },
      ));
    }
    return adapter;
  }

  async dispatch(record, options = {}) {
    if (this.closed) throw new Error('Logger transport dispatcher is closed.');
    const definition = this.levelRegistry.get(record?.level);
    const highSeverity = definition?.droppable === false;

    if (!this.adapters.length) {
      this.metrics?.increment?.('records_without_transport');
      if (highSeverity) directEmergency(record, new Error('No logger transports are configured.'), this.emergencyWriter);
      return [];
    }

    const promises = this.adapters.map((adapter) => adapter.enqueue(record, options));
    if (options.awaitDelivery === true) {
      const results = await Promise.allSettled(promises);
      if (highSeverity && !results.some(isDelivered)) {
        directEmergency(record, new AggregateError(
          results.filter((result) => result.status === 'rejected').map((result) => result.reason),
          'All logger transports failed.',
        ), this.emergencyWriter);
      }
      if (options.throwOnError === true) {
        const failures = results.filter((result) => result.status === 'rejected');
        if (failures.length) throw new AggregateError(failures.map((result) => result.reason), 'One or more logger transports failed.');
      }
      return results;
    }

    for (const promise of promises) {
      promise.catch((error) => this.diagnostics?.report?.('LOGGER_TRANSPORT_ASYNC_ERROR', error, { recordId: record?.id }));
    }
    return promises;
  }

  async flush(options = {}) {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(this.adapters.map((adapter) => adapter.flush(options)));
    const failures = results.filter((result) => result.status === 'rejected');
    if (failures.length && options.throwOnError === true) {
      throw new AggregateError(failures.map((result) => result.reason), 'One or more logger transports failed to flush.');
    }
    return results;
  }

  async reopen(options = {}) {
    await this.flush(options);
    return await Promise.allSettled(this.adapters.map((adapter) => adapter.reopen(options)));
  }

  async close(options = {}) {
    if (this.closed) return;
    await this.flush(options);
    this.closed = true;
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(this.adapters.map((adapter) => adapter.close(options)));
    const failures = results.filter((result) => result.status === 'rejected');
    if (failures.length && options.throwOnError === true) {
      throw new AggregateError(failures.map((result) => result.reason), 'One or more logger transports failed to close.');
    }
    return results;
  }

  health() { return this.adapters.map((adapter) => adapter.health()); }

  stats() {
    return this.adapters.map((adapter) => ({
      name: adapter.name,
      ...adapter.stats,
      queueItems: adapter.queue.length,
      queueBytes: adapter.queueBytes,
      activeItems: adapter.activeItems,
      activeBytes: adapter.activeBytes,
    }));
  }

  snapshot() {
    return {
      closed: this.closed,
      transports: this.health(),
    };
  }
}

export default TransportDispatcher;

'use strict';

const TRANSITIONS = Object.freeze({
  created: new Set(['initializing', 'ready', 'closing', 'closed']),
  initializing: new Set(['ready', 'degraded', 'closing']),
  ready: new Set(['degraded', 'draining', 'closing']),
  degraded: new Set(['ready', 'draining', 'closing']),
  draining: new Set(['ready', 'degraded', 'closing']),
  closing: new Set(['closed']),
  closed: new Set(),
});

export class LoggerLifecycle {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.state = 'created';
    this.changedAt = Date.now();
    this.history = [{ state: this.state, at: this.changedAt }];
    this.onChange = typeof options.onChange === 'function' ? options.onChange : null;
  }

  transition(next, details = null) {
    if (next === this.state) return this.state;
    if (!TRANSITIONS[this.state]?.has(next)) throw new Error(`Invalid logger lifecycle transition: ${this.state} -> ${next}`);
    const previous = this.state;
    this.state = next;
    this.changedAt = Date.now();
    this.history.push({ state: next, previous, at: this.changedAt, details });
    this.onChange?.(next, previous, details);
    return next;
  }

  is(...states) {
    return states.includes(this.state);
  }

  snapshot() {
    return { state: this.state, changedAt: this.changedAt, history: this.history.map((entry) => ({ ...entry })) };
  }
}

export default LoggerLifecycle;

================================================

'use strict';

import { once } from 'node:events';
import { performance } from 'node:perf_hooks';

export const COMPATIBILITY_VIEW = Symbol.for('rawnode.logger.compatibilityView');
const EMERGENCY_FALLBACK_WRITTEN = Symbol.for('rawnode.logger.emergencyFallbackWritten');

function isPromise(value) {
  return value && typeof value.then === 'function';
}

function finiteInteger(value, fallback, minimum = 0) {
  const number = Number(value);
  return Number.isSafeInteger(number) && number >= minimum ? number : fallback;
}

function byteLength(value, serializer) {
  if (Buffer.isBuffer(value)) return value.length;
  if (typeof value === 'string') return Buffer.byteLength(value);
  try { return Buffer.byteLength(serializer.stringify(value)); }
  catch { return 512; }
}

function normalizeSet(value) {
  if (value == null || value === false) return null;
  const list = Array.isArray(value) ? value : String(value).split(',');
  const set = new Set(list.map((item) => String(item).trim()).filter(Boolean));
  return set.size ? set : null;
}

function createTransportError(code, message, details = null) {
  const error = new Error(message);
  error.code = code;
  if (details) error.details = details;
  return error;
}

function directEmergency(record, error, writer = null) {
  if (record && record[EMERGENCY_FALLBACK_WRITTEN]) return false;
  try {
    if (record && Object.isExtensible(record)) {
      Object.defineProperty(record, EMERGENCY_FALLBACK_WRITTEN, { value: true, enumerable: false });
    }
  } catch {}

  if (typeof writer === 'function') {
    try { writer(record, error); return true; } catch {}
  }

  const text = `[${record?.timestamp || new Date().toISOString()}] [${String(record?.level || 'error').toUpperCase()}] ${record?.message || 'Logger transport failure'}`;
  try {
    process.stderr.write(`${text}${error ? ` transport_error=${error.message || error}` : ''}\n`);
    return true;
  } catch { return false; }
}

function withTimeout(factory, timeoutMs, name, signal = null) {
  if (signal?.aborted) return Promise.reject(signal.reason || createTransportError('LOGGER_TRANSPORT_ABORTED', `Transport [${name}] write aborted.`));
  if (!timeoutMs || timeoutMs <= 0) return Promise.resolve().then(factory);
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    let settled = false;
    const cleanup = () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      signal?.removeEventListener?.('abort', onAbort);
    };
    const finish = (callback, value) => {
      if (settled) return;
      settled = true;
      cleanup();
      callback(value);
    };
    const onAbort = () => finish(reject, signal.reason || createTransportError('LOGGER_TRANSPORT_ABORTED', `Transport [${name}] write aborted.`));
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      finish(reject, createTransportError('LOGGER_TRANSPORT_TIMEOUT', `Transport [${name}] write timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms.`));
    }, timeoutMs);
    signal?.addEventListener?.('abort', onAbort, { once: true });
    Promise.resolve().then(factory).then(
      (value) => finish(resolve, value),
      (error) => finish(reject, error),
    );
  });
}

function delay(ms) {
  if (!ms || ms <= 0) return Promise.resolve();
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const timer = setTimeout(resolve, ms);
  });
}

export class TransportAdapter {
  constructor(transport, options = {}) {
    if (!transport) throw new TypeError('TransportAdapter requires a transport.');
    this.transport = transport;
    this.serializer = options.serializer;
    this.levelRegistry = options.levelRegistry;
    this.diagnostics = options.diagnostics;
    this.metrics = options.metrics;
    this.name = options.name || transport.name || transport.constructor?.name || 'transport';
    this.payloadMode = options.payloadMode || transport.payloadMode || (transport.rawnodeTransportMode === 'legacy' ? 'object' : 'auto');
    this.level = options.level || options.minLevel || transport.level || null;
    this.channels = normalizeSet(options.channels ?? transport.channels);
    this.excludeChannels = normalizeSet(options.excludeChannels ?? transport.excludeChannels);
    this.filter = typeof options.filter === 'function'
      ? options.filter
      : (typeof transport.recordFilter === 'function' ? transport.recordFilter.bind(transport) : null);
    this.owned = options.owned ?? transport.owned ?? true;

    this.maxItems = finiteInteger(options.maxItems, 10_000, 1);
    this.maxBytes = finiteInteger(options.maxBytes, 64 * 1024 * 1024, 1_024);
    this.maxOvercommitItems = finiteInteger(options.maxOvercommitItems, Math.max(8, Math.ceil(this.maxItems * 0.05)), 0);
    this.maxOvercommitBytes = finiteInteger(options.maxOvercommitBytes, Math.max(512 * 1024, Math.ceil(this.maxBytes * 0.10)), 0);
    this.overflow = options.overflow || 'severity-aware';
    this.budgetMs = Math.max(1, Number(options.budgetMs) || 6);
    this.maxPerSlice = finiteInteger(options.maxPerSlice, 1_000, 1);
    this.batchSize = finiteInteger(options.batchSize ?? transport.batchSize, 1, 1);
    this.batchIntervalMs = finiteInteger(
      options.batchIntervalMs ?? transport.batchIntervalMs,
      this.batchSize > 1 ? 10 : 0,
      0,
    );
    this.writeTimeoutMs = finiteInteger(options.writeTimeoutMs, 30_000, 0);
    this.flushTimeoutMs = finiteInteger(options.flushTimeoutMs, 30_000, 0);

    this.maxRetries = finiteInteger(options.maxRetries, 0, 0);
    this.retryDelayMs = finiteInteger(options.retryDelayMs, 50, 0);
    this.retryMaxDelayMs = finiteInteger(options.retryMaxDelayMs, 2_000, 0);
    this.retryJitter = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(options.retryJitter) || 0));
    this.retryOn = typeof options.retryOn === 'function' ? options.retryOn : null;

    this.failureThreshold = finiteInteger(options.failureThreshold, 5, 1);
    this.circuitOpenMs = finiteInteger(options.circuitOpenMs, 2_000, 1);
    this.emergencyWriter = typeof options.emergencyWriter === 'function' ? options.emergencyWriter : null;

    this.queue = [];
    this.queueBytes = 0;
    this.activeItems = 0;
    this.activeBytes = 0;
    this.overcommitItems = 0;
    this.overcommitBytes = 0;
    this.running = false;
    this._scheduled = false;
    this._batchTimer = null;
    this.closed = false;
    this.failures = 0;
    this.circuitOpenedAt = 0;
    this.flushWaiters = new Set();
    this.stats = {
      accepted: 0,
      delivered: 0,
      skipped: 0,
      dropped: 0,
      evicted: 0,
      overcommitted: 0,
      emergencyFallbacks: 0,
      batches: 0,
      retries: 0,
      errors: 0,
      timeouts: 0,
      circuitOpens: 0,
      fallbackWrites: 0,
      maxQueueItems: 0,
      maxQueueBytes: 0,
      maxQueueLatencyMs: 0,
      lastError: null,
      lastSuccessAt: null,
    };
  }

  accepts(record) {
    if (!record) return false;
    if (this.level && !this.levelRegistry.isEnabled(record.level, this.level)) return false;
    if (this.channels && !this.channels.has(String(record.channel))) return false;
    if (this.excludeChannels?.has(String(record.channel))) return false;
    if (this.filter) {
      try { return this.filter(record, this) !== false; }
      catch (error) {
        this.diagnostics?.report?.('LOGGER_TRANSPORT_FILTER_ERROR', error, { transport: this.name });
        return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  enqueue(record, options = {}) {
    if (this.closed) {
      return Promise.reject(createTransportError('LOGGER_TRANSPORT_CLOSED', `Transport [${this.name}] is closed.`));
    }
    if (!this.accepts(record)) {
      this.stats.skipped++;
      return Promise.resolve({ skipped: true, reason: 'transport-filter' });
    }

    const bytes = byteLength(record, this.serializer);
    const definition = this.levelRegistry.get(record.level);
    const highSeverity = definition?.droppable === false;

    if (this._isCircuitOpen()) {
      this.stats.dropped++;
      this.metrics?.increment?.('records_dropped');
      if (highSeverity && directEmergency(record, createTransportError('LOGGER_TRANSPORT_CIRCUIT_OPEN', `Transport circuit [${this.name}] is open.`), this.emergencyWriter)) {
        this.stats.fallbackWrites++;
        this.stats.emergencyFallbacks++;
      }
      return Promise.resolve({ dropped: true, circuitOpen: true, emergencyFallback: highSeverity });
    }

    if (!this._fits(bytes)) this._evictDroppable(bytes);
    let overcommitted = false;
    if (!this._fits(bytes)) {
      if (!highSeverity) {
        this.stats.dropped++;
        this.metrics?.increment?.('records_dropped');
        if (this.overflow === 'throw' || this.overflow === 'reject') {
          return Promise.reject(createTransportError('LOGGER_TRANSPORT_QUEUE_OVERFLOW', `Transport queue [${this.name}] is full.`, this.health()));
        }
        return Promise.resolve({ dropped: true, reason: 'queue-overflow' });
      }
      if (!this._canOvercommit(bytes)) {
        const error = createTransportError('LOGGER_TRANSPORT_OVERCOMMIT_EXHAUSTED', `Transport queue [${this.name}] exhausted its emergency overcommit budget.`);
        if (directEmergency(record, error, this.emergencyWriter)) {
          this.stats.fallbackWrites++;
          this.stats.emergencyFallbacks++;
        }
        return Promise.resolve({ delivered: true, emergencyFallback: true });
      }
      overcommitted = true;
      this.overcommitItems++;
      this.overcommitBytes += bytes;
      this.stats.overcommitted++;
    }

    this.stats.accepted++;
    this.metrics?.increment?.('transport_records_accepted');
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      this.queue.push({
        record,
        bytes,
        resolve,
        reject,
        signal: options.signal || null,
        overcommitted,
        enqueuedAt: performance.now(),
      });
      this.queueBytes += bytes;
      this.stats.maxQueueItems = Math.max(this.stats.maxQueueItems, this.queue.length + this.activeItems);
      this.stats.maxQueueBytes = Math.max(this.stats.maxQueueBytes, this.queueBytes + this.activeBytes);
      this._schedule();
    });
  }

  _fits(bytes) {
    return this.queue.length + this.activeItems < this.maxItems
      && this.queueBytes + this.activeBytes + bytes <= this.maxBytes;
  }

  _canOvercommit(bytes) {
    return this.overcommitItems < this.maxOvercommitItems
      && this.overcommitBytes + bytes <= this.maxOvercommitBytes;
  }

  _evictDroppable(bytes) {
    if (!['drop-oldest', 'drop-lowest', 'severity-aware'].includes(this.overflow)) return;
    while (!this._fits(bytes) && this.queue.length) {
      let index = -1;
      if (this.overflow === 'drop-oldest') {
        index = this.queue.findIndex((item) => this.levelRegistry.get(item.record.level)?.droppable !== false);
      } else {
        let lowestRank = -Infinity;
        let lowestSeverity = Infinity;
        for (let cursor = 0; cursor < this.queue.length; cursor++) {
          const definition = this.levelRegistry.get(this.queue[cursor].record.level);
          if (definition?.droppable === false) continue;
          const rank = definition?.rank ?? Infinity;
          const severity = definition?.severity ?? -Infinity;
          if (index < 0 || rank > lowestRank || (rank === lowestRank && severity < lowestSeverity)) {
            lowestRank = rank;
            lowestSeverity = severity;
            index = cursor;
          }
        }
      }
      if (index < 0) return;
      const [item] = this.queue.splice(index, 1);
      this.queueBytes -= item.bytes;
      if (item.overcommitted) {
        this.overcommitItems = Math.max(0, this.overcommitItems - 1);
        this.overcommitBytes = Math.max(0, this.overcommitBytes - item.bytes);
      }
      this.stats.dropped++;
      this.stats.evicted++;
      this.metrics?.increment?.('records_dropped');
      item.resolve({ dropped: true, reason: 'queue-overflow-evicted' });
    }
  }

  _schedule(force = false) {
    if (this.running || !this.queue.length) return;

    if (this._scheduled) {
      if (force || this.queue.length >= this.batchSize) {
        if (this._batchTimer) clearTimeout(this._batchTimer);
        this._batchTimer = null;
        this._scheduled = false;
        queueMicrotask(() => this._beginDrain());
      }
      return;
    }

    const shouldLinger = !force
      && this.batchSize > 1
      && this.batchIntervalMs > 0
      && this.queue.length < this.batchSize;
    this._scheduled = true;
    if (shouldLinger) {
      this._batchTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        this._batchTimer = null;
        this._scheduled = false;
        this._beginDrain();
      }, this.batchIntervalMs);
      this._batchTimer.unref?.();
      return;
    }
    queueMicrotask(() => {
      this._scheduled = false;
      this._beginDrain();
    });
  }

  _beginDrain() {
    if (this.running || this.closed || !this.queue.length) {
      this._resolveFlushWaiters();
      return;
    }
    this.running = true;
    this._drain().catch((error) => {
      this.diagnostics?.report?.('LOGGER_TRANSPORT_DRAIN_ERROR', error, { transport: this.name });
      this.running = false;
      if (this.queue.length) this._schedule();
      else this._resolveFlushWaiters();
    });
  }

  async _drain() {
    let sliceStart = performance.now();
    let processed = 0;
    try {
      while (this.queue.length) {
        const items = this._takeBatch();
        const live = items.filter((item) => !item.signal?.aborted);
        for (const item of items) {
          if (item.signal?.aborted) item.resolve({ dropped: true, reason: 'aborted' });
        }
        if (live.length) {
          try {
            await this._deliverWithRetry(live);
            this.failures = 0;
            this.stats.lastError = null;
            this.stats.lastSuccessAt = new Date().toISOString();
            this.stats.delivered += live.length;
            this.metrics?.increment?.('transport_records_delivered', live.length);
            for (const item of live) item.resolve({ delivered: true });
          } catch (error) {
            this._recordFailure(error, live);
          }
        }

        this._releaseActive(items);
        processed += items.length;
        if (processed >= this.maxPerSlice || performance.now() - sliceStart >= this.budgetMs) {
          processed = 0;
          sliceStart = performance.now();
          await new Promise((resolve) => setImmediate(resolve));
        }
      }
    } finally {
      this.running = false;
      this._resolveFlushWaiters();
      if (this.queue.length) this._schedule();
    }
  }

  _takeBatch() {
    const count = Math.min(this.batchSize, this.queue.length);
    const items = this.queue.splice(0, count);
    for (const item of items) {
      this.queueBytes -= item.bytes;
      this.activeItems++;
      this.activeBytes += item.bytes;
      this.stats.maxQueueLatencyMs = Math.max(this.stats.maxQueueLatencyMs, performance.now() - item.enqueuedAt);
    }
    return items;
  }

  _releaseActive(items) {
    for (const item of items) {
      this.activeItems = Math.max(0, this.activeItems - 1);
      this.activeBytes = Math.max(0, this.activeBytes - item.bytes);
      if (item.overcommitted) {
        this.overcommitItems = Math.max(0, this.overcommitItems - 1);
        this.overcommitBytes = Math.max(0, this.overcommitBytes - item.bytes);
      }
    }
  }

  async _deliverWithRetry(items) {
    let attempt = 0;
    while (true) {
      try {
        await withTimeout(() => this._deliver(items), this.writeTimeoutMs, this.name, items[0]?.signal);
        return;
      } catch (error) {
        const retryable = attempt < this.maxRetries && this._isRetryable(error, attempt, items);
        if (!retryable) throw error;
        attempt++;
        this.stats.retries += items.length;
        this.metrics?.increment?.('transport_retries', items.length);
        let wait = Math.min(this.retryMaxDelayMs, this.retryDelayMs * (2 ** (attempt - 1)));
        if (this.retryJitter > 0) wait *= 1 + ((Math.random() * 2 - 1) * this.retryJitter);
        await delay(Math.max(0, Math.round(wait)));
      }
    }
  }

  _isRetryable(error, attempt, items) {
    if (this.retryOn) {
      try { return this.retryOn(error, { attempt, records: items.map((item) => item.record), adapter: this }) !== false; }
      catch { return false; }
    }
    if (['LOGGER_TRANSPORT_CLOSED', 'ERR_INVALID_ARG_TYPE', 'ERR_INVALID_ARG_VALUE'].includes(error?.code)) return false;
    return true;
  }

  async _deliver(items) {
    if (items.length > 1 && this.transport.rawnodeEnterpriseTransport === true && typeof this.transport.writeBatch === 'function') {
      await this.transport.writeBatch(items.map((item) => item.record));
      this.stats.batches++;
      return;
    }
    for (const item of items) await this._write(item.record);
  }

  _recordFailure(error, items) {
    this.failures++;
    this.stats.errors += items.length;
    if (error?.code === 'LOGGER_TRANSPORT_TIMEOUT') this.stats.timeouts += items.length;
    this.stats.lastError = {
      message: error?.message || String(error),
      code: error?.code,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    this.metrics?.increment?.('transport_errors', items.length);
    this.diagnostics?.report?.('LOGGER_TRANSPORT_ERROR', error, { transport: this.name, records: items.length });
    if (this.failures >= this.failureThreshold && !this.circuitOpenedAt) {
      this.circuitOpenedAt = Date.now();
      this.stats.circuitOpens++;
    }
    for (const item of items) {
      const definition = this.levelRegistry.get(item.record.level);
      if (definition?.droppable === false && directEmergency(item.record, error, this.emergencyWriter)) {
        this.stats.fallbackWrites++;
        this.stats.emergencyFallbacks++;
      }
      item.reject(error);
    }
  }

  async _write(record) {
    const transport = this.transport;
    const compatibility = record[COMPATIBILITY_VIEW] || null;

    if (transport.rawnodeEnterpriseTransport === true && typeof transport.writeRecord === 'function') {
      return await transport.writeRecord(record);
    }

    if (transport.rawnodeCompatibilityTransport === true && typeof transport.writeRecord === 'function') {
      return await transport.writeRecord(this._compatibilityPayload(record, compatibility));
    }

    if (typeof transport.writeRecord === 'function') return await transport.writeRecord(record);
    if (typeof transport.write !== 'function') {
      throw new TypeError(`Transport [${this.name}] does not expose write() or writeRecord().`);
    }

    // Historic RawNode transports accept write(level, formatted).
    if (transport.writable !== true && !transport._writableState && transport.write.length >= 2) {
      const result = transport.write(record.level, compatibility?.formatted || record.formatted);
      return isPromise(result) ? await result : result;
    }

    const payload = this._compatibilityPayload(record, compatibility);
    if (transport.writable === true || transport._writableState) {
      return await this._writeNodeStream(transport, payload);
    }

    const result = transport.write(payload);
    return isPromise(result) ? await result : result;
  }

  async _writeNodeStream(stream, payload) {
    let accepted = true;
    await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      let callbackDone = false;
      let drainDone = true;
      let settled = false;
      const cleanup = () => stream.off?.('error', onError);
      const finish = (error) => {
        if (settled) return;
        if (error) {
          settled = true;
          cleanup();
          reject(error);
        } else if (callbackDone && drainDone) {
          settled = true;
          cleanup();
          resolve();
        }
      };
      const onError = (error) => finish(error);
      stream.once?.('error', onError);
      try {
        accepted = stream.write(payload, (error) => {
          callbackDone = true;
          finish(error);
        });
        if (accepted === false) {
          drainDone = false;
          stream.once('drain', () => {
            drainDone = true;
            finish();
          });
        }
      } catch (error) {
        finish(error);
      }
    });
    return accepted;
  }

  _compatibilityPayload(record, compatibility) {
    const configured = this.payloadMode === 'auto'
      ? (this.transport.rawnodeTransportMode === 'legacy' ? 'object' : 'object')
      : this.payloadMode;
    const source = compatibility || record;
    if (configured === 'json') return this.serializer.stringify(source);
    if (configured === 'text' || configured === 'formatted') return source.formatted || record.formatted;
    return source;
  }

  _isCircuitOpen() {
    if (!this.circuitOpenedAt) return false;
    if (Date.now() - this.circuitOpenedAt >= this.circuitOpenMs) {
      this.circuitOpenedAt = 0;
      this.failures = 0;
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  async flush(options = {}) {
    if (!this.running && this.activeItems === 0 && this.queue.length === 0) {
      if (typeof this.transport.flush === 'function') await this.transport.flush(options);
      return;
    }
    const timeoutMs = options.timeoutMs == null ? this.flushTimeoutMs : Math.max(0, Number(options.timeoutMs) || 0);
    let timer = null;
    await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const waiter = {
        resolve: () => {
          if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
          this.flushWaiters.delete(waiter);
          resolve();
        },
      };
      this.flushWaiters.add(waiter);
      if (timeoutMs > 0) {
        timer = setTimeout(() => {
          this.flushWaiters.delete(waiter);
          reject(createTransportError('LOGGER_TRANSPORT_FLUSH_TIMEOUT', `Transport [${this.name}] flush timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms.`, this.health()));
        }, timeoutMs);
      }
      this._schedule(true);
    });
    if (typeof this.transport.flush === 'function') await this.transport.flush(options);
  }

  _resolveFlushWaiters() {
    if (this.running || this.activeItems || this.queue.length) return;
    for (const waiter of this.flushWaiters) waiter.resolve();
    this.flushWaiters.clear();
  }

  async reopen(options = {}) {
    await this.flush(options);
    if (typeof this.transport.reopen === 'function') return await this.transport.reopen(options);
    return false;
  }

  async close(options = {}) {
    if (this.closed) return;
    await this.flush(options);
    if (this._batchTimer) clearTimeout(this._batchTimer);
    this._batchTimer = null;
    this._scheduled = false;
    this.closed = true;
    if (this.owned === false) return;
    const transport = this.transport;
    if (typeof transport.close === 'function') await transport.close(options);
    else if (typeof transport.end === 'function') {
      await new Promise((resolve, reject) => transport.end((error) => error ? reject(error) : resolve()));
    } else if (typeof transport.destroy === 'function') transport.destroy();
  }

  health() {
    const nested = typeof this.transport.health === 'function' ? this.transport.health() : null;
    const circuitOpen = this._isCircuitOpen();
    return {
      name: this.name,
      status: this.closed ? 'closed' : (circuitOpen || this.stats.lastError ? 'degraded' : 'healthy'),
      closed: this.closed,
      owned: this.owned,
      level: this.level,
      channels: this.channels ? [...this.channels] : null,
      circuitOpen,
      queueItems: this.queue.length,
      queueBytes: this.queueBytes,
      batchSize: this.batchSize,
      batchIntervalMs: this.batchIntervalMs,
      scheduled: this._scheduled,
      activeItems: this.activeItems,
      activeBytes: this.activeBytes,
      overcommitItems: this.overcommitItems,
      overcommitBytes: this.overcommitBytes,
      stats: { ...this.stats },
      transport: nested,
    };
  }
}

export { EMERGENCY_FALLBACK_WRITTEN, directEmergency };
export default TransportAdapter;

'use strict';

export { default as LoggerRuntime, LoggerRuntime as Runtime, createLogger as createRuntimeLogger } from './LoggerRuntime.js';

'use strict';

import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';

function safeText(error) {
  if (error instanceof Error) return error.stack || `${error.name}: ${error.message}`;
  try { return typeof error === 'string' ? error : JSON.stringify(error); }
  catch { return String(error); }
}

export class InternalDiagnostics extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super();
    this.limit = Math.max(1, Number(options.limit) || 256);
    this.stderr = options.stderr !== false;
    this.prefix = options.prefix || '[rawnode-logger]';
    this.history = [];
    this._reporting = false;
  }

  report(code, error, metadata = {}) {
    const entry = Object.freeze({
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      code: String(code || 'LOGGER_INTERNAL_ERROR'),
      message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : safeText(error),
      stack: error instanceof Error ? error.stack : undefined,
      metadata: { ...(metadata || {}) },
    });
    if (this.history.length >= this.limit) this.history.shift();
    this.history.push(entry);
    try { super.emit('diagnostic', entry); } catch {}

    if (this.stderr && !this._reporting) {
      this._reporting = true;
      try {
        process.stderr.write(`${this.prefix} ${entry.code}: ${entry.message}\n`);
      } catch {}
      this._reporting = false;
    }
    return entry;
  }

  snapshot() {
    return this.history.map((entry) => ({ ...entry, metadata: { ...entry.metadata } }));
  }

  clear() {
    this.history.length = 0;
  }
}

export default InternalDiagnostics;

'use strict';

const DEFAULT_LEVELS = Object.freeze([
  { name: 'emergency', rank: 0, severity: 80, syslog: 0, consoleMethod: 'error', category: 'operational', droppable: false },
  { name: 'alert', rank: 1, severity: 75, syslog: 1, consoleMethod: 'error', category: 'operational', droppable: false },
  { name: 'critical', rank: 2, severity: 70, syslog: 2, consoleMethod: 'error', category: 'operational', droppable: false },
  { name: 'error', rank: 3, severity: 60, syslog: 3, consoleMethod: 'error', category: 'operational', droppable: false },
  { name: 'urgent', rank: 4, severity: 55, syslog: null, consoleMethod: 'error', category: 'operational', droppable: false },
  { name: 'priority', rank: 5, severity: 50, syslog: null, consoleMethod: 'warn', category: 'operational', droppable: false },
  { name: 'warn', rank: 6, severity: 40, syslog: 4, consoleMethod: 'warn', category: 'operational', droppable: true },
  { name: 'notice', rank: 7, severity: 35, syslog: 5, consoleMethod: 'log', category: 'operational', droppable: true },
  { name: 'success', rank: 8, severity: 32, syslog: null, consoleMethod: 'log', category: 'success', droppable: true },
  { name: 'info', rank: 9, severity: 30, syslog: 6, consoleMethod: 'log', category: 'operational', droppable: true },
  { name: 'debug', rank: 10, severity: 20, syslog: 7, consoleMethod: 'debug', category: 'diagnostic', droppable: true },
  { name: 'metric', rank: 11, severity: 15, syslog: null, consoleMethod: 'log', category: 'metric', droppable: true },
  { name: 'metrics', rank: 12, severity: 15, syslog: null, consoleMethod: 'log', category: 'metric', droppable: true },
  { name: 'important', rank: 13, severity: 45, syslog: null, consoleMethod: 'warn', category: 'important', droppable: false },
]);

const DEFAULT_ALIASES = Object.freeze({
  warning: 'warn',
  fatal: 'critical',
  trace: 'debug',
});

function normalizeName(value) {
  return String(value ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
}

function normalizeDefinition(input) {
  if (!input || typeof input !== 'object') throw new TypeError('Level definition must be an object.');
  const name = normalizeName(input.name);
  if (!name) throw new TypeError('Level definition requires a name.');
  const rank = Number(input.rank);
  if (!Number.isFinite(rank)) throw new TypeError(`Level [${name}] requires a finite rank.`);
  return Object.freeze({
    name,
    rank,
    severity: Number.isFinite(Number(input.severity)) ? Number(input.severity) : Math.max(0, 100 - rank),
    syslog: input.syslog == null ? null : Number(input.syslog),
    consoleMethod: input.consoleMethod || 'log',
    category: input.category || 'custom',
    droppable: input.droppable !== false,
    aliases: Object.freeze(Array.isArray(input.aliases) ? input.aliases.map(normalizeName).filter(Boolean) : []),
    metadata: Object.freeze({ ...(input.metadata || {}) }),
  });
}

export class LevelRegistry {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this._levels = new Map();
    this._aliases = new Map();
    const definitions = options.levels || DEFAULT_LEVELS;
    for (const definition of definitions) this.register(definition, { replace: true });
    const aliases = { ...DEFAULT_ALIASES, ...(options.aliases || {}) };
    for (const [alias, target] of Object.entries(aliases)) this.alias(alias, target, { replace: true });
  }

  register(definition, options = {}) {
    const normalized = normalizeDefinition(definition);
    if (this._levels.has(normalized.name) && options.replace !== true) {
      throw new Error(`Log level [${normalized.name}] is already registered.`);
    }
    this._levels.set(normalized.name, normalized);
    for (const alias of normalized.aliases) this.alias(alias, normalized.name, { replace: options.replace === true });
    return this;
  }

  alias(aliasName, targetName, options = {}) {
    const alias = normalizeName(aliasName);
    const target = normalizeName(targetName);
    if (!alias || !target) throw new TypeError('Level alias and target are required.');
    if (!this._levels.has(target)) throw new Error(`Cannot alias unknown log level [${target}].`);
    if (this._aliases.has(alias) && options.replace !== true) throw new Error(`Log level alias [${alias}] already exists.`);
    this._aliases.set(alias, target);
    return this;
  }

  resolveName(name) {
    const normalized = normalizeName(name);
    return this._aliases.get(normalized) || normalized;
  }

  get(name) {
    return this._levels.get(this.resolveName(name)) || null;
  }

  has(name) {
    return this.get(name) !== null;
  }

  shouldLog(level, threshold) {
    return this.isEnabled(level, threshold);
  }

  isEnabled(level, threshold) {
    const candidate = this.get(level);
    const minimum = this.get(threshold);
    if (!candidate || !minimum) return false;
    return candidate.rank <= minimum.rank;
  }

  isHighSeverity(level) {
    return this.get(level)?.droppable === false;
  }

  isDroppable(level) {
    const definition = this.get(level);
    return definition ? definition.droppable !== false : true;
  }

  unregister(name) {
    const normalized = this.resolveName(name);
    const deleted = this._levels.delete(normalized);
    if (deleted) {
      for (const [alias, target] of [...this._aliases]) {
        if (target === normalized || alias === normalized) this._aliases.delete(alias);
      }
    }
    return deleted;
  }

  compare(left, right) {
    const a = this.get(left);
    const b = this.get(right);
    if (!a || !b) return Number.NaN;
    return a.rank - b.rank;
  }

  names() {
    return Array.from(this._levels.values()).sort((a, b) => a.rank - b.rank).map((item) => item.name);
  }

  definitions() {
    return Array.from(this._levels.values()).sort((a, b) => a.rank - b.rank);
  }

  snapshot() {
    return {
      levels: this.definitions().map((item) => ({ ...item, aliases: [...item.aliases], metadata: { ...item.metadata } })),
      aliases: Object.fromEntries(this._aliases),
    };
  }

  clone() {
    return new LevelRegistry({ levels: this.definitions(), aliases: Object.fromEntries(this._aliases) });
  }
}

export { DEFAULT_LEVELS };
export default LevelRegistry;
