'use strict';

import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';
import LevelRegistry from './LevelRegistry.js';
import InternalDiagnostics from './InternalDiagnostics.js';
import DeliveryQueue from './DeliveryQueue.js';
import LoggerLifecycle from './LoggerLifecycle.js';
import TransportDispatcher from './TransportDispatcher.js';
import { COMPATIBILITY_VIEW } from './TransportAdapter.js';
import { resolveCompatibilityProfile } from '../compatibility/CompatibilityProfile.js';
import ContextManager, { globalContextManager } from '../context/ContextManager.js';
import SafeSerializer from '../serialization/SafeSerializer.js';
import TextFormatter from '../serialization/TextFormatter.js';
import ErrorNormalizer from '../records/ErrorNormalizer.js';
import RecordFactory from '../records/RecordFactory.js';
import Redactor from '../security/Redactor.js';
import ProcessorRegistry from '../processing/ProcessorRegistry.js';
import PluginManager from '../plugins/PluginManager.js';
import LoggerMetrics from '../observability/LoggerMetrics.js';

const EMPTY_CONTEXT = Object.freeze(Object.create(null));

function freezeRecord(record) {
  if (!record || typeof record !== 'object') return record;
  for (const key of ['data', 'context', 'error', 'tags']) {
    const value = record[key];
    if (value && typeof value === 'object' && !Object.isFrozen(value)) Object.freeze(value);
  }
  return Object.freeze(record);
}

function attachLevelDefinition(record, definition) {
  if (!record || typeof record !== 'object') return record;
  try {
    Object.defineProperty(record, 'levelDefinition', {
      value: definition,
      enumerable: false,
      configurable: false,
    });
  } catch {}
  return record;
}

function requestContext(req = {}) {
  const headers = req.headers || EMPTY_CONTEXT;
  const user = req.user || req.auth || null;
  return {
    requestId: req.id || req.requestId || headers['x-request-id'] || headers['x-correlation-id'] || null,
    correlationId: req.correlationId || headers['x-correlation-id'] || null,
    traceId: req.traceId || headers['x-trace-id'] || null,
    spanId: req.spanId || headers['x-span-id'] || null,
    tenantId: req.tenantId || req.tenant?.id || headers['x-tenant-id'] || null,
    userId: user?.id || user?._id || user?.sub || null,
    method: req.method || null,
    path: (typeof req.path === 'function' ? req.path() : req.path) || req.url || null,
    route: req.route?.name || req.route?.path || req.routeName || null,
    protocol: req.protocol || (req.socket?.encrypted ? 'https' : 'http'),
    remoteAddress: req.ip || req.socket?.remoteAddress || null,
  };
}

function jobContext(job = {}) {
  return {
    jobId: job.id || job.jobId || null,
    queue: job.queue || job.queueName || null,
    attempt: job.attempt || job.attempts || null,
    maxAttempts: job.maxAttempts || job.tries || null,
    tenantId: job.tenantId || job.tenant_id || null,
    traceId: job.traceId || job.trace_id || null,
    correlationId: job.correlationId || job.correlation_id || null,
  };
}

function roughBytes(value) {
  if (value == null) return 0;
  if (Buffer.isBuffer(value)) return value.length;
  if (ArrayBuffer.isView(value)) return value.byteLength;
  if (value instanceof ArrayBuffer) return value.byteLength;
  if (typeof value === 'string') return Buffer.byteLength(value);
  if (typeof value === 'number' || typeof value === 'boolean' || typeof value === 'bigint') return 16;
  if (typeof value === 'function') return 64;
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return Math.min(64 * 1024, 128 + value.length * 32);
  if (typeof value === 'object') {
    try { return Math.min(64 * 1024, 256 + Object.keys(value).length * 64); }
    catch { return 512; }
  }
  return 64;
}

function emergencyFallback(level, message, error) {
  try {
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
    const text = typeof message === 'function' ? '[lazy message]' : String(message ?? '');
    process.stderr.write(`[${timestamp}] [${String(level || 'error').toUpperCase()}] ${text}${error ? ` logger_error=${error.message || error}` : ''}\n`);
  } catch {}
}

function cloneProcessors(registry) {
  return typeof registry?.clone === 'function' ? registry.clone() : registry;
}

function clonePlugins(manager) {
  return typeof manager?.clone === 'function' ? manager.clone() : manager;
}

/**
 * Canonical RawNode logging runtime.
 *
 * Guarantees:
 * - disabled levels take the fast path before lazy values are evaluated;
 * - accepted records are processed in deterministic order through a bounded queue;
 * - critical records are never silently dropped under queue pressure;
 * - transports are isolated behind bounded adapters and explicit lifecycle APIs;
 * - structured records remain uncolored; presentation is transport-specific.
 */
export class LoggerRuntime extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super();
    const shared = options._shared || null;

    this.profile = resolveCompatibilityProfile(options.profile || shared?.profile || 'v1', options.profileOverrides);
    this.levelRegistry = shared?.levelRegistry || options.levelRegistry || new LevelRegistry(options.levels || {});
    this.level = this.levelRegistry.resolveName(options.level || shared?.level || 'info');
    if (!this.levelRegistry.has(this.level)) {
      if (this.profile.strictLevels) throw new Error(`Unknown log level [${this.level}].`);
      this.level = 'info';
    }

    this.serializer = shared?.serializer || options.serializer || new SafeSerializer(options.serialization);
    this.diagnostics = shared?.diagnostics || options.diagnostics || new InternalDiagnostics(options.diagnosticsOptions);
    const metricsOptions = options.observability && typeof options.observability === 'object' ? options.observability : {};
    this.metricsRegistry = shared?.metricsRegistry || shared?.metrics || options.metricsRegistry || options.metrics || new LoggerMetrics({
      enabled: metricsOptions.enabled ?? this.profile.metrics,
      ...metricsOptions,
    });
    this.contextManager = shared?.contextManager || options.contextManager || (this.profile.context
      ? globalContextManager
      : new ContextManager({ enabled: false }));
    this.errorNormalizer = shared?.errorNormalizer || options.errorNormalizer || new ErrorNormalizer(this.serializer, options.errors);
    this.recordFactory = shared?.recordFactory || options.recordFactory || new RecordFactory({
      serializer: this.serializer,
      errorNormalizer: this.errorNormalizer,
      service: options.service,
      environment: options.environment,
      clock: options.clock,
      hrtime: options.hrtime,
      idFactory: options.idFactory,
      schemaVersion: options.schemaVersion,
    });
    this.redactor = shared?.redactor || options.redactor || new Redactor({
      enabled: options.security?.enabled ?? options.security?.redact ?? this.profile.redact,
      sanitizeControls: options.security?.sanitizeControls ?? this.profile.sanitizeControls,
      stripAnsi: options.security?.stripAnsi ?? this.profile.stripAnsi,
      ...(options.security || {}),
    });

    this.processors = options.processors instanceof ProcessorRegistry
      ? options.processors
      : new ProcessorRegistry({
          errorPolicy: this.profile.isolateProcessorErrors ? 'isolate' : 'propagate',
          diagnostics: this.diagnostics,
        });
    this.plugins = options.plugins instanceof PluginManager
      ? options.plugins
      : new PluginManager({
          duplicate: options.pluginOptions?.duplicate || 'reject',
          errorPolicy: this.profile.isolatePluginErrors ? 'isolate' : 'propagate',
          timeoutMs: options.pluginOptions?.timeoutMs,
          diagnostics: this.diagnostics,
        });

    this.formatter = options.formatter || shared?.formatter || new TextFormatter(options.textFormatter);
    this.compatibilityViewFactory = options.compatibilityViewFactory || shared?.compatibilityViewFactory || null;
    this.awaitTransportDelivery = options.awaitTransportDelivery ?? this.profile.awaitTransportDelivery;
    this.channelName = options.channel || 'app';
    this.service = options.service || shared?.service || this.recordFactory.service;
    this.environment = options.environment || shared?.environment || this.recordFactory.environment;
    this.staticContext = Object.freeze({ ...(shared?.staticContext || {}), ...(options.context || {}) });
    this.tags = Object.freeze([...(shared?.tags || []), ...(options.tags || [])]);
    this.boundError = options.boundError || null;
    const requestedSampleRate = options.sampleRate == null ? 1 : Number(options.sampleRate);
    this.sampleRate = Number.isFinite(requestedSampleRate) ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, requestedSampleRate)) : 1;
    this.onceKey = options.onceKey || null;
    this.ratePolicy = options.ratePolicy || null;
    this.disabled = options.disabled === true;
    this.defaultLevel = options.defaultLevel || null;

    const stateLimits = options.stateLimits || options.limits?.state || {};
    this.sharedState = shared?.sharedState || {
      onceKeys: new Set(),
      rateBuckets: new Map(),
      random: options.random || Math.random,
      maxOnceKeys: Math.max(1, Number(stateLimits.maxOnceKeys) || 10_000),
      maxRateBuckets: Math.max(1, Number(stateLimits.maxRateBuckets) || 10_000),
    };

    const transportQueue = options.transportQueue || options.queue?.transport || options.queue || {};
    if (shared?.dispatcher) {
      this.dispatcher = shared.dispatcher;
      this.ownsDispatcher = false;
    } else {
      this.dispatcher = options.dispatcher || new TransportDispatcher({
        serializer: this.serializer,
        levelRegistry: this.levelRegistry,
        diagnostics: this.diagnostics,
        metrics: this.metricsRegistry,
        emergencyWriter: options.emergencyWriter,
        transports: options.transports || [],
        adapter: {
          payloadMode: options.transportPayload || this.profile.legacyTransportPayload,
          maxItems: transportQueue.maxItems,
          maxBytes: transportQueue.maxBytes,
          maxOvercommitItems: transportQueue.maxOvercommitItems,
          maxOvercommitBytes: transportQueue.maxOvercommitBytes,
          overflow: transportQueue.overflow || this.profile.queueOverflow,
          budgetMs: transportQueue.budgetMs,
          maxPerSlice: transportQueue.maxPerSlice,
          flushTimeoutMs: transportQueue.flushTimeoutMs,
          writeTimeoutMs: transportQueue.writeTimeoutMs,
          batchSize: transportQueue.batchSize,
          failureThreshold: options.circuitBreaker?.failureThreshold,
          circuitOpenMs: options.circuitBreaker?.openMs,
          maxRetries: transportQueue.maxRetries,
          retryDelayMs: transportQueue.retryDelayMs,
          retryMaxDelayMs: transportQueue.retryMaxDelayMs,
          retryJitter: transportQueue.retryJitter,
        },
      });
      this.ownsDispatcher = true;
    }

    const pipelineOptions = options.pipelineQueue || options.queue?.pipeline || {};
    if (shared?.pipelineQueue) {
      this.pipelineQueue = shared.pipelineQueue;
      this.ownsPipelineQueue = false;
    } else {
      this.pipelineQueue = new DeliveryQueue({
        name: options.name ? `${options.name}:pipeline` : 'logger-pipeline',
        maxItems: pipelineOptions.maxItems ?? options.maxPendingItems ?? 10_000,
        maxBytes: pipelineOptions.maxBytes ?? options.maxPendingBytes ?? 64 * 1024 * 1024,
        maxOvercommitItems: pipelineOptions.maxOvercommitItems,
        maxOvercommitBytes: pipelineOptions.maxOvercommitBytes,
        overflow: pipelineOptions.overflow || this.profile.queueOverflow,
        budgetMs: pipelineOptions.budgetMs,
        maxPerSlice: pipelineOptions.maxPerSlice,
        flushTimeoutMs: pipelineOptions.flushTimeoutMs,
        onDrop: (result, metadata) => {
          this.metricsRegistry.increment('records_dropped');
          try { this.emit('drop', { ...result, metadata }); } catch {}
        },
        onError: (error, metadata) => this.diagnostics.report('LOGGER_PIPELINE_ERROR', error, metadata),
        onEmergency: (metadata) => {
          this.metricsRegistry.increment('records_emergency_fallback');
          return emergencyFallback(metadata?.level, metadata?.message, metadata?.error);
        },
      });
      this.ownsPipelineQueue = true;
    }

    for (const processor of options.processorList || []) {
      if (typeof processor === 'function') this.use(processor);
      else if (processor?.processor) this.use(processor.processor, processor.options || processor);
    }
    for (const plugin of options.pluginList || []) this.plugin(plugin);

    this.lifecycle = new LoggerLifecycle({
      onChange: (state, previous, details) => {
        try { this.emit('state', { state, previous, details }); } catch {}
      },
    });
    this.lifecycle.transition('ready');
    this.state = 'ready';
    this.createdAt = Date.now();
    this._pending = new Set();
    this._closed = false;
    this._parent = options._parent || null;
  }

  isLevelEnabled(level) {
    if (this.disabled || this._closed || !this.pipelineQueue.accepting) return false;
    return this.levelRegistry.isEnabled(level, this.level);
  }

  setLevel(level) {
    const normalized = this.levelRegistry.resolveName(level);
    if (!this.levelRegistry.has(normalized)) {
      if (this.profile.strictLevels) throw new Error(`Unknown log level [${level}].`);
      return this;
    }
    this.level = normalized;
    return this;
  }

  registerLevel(definition, options = {}) {
    this.levelRegistry.register(definition, options);
    return this;
  }

  levels() { return this.levelRegistry.definitions(); }

  log(level, message, data = null, options = {}) {
    const submitted = this.submit(level, message, data, options);
    if (!submitted.accepted) return;
    submitted.promise.catch((error) => {
      this.diagnostics.report('LOGGER_PIPELINE_ERROR', error, { level: submitted.level });
      const definition = this.levelRegistry.get(submitted.level);
      if (definition?.droppable === false) emergencyFallback(submitted.level, message, error);
    });
  }

  async logAsync(level, message, data = null, options = {}) {
    const submitted = this.submit(level, message, data, {
      ...options,
      awaitDelivery: options.awaitDelivery !== false,
      throwOnError: options.throwOnError !== false,
    });
    if (!submitted.accepted) return null;
    return await submitted.promise;
  }

  submit(level, message, data = null, options = {}) {
    const normalized = this.levelRegistry.resolveName(level || this.defaultLevel || 'info');
    if (!this._accept(normalized, message, options)) {
      return { accepted: false, level: normalized, promise: Promise.resolve(null) };
    }

    const definition = this.levelRegistry.get(normalized);
    const acceptedOptions = {
      ...options,
      _acceptedTimeUnixMs: Number(this.recordFactory.clock()),
      _acceptedHrtimeNs: this.recordFactory.hrtime(),
    };
    const bytes = 512 + roughBytes(message) + roughBytes(data) + roughBytes(options.context);
    const queued = this.pipelineQueue.enqueue(
      () => this._process(normalized, message, data, acceptedOptions, acceptedOptions.awaitDelivery === true),
      {
        bytes,
        signal: options.signal,
        meta: {
          level: normalized,
          severity: definition?.severity ?? null,
          rank: definition?.rank ?? null,
          droppable: definition?.droppable !== false,
          message,
          channel: options.channel || this.channelName,
        },
        emergency: () => {
          this.metricsRegistry.increment('records_emergency_fallback');
          return emergencyFallback(normalized, message, new Error('Logger pipeline emergency overcommit budget exhausted.'));
        },
      },
    );

    if (!queued.accepted) this.metricsRegistry.increment('records_rejected');
    else this.metricsRegistry.increment('records_enqueued');
    this._pending.add(queued.promise);
    queued.promise.finally(() => this._pending.delete(queued.promise)).catch(() => {});
    return { accepted: queued.accepted, level: normalized, promise: queued.promise, overcommitted: queued.overcommitted === true };
  }

  _accept(level, message, options = {}) {
    if (!this.levelRegistry.has(level)) {
      if (this.profile.strictLevels) throw new Error(`Unknown log level [${level}].`);
      return false;
    }
    if (!this.isLevelEnabled(level)) return false;

    const definition = this.levelRegistry.get(level);
    if (this.sampleRate < 1 && definition?.droppable !== false && this.sharedState.random() >= this.sampleRate) {
      this.metricsRegistry.increment('records_sampled_out');
      return false;
    }
    if (this.onceKey) {
      const key = String(this.onceKey);
      if (this.sharedState.onceKeys.has(key)) return false;
      while (this.sharedState.onceKeys.size >= this.sharedState.maxOnceKeys) {
        const oldest = this.sharedState.onceKeys.values().next().value;
        if (oldest === undefined) break;
        this.sharedState.onceKeys.delete(oldest);
      }
      this.sharedState.onceKeys.add(key);
    }
    if (this.ratePolicy) {
      const now = Date.now();
      const bucketKey = `${this.ratePolicy.key}:${level}`;
      const bucket = this.sharedState.rateBuckets.get(bucketKey) || { startedAt: now, count: 0 };
      if (now - bucket.startedAt >= this.ratePolicy.intervalMs) {
        bucket.startedAt = now;
        bucket.count = 0;
      }
      if (bucket.count >= this.ratePolicy.limit && definition?.droppable !== false) {
        this.metricsRegistry.increment('records_rate_limited');
        this.sharedState.rateBuckets.set(bucketKey, bucket);
        return false;
      }
      bucket.count++;
      if (!this.sharedState.rateBuckets.has(bucketKey)
        && this.sharedState.rateBuckets.size >= this.sharedState.maxRateBuckets) {
        for (const [candidateKey, candidate] of this.sharedState.rateBuckets) {
          if (now - candidate.startedAt >= this.ratePolicy.intervalMs) this.sharedState.rateBuckets.delete(candidateKey);
        }
        while (this.sharedState.rateBuckets.size >= this.sharedState.maxRateBuckets) {
          const oldest = this.sharedState.rateBuckets.keys().next().value;
          if (oldest === undefined) break;
          this.sharedState.rateBuckets.delete(oldest);
        }
      }
      this.sharedState.rateBuckets.set(bucketKey, bucket);
    }
    if (options.signal?.aborted) return false;
    return true;
  }

  async _process(level, message, data, options, awaitDelivery) {
    if (options.signal?.aborted) return null;
    const started = process.hrtime.bigint();
    const definition = this.levelRegistry.get(level);
    const context = {
      ...this.contextManager.get(),
      ...this.staticContext,
      ...(options.context || {}),
    };

    let record = this.recordFactory.create({
      level,
      levelDefinition: definition,
      message,
      data,
      error: options.error || this.boundError,
      context,
      channel: options.channel || this.channelName,
      service: options.service || this.service,
      environment: options.environment || this.environment,
      tags: [...this.tags, ...(options.tags || [])],
      requestId: options.requestId,
      correlationId: options.correlationId,
      traceId: options.traceId,
      spanId: options.spanId,
      tenantId: options.tenantId,
      userId: options.userId,
      timeUnixMs: options._acceptedTimeUnixMs,
      hrtimeNs: options._acceptedHrtimeNs,
    });
    attachLevelDefinition(record, definition);

    const hookContext = { logger: this, signal: options.signal, levelDefinition: definition };
    const trustedFilter = { trustedOnly: this.redactor.enabled === true };
    const untrustedFilter = { untrustedOnly: this.redactor.enabled === true };

    if (this.plugins.hasHook('beforeBuild', trustedFilter)) {
      record = await this.plugins.runHook('beforeBuild', record, { ...hookContext, ...trustedFilter });
      if (record == null) return this._processorDrop();
      attachLevelDefinition(record, definition);
    }
    if (this.processors.hasPhase('before-redaction')) {
      record = await this.processors.runPhase('before-redaction', record, hookContext);
      if (record == null) return this._processorDrop();
      attachLevelDefinition(record, definition);
    }
    if (this.plugins.hasHook('afterBuild', trustedFilter)) {
      record = await this.plugins.runHook('afterBuild', record, { ...hookContext, ...trustedFilter });
      if (record == null) return this._processorDrop();
      attachLevelDefinition(record, definition);
    }

    if (this.redactor.enabled) this.redactor.redact(record, { inPlace: true });

    let postRedactionEnriched = false;
    if (this.processors.hasPhase('after-redaction')) {
      record = await this.processors.runPhase('after-redaction', record, hookContext);
      if (record == null) return this._processorDrop();
      attachLevelDefinition(record, definition);
      postRedactionEnriched = true;
    }
    if (this.plugins.hasHook('afterRedaction', untrustedFilter)) {
      record = await this.plugins.runHook('afterRedaction', record, { ...hookContext, ...untrustedFilter });
      if (record == null) return this._processorDrop();
      attachLevelDefinition(record, definition);
      postRedactionEnriched = true;
    }
    if (postRedactionEnriched && this.redactor.enabled) this.redactor.redact(record, { inPlace: true });

    try {
      const formatted = typeof this.formatter === 'function'
        ? await this.formatter(record, this.serializer)
        : await this.formatter.format(record, this.serializer);
      record.formatted = String(formatted ?? '');
    } catch (error) {
      this.diagnostics.report('LOGGER_FORMATTER_ERROR', error, { level, formatter: this.formatter?.constructor?.name });
      record.formatted = `[${record.timestamp}] [${String(level).toUpperCase()}] ${record.message}`;
    }

    const shouldBuildCompatibility = this.compatibilityViewFactory
      && (typeof this.compatibilityViewFactory.shouldBuild !== 'function'
        || this.compatibilityViewFactory.shouldBuild(record, this) !== false);
    if (shouldBuildCompatibility) {
      try {
        const view = await this.compatibilityViewFactory(record, this);
        const safeView = this.redactor.enabled && view !== undefined ? this.redactor.redact(view) : view;
        if (safeView !== undefined) Object.defineProperty(record, COMPATIBILITY_VIEW, { value: safeView, enumerable: false, configurable: false });
      } catch (error) {
        this.diagnostics.report('LOGGER_COMPATIBILITY_FORMAT_ERROR', error, { level });
      }
    }

    let beforeWriteEnriched = false;
    if (this.processors.hasPhase('before-write')) {
      record = await this.processors.runPhase('before-write', record, hookContext);
      if (record == null) return this._processorDrop();
      attachLevelDefinition(record, definition);
      beforeWriteEnriched = true;
    }
    if (this.plugins.hasHook('beforeWrite')) {
      record = await this.plugins.runHook('beforeWrite', record, hookContext);
      if (record == null) return this._processorDrop();
      attachLevelDefinition(record, definition);
      beforeWriteEnriched = true;
    }
    if (beforeWriteEnriched && this.redactor.enabled) this.redactor.redact(record, { inPlace: true });
    if (this.profile.freezeRecords) freezeRecord(record);

    const delivery = await this.dispatcher.dispatch(record, {
      awaitDelivery: awaitDelivery || this.awaitTransportDelivery,
      throwOnError: options.throwOnError === true || (awaitDelivery && options.throwOnError !== false),
    });
    if (this.plugins.hasHook('afterWrite')) {
      await this.plugins.runHook('afterWrite', record, { ...hookContext, delivery });
    }

    const durationMs = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e6;
    this.metricsRegistry.increment('records_accepted');
    this.metricsRegistry.increment(`level_${level}`);
    this.metricsRegistry.observe('pipeline_duration_ms', durationMs);
    this.metricsRegistry.gauge('pending_records', this._pending.size);
    this.metricsRegistry.gauge('pipeline_queue_items', this.pipelineQueue.queue.length);
    this.metricsRegistry.gauge('pipeline_queue_bytes', this.pipelineQueue.queuedBytes);

    try { this.emit('record', record); } catch (error) { this.diagnostics.report('LOGGER_OBSERVER_ERROR', error); }
    try { this.emit('log', record); } catch (error) { this.diagnostics.report('LOGGER_OBSERVER_ERROR', error); }
    return record;
  }

  _processorDrop() {
    this.metricsRegistry.increment('records_processor_dropped');
    return null;
  }

  emergency(message, data, options) { return this.log('emergency', message, data, options); }
  alert(message, data, options) { return this.log('alert', message, data, options); }
  critical(message, data, options) { return this.log('critical', message, data, options); }
  error(message, data, options) { return this.log('error', message, data, options); }
  urgent(message, data, options) { return this.log('urgent', message, data, options); }
  priority(message, data, options) { return this.log('priority', message, data, options); }
  warn(message, data, options) { return this.log('warn', message, data, options); }
  notice(message, data, options) { return this.log('notice', message, data, options); }
  success(message, data, options) { return this.log('success', message, data, options); }
  info(message, data, options) { return this.log('info', message, data, options); }
  debug(message, data, options) { return this.log('debug', message, data, options); }
  metric(message, data, options) { return this.log('metric', message, data, options); }
  metrics(message, data, options) { return this.log('metrics', message, data, options); }
  important(message, data, options) { return this.log('important', message, data, options); }

  use(processor, options = {}) { this.processors.use(processor, options); return this; }
  processor(processor, options = {}) { return this.use(processor, options); }
  plugin(plugin) { this.plugins.use(plugin); return this; }

  transport(transport, options = {}) { return this.addTransport(transport, options); }

  addTransport(transport, options = {}) {
    if (transport && !transport.serializer) {
      try { transport.serializer = this.serializer; } catch {}
    }
    this.dispatcher.add(transport, options);
    return this;
  }

  removeTransport(nameOrTransport, options = {}) {
    return this.dispatcher.remove(nameOrTransport, options);
  }

  child(context = {}, options = {}) {
    return new LoggerRuntime({
      ...options,
      level: options.level || this.level,
      channel: options.channel || this.channelName,
      context,
      tags: options.tags || [],
      boundError: options.boundError,
      sampleRate: options.sampleRate ?? this.sampleRate,
      onceKey: options.onceKey,
      ratePolicy: options.ratePolicy,
      disabled: options.disabled,
      defaultLevel: options.defaultLevel,
      compatibilityViewFactory: options.compatibilityViewFactory || this.compatibilityViewFactory,
      processors: options.processors || cloneProcessors(this.processors),
      plugins: options.plugins || clonePlugins(this.plugins),
      _parent: this,
      _shared: {
        profile: this.profile,
        levelRegistry: this.levelRegistry,
        level: this.level,
        serializer: this.serializer,
        diagnostics: this.diagnostics,
        metricsRegistry: this.metricsRegistry,
        contextManager: this.contextManager,
        errorNormalizer: this.errorNormalizer,
        recordFactory: this.recordFactory,
        redactor: this.redactor,
        formatter: this.formatter,
        compatibilityViewFactory: this.compatibilityViewFactory,
        dispatcher: this.dispatcher,
        pipelineQueue: this.pipelineQueue,
        service: this.service,
        environment: this.environment,
        staticContext: this.staticContext,
        tags: this.tags,
        sharedState: this.sharedState,
      },
    });
  }

  with(context = {}) { return this.child(context); }
  withTags(...tags) { return this.child({}, { tags: tags.flat().filter((value) => value != null).map(String) }); }
  channel(name) { return this.child({}, { channel: String(name || 'app') }); }
  withError(error, context = {}) { return this.child(context, { boundError: error }); }
  when(condition) { return condition ? this : this.child({}, { disabled: true }); }
  sample(rate) { return this.child({}, { sampleRate: rate }); }
  once(key = 'default') { return this.child({}, { onceKey: String(key) }); }

  rateLimit(key, options = {}) {
    return this.child({}, {
      ratePolicy: {
        key: String(key || 'default'),
        limit: Math.max(1, Number(options.limit) || 1),
        intervalMs: Math.max(1, Number(options.intervalMs || options.windowMs) || 1000),
      },
    });
  }

  at(level) {
    const logger = this;
    const normalized = this.levelRegistry.resolveName(level);
    return Object.freeze({
      log(message, data, options) { return logger.log(normalized, message, data, options); },
      logAsync(message, data, options) { return logger.logAsync(normalized, message, data, options); },
      isEnabled() { return logger.isLevelEnabled(normalized); },
      child(context) { return logger.child(context).at(normalized); },
    });
  }

  forRequest(req, extra = {}) { return this.child({ ...requestContext(req), ...extra }); }
  forJob(job, extra = {}) { return this.child({ ...jobContext(job), ...extra }); }

  runWithContext(context, callback, ...args) {
    return this.contextManager.run(context, callback, ...args);
  }

  getContext() { return this.contextManager.get(); }

  time(label, context = {}) {
    const started = process.hrtime.bigint();
    let ended = false;
    return Object.freeze({
      end: (first = 'info', second = {}) => {
        if (ended) return null;
        ended = true;
        const durationMs = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e6;
        const level = typeof first === 'string' ? first : 'info';
        const data = typeof first === 'string' ? (second && typeof second === 'object' ? second : {}) : (first || {});
        this.log(level, label, { ...context, ...data, durationMs });
        return durationMs;
      },
      stop: (first = 'info', second = {}) => {
        if (ended) return null;
        ended = true;
        const durationMs = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e6;
        const level = typeof first === 'string' ? first : 'info';
        const data = typeof first === 'string' ? (second && typeof second === 'object' ? second : {}) : (first || {});
        this.log(level, label, { ...context, ...data, durationMs });
        return durationMs;
      },
      cancel: () => { ended = true; },
    });
  }

  async measure(label, operation, context = {}, options = {}) {
    if (typeof operation !== 'function') throw new TypeError('logger.measure requires an operation function.');
    const started = process.hrtime.bigint();
    try {
      const result = await operation();
      const durationMs = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e6;
      this.log(options.level || 'info', label, { ...context, durationMs, success: true });
      return result;
    } catch (error) {
      const durationMs = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e6;
      this.log(options.errorLevel || 'error', label, { ...context, durationMs, success: false }, { error });
      throw error;
    }
  }

  clearOnce() { this.sharedState.onceKeys.clear(); return this; }
  resetRateLimits() { this.sharedState.rateBuckets.clear(); return this; }

  async ready() {
    await this.plugins.runHook('ready', this, { logger: this });
    await Promise.allSettled(this.dispatcher.adapters.map((adapter) => adapter.transport?.ready?.()));
    return this;
  }

  async flush(options = {}) {
    await this.pipelineQueue.flush(options);
    await Promise.allSettled([...this._pending]);
    await this.dispatcher.flush(options);
    this.metricsRegistry.gauge('pending_records', 0);
  }

  async reopen(options = {}) {
    return await this.dispatcher.reopen(options);
  }

  async close(options = {}) {
    if (this._closed) return;
    this.state = 'draining';
    if (this.lifecycle.is('ready', 'degraded')) this.lifecycle.transition('draining');

    if (this.ownsPipelineQueue) {
      this.pipelineQueue.accepting = false;
      await this.pipelineQueue.flush(options);
    } else {
      await Promise.allSettled([...this._pending]);
    }

    this.state = 'closing';
    if (!this.lifecycle.is('closing', 'closed')) this.lifecycle.transition('closing');
    if (this.ownsDispatcher) await this.dispatcher.close(options);
    await this.plugins.close({ logger: this });
    this._closed = true;
    this.state = 'closed';
    if (!this.lifecycle.is('closed')) this.lifecycle.transition('closed');
    this.removeAllListeners();
  }

  destroy(options) { return this.close(options); }

  health() {
    const transports = this.dispatcher.health();
    const degraded = transports.some((item) => item.status === 'degraded');
    const pipeline = this.pipelineQueue.snapshot();
    return {
      status: this._closed ? 'closed' : (degraded ? 'degraded' : 'healthy'),
      state: this.state,
      level: this.level,
      profile: this.profile.name,
      uptimeMs: Date.now() - this.createdAt,
      pending: this._pending.size,
      pipeline,
      transports,
      diagnostics: this.diagnostics.snapshot().slice(-20),
    };
  }

  stats() {
    const metrics = this.metricsRegistry.snapshot();
    const counters = { ...metrics.counters };
    for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(metrics.counters)) counters[`${name}_total`] ??= value;
    return {
      metrics,
      counters,
      gauges: { ...metrics.gauges },
      histograms: { ...metrics.histograms },
      pipeline: this.pipelineQueue.snapshot(),
      transports: this.dispatcher.stats(),
      processors: this.processors.snapshot(),
      plugins: this.plugins.snapshot(),
    };
  }

  metricsSnapshot() { return this.metricsRegistry.snapshot(); }
  prometheus(labels = {}) { return this.toPrometheus(labels); }

  diagnosticsSnapshot() { return this.diagnostics.snapshot(); }
  toPrometheus(labels = {}) { return this.metricsRegistry.toPrometheus({ service: this.service, ...labels }); }
}

/** Bare structured runtime factory; root createLogger returns the legacy-compatible facade. */
export function createLogger(options = {}) {
  const logger = new LoggerRuntime(options);
  for (const transport of options.transports || []) {
    if (!transport.serializer) {
      try { transport.serializer = logger.serializer; } catch {}
    }
  }
  return logger;
}

export default LoggerRuntime;

FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/config/LoggerConfiguration.js
================================================

'use strict';

import defaultConfig from '../../../config/logger.js';
import { COMPATIBILITY_PROFILES } from '../compatibility/CompatibilityProfile.js';

const BLOCKED = new Set(['__proto__', 'prototype', 'constructor']);

function isObject(value) {
  return value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);
}

function clone(value, seen = new WeakMap()) {
  if (value == null || typeof value !== 'object') return value;
  if (seen.has(value)) return seen.get(value);
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    const output = [];
    seen.set(value, output);
    for (const item of value) output.push(clone(item, seen));
    return output;
  }
  const output = Object.create(null);
  seen.set(value, output);
  for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(value)) {
    if (!BLOCKED.has(key)) output[key] = clone(item, seen);
  }
  return output;
}

function merge(base, override) {
  if (!isObject(base)) return clone(override);
  const output = clone(base);
  if (!isObject(override)) return output;
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(override)) {
    if (BLOCKED.has(key)) continue;
    output[key] = isObject(value) && isObject(output[key]) ? merge(output[key], value) : clone(value);
  }
  return output;
}

function freezeDeep(value, seen = new WeakSet()) {
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || seen.has(value)) return value;
  seen.add(value);
  for (const item of Object.values(value)) freezeDeep(item, seen);
  return Object.freeze(value);
}

function atPath(value, path, fallback) {
  const parts = Array.isArray(path) ? path : String(path || '').split('.').filter(Boolean);
  let current = value;
  for (const part of parts) {
    if (current == null || !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(current, part)) return fallback;
    current = current[part];
  }
  return current === undefined ? fallback : current;
}

export class LoggerConfigurationError extends Error {
  constructor(message, diagnostics = []) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'LoggerConfigurationError';
    this.code = 'LOGGER_CONFIGURATION_ERROR';
    this.diagnostics = diagnostics;
  }
}

/** Immutable, validated logger configuration snapshot. */
export class LoggerConfiguration {
  constructor(configuration = {}, options = {}) {
    const defaults = options.defaults === false ? {} : (options.defaults || defaultConfig);
    this.value = merge(defaults, configuration || {});
    this.diagnostics = this.validate();
    if (options.strict !== false && this.diagnostics.some((item) => item.severity === 'error')) {
      throw new LoggerConfigurationError('RawNode Logger configuration is invalid.', this.diagnostics);
    }
    freezeDeep(this.value);
    Object.freeze(this.diagnostics);
  }

  get(path, fallback = undefined) { return atPath(this.value, path, fallback); }
  has(path) { return atPath(this.value, path, Symbol.for('missing')) !== Symbol.for('missing'); }
  snapshot() { return clone(this.value); }
  toJSON() { return this.snapshot(); }
  with(overrides = {}, options = {}) {
    return new LoggerConfiguration(merge(this.value, overrides), { defaults: false, ...options });
  }

  validate() {
    const diagnostics = [];
    const push = (severity, code, path, message) => diagnostics.push(Object.freeze({ severity, code, path, message }));
    const profile = String(this.value.profile || 'v1');
    if (!COMPATIBILITY_PROFILES[profile]) push('error', 'LOGGER_PROFILE_UNKNOWN', 'profile', `Unknown compatibility profile [${profile}].`);
    if (!this.value.level || typeof this.value.level !== 'string') push('error', 'LOGGER_LEVEL_INVALID', 'level', 'Logger level must be a string.');

    const queue = this.value.queue || {};
    for (const field of ['maxItems', 'maxBytes', 'budgetMs', 'maxPerSlice']) {
      if (queue[field] != null && (!Number.isFinite(Number(queue[field])) || Number(queue[field]) < 0)) {
        push('error', 'LOGGER_QUEUE_LIMIT_INVALID', `queue.${field}`, `${field} must be a non-negative finite number.`);
      }
    }

    const channels = this.value.channels;
    if (!isObject(channels) || !Object.keys(channels).length) {
      push('error', 'LOGGER_CHANNELS_MISSING', 'channels', 'At least one logger channel is required.');
      return diagnostics;
    }
    const selected = String(this.value.channel || this.value.default || '');
    if (selected && !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(channels, selected)) {
      push('error', 'LOGGER_DEFAULT_CHANNEL_MISSING', 'channel', `Default channel [${selected}] is not defined.`);
    }

    for (const [name, definition] of Object.entries(channels)) {
      if (!isObject(definition) && typeof definition !== 'function') {
        push('error', 'LOGGER_CHANNEL_INVALID', `channels.${name}`, 'Channel definition must be an object or factory function.');
        continue;
      }
      if (!isObject(definition)) continue;
      const driver = String(definition.driver || 'console');
      if (driver === 'stack') {
        const members = definition.channels;
        if (!Array.isArray(members) || !members.length) {
          push('error', 'LOGGER_STACK_EMPTY', `channels.${name}.channels`, 'Stack channels require a non-empty channels array.');
        } else {
          for (const member of members) {
            if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(channels, String(member))) {
              push('error', 'LOGGER_STACK_MEMBER_MISSING', `channels.${name}.channels`, `Stack member [${member}] is not defined.`);
            }
          }
        }
      }
      if (driver === 'audit' && !definition.secret && !definition.key && !definition.keyResolver) {
        push('warning', 'LOGGER_AUDIT_KEY_MISSING', `channels.${name}`, 'Audit channel has no explicit key; APP_KEY/LOG_AUDIT_SECRET must be available at runtime.');
      }
    }
    return diagnostics;
  }
}

export { clone as cloneLoggerConfiguration, merge as mergeLoggerConfiguration };
export default LoggerConfiguration;

FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/security/Redactor.js

'use strict';

const BLOCKED_KEYS = new Set(['__proto__', 'prototype', 'constructor']);
const DEFAULT_KEYS = Object.freeze([
  /pass(?:word|wd)?/i,
  /secret/i,
  /token/i,
  /api[_-]?key/i,
  /private[_-]?key/i,
  /authorization/i,
  /cookie/i,
  /session(?:[_-]?(?:id|key|secret))?/i,
  /credential/i,
  /signature/i,
  /(?:^|[_-])jwt(?:$|[_-])/i,
  /pepper/i,
  /client[_-]?secret/i,
  /card(?:[_-]?number)?/i,
  /cvv|cvc/i,
]);

const ANSI_PATTERN = /[\u001B\u009B][[\]()#;?]*(?:(?:(?:[a-zA-Z\d]*(?:;[-a-zA-Z\d/#&.:=?%@~_]+)*)?\u0007)|(?:(?:\d{1,4}(?:[;:]\d{0,4})*)?[\dA-PR-TZcf-nq-uy=><~]))/g;
const BIDI_PATTERN = /[\u061C\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E\u2066-\u2069]/g;
const JWT_PATTERN = /\beyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\b/g;
const BEARER_PATTERN = /\bBearer\s+[^\s,;]+/gi;
const URI_PASSWORD_PATTERN = /(\w+:\/\/[^:/\s]+:)[^@/\s]+(@)/g;
const QUERY_SECRET_PATTERN = /([?&](?:token|access_token|refresh_token|api_key|apikey|secret|password|signature)=)[^&#\s]*/gi;

function patternMatches(pattern, value) {
  try {
    pattern.lastIndex = 0;
    return pattern.test(String(value));
  } catch { return false; }
}

function shouldRedactKey(key, patterns) {
  return patterns.some((pattern) => patternMatches(pattern, key));
}

export function sanitizeString(value, options = {}) {
  let output = String(value);
  output = output.replace(BEARER_PATTERN, 'Bearer [REDACTED]');
  output = output.replace(JWT_PATTERN, '[REDACTED_JWT]');
  output = output.replace(URI_PASSWORD_PATTERN, '$1[REDACTED]$2');
  output = output.replace(QUERY_SECRET_PATTERN, '$1[REDACTED]');
  if (options.sanitizeControls) {
    output = output
      .replace(/\r/g, '\\r')
      .replace(/\n/g, '\\n')
      .replace(/\0/g, '\\0')
      .replace(/[\u0001-\u0008\u000B\u000C\u000E-\u001F\u007F]/g, '');
  }
  if (options.stripAnsi) output = output.replace(ANSI_PATTERN, '');
  if (options.blockBidiControls) output = output.replace(BIDI_PATTERN, '');
  return output;
}

export class Redactor {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.enabled = options.enabled !== false;
    this.replacement = options.replacement || '[REDACTED]';
    this.keyPatterns = [...DEFAULT_KEYS, ...(options.keyPatterns || [])];
    this.paths = new Set((options.paths || []).map(String));
    this.allowPaths = new Set((options.allowPaths || []).map(String));
    this.sanitizeControls = options.sanitizeControls === true;
    this.stripAnsi = options.stripAnsi === true;
    this.blockBidiControls = options.blockBidiControls !== false;
    this.maxDepth = Math.max(1, Number(options.maxDepth) || 64);
  }

  redact(value, options = {}) {
    if (!this.enabled) return value;
    if (options.inPlace === true) return this._visitInPlace(value, '$', 0, new WeakMap());
    return this._visit(value, '$', 0, new WeakMap());
  }

  _visitInPlace(value, path, depth, seen) {
    if (typeof value === 'string') return sanitizeString(value, this);
    if (value == null || typeof value !== 'object') return value;
    if (depth >= this.maxDepth) return '[MaxRedactionDepth]';
    if (seen.has(value)) return seen.get(value);
    seen.set(value, value);

    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index++) {
        value[index] = this._visitInPlace(value[index], `${path}[${index}]`, depth + 1, seen);
      }
      return value;
    }

    let descriptors;
    try { descriptors = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptors(value); }
    catch { return '[Uninspectable]'; }

    for (const [key, descriptor] of Object.entries(descriptors)) {
      if (!descriptor.enumerable) continue;
      if (BLOCKED_KEYS.has(key)) {
        try { delete value[key]; } catch {}
        continue;
      }
      const itemPath = `${path}.${key}`;
      let next;
      if (!('value' in descriptor)) {
        next = '[Accessor]';
      } else if (this.allowPaths.has(itemPath)) {
        next = typeof descriptor.value === 'string' ? sanitizeString(descriptor.value, this) : descriptor.value;
      } else if (this.paths.has(itemPath) || shouldRedactKey(key, this.keyPatterns)) {
        next = this.replacement;
      } else {
        next = this._visitInPlace(descriptor.value, itemPath, depth + 1, seen);
      }

      try { value[key] = next; }
      catch {
        try {
          Object.defineProperty(value, key, {
            value: next,
            enumerable: descriptor.enumerable,
            configurable: descriptor.configurable,
            writable: true,
          });
        } catch {}
      }
    }
    return value;
  }

  _visit(value, path, depth, seen) {
    if (typeof value === 'string') return sanitizeString(value, this);
    if (value == null || typeof value !== 'object') return value;
    if (depth >= this.maxDepth) return '[MaxRedactionDepth]';
    if (seen.has(value)) return seen.get(value);

    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      const output = [];
      seen.set(value, output);
      for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index++) {
        output.push(this._visit(value[index], `${path}[${index}]`, depth + 1, seen));
      }
      return output;
    }

    const output = Object.create(null);
    seen.set(value, output);
    let descriptors;
    try { descriptors = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptors(value); }
    catch { return '[Uninspectable]'; }

    for (const [key, descriptor] of Object.entries(descriptors)) {
      if (BLOCKED_KEYS.has(key)) continue;
      const itemPath = `${path}.${key}`;
      if (!('value' in descriptor)) {
        output[key] = '[Accessor]';
        continue;
      }
      const item = descriptor.value;
      if (this.allowPaths.has(itemPath)) {
        output[key] = typeof item === 'string' ? sanitizeString(item, this) : item;
      } else if (this.paths.has(itemPath) || shouldRedactKey(key, this.keyPatterns)) {
        output[key] = this.replacement;
      } else {
        output[key] = this._visit(item, itemPath, depth + 1, seen);
      }
    }
    return output;
  }
}

export { DEFAULT_KEYS, ANSI_PATTERN, BIDI_PATTERN };
export default Redactor;

'use strict';

const ANSI = /[\u001B\u009B][[\]()#;?]*(?:(?:(?:[a-zA-Z\d]*(?:;[-a-zA-Z\d\/#&.:=?%@~_]+)*)?\u0007)|(?:(?:\d{1,4}(?:;\d{0,4})*)?[\dA-PR-TZcf-nq-uy=><~]))/g;
const BIDI = /[\u061C\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E\u2066-\u2069]/g;
const C0 = /[\u0000-\u0008\u000B\u000C\u000E-\u001F\u007F]/g;

export class ControlCharacterSanitizer {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.options = {
      escapeNewlines: true,
      stripAnsi: true,
      blockBidi: true,
      stripControls: true,
      ...options,
    };
  }

  sanitize(value, overrides = {}) {
    const options = { ...this.options, ...overrides };
    let text = String(value ?? '');
    if (options.stripAnsi) text = text.replace(ANSI, '');
    if (options.blockBidi) text = text.replace(BIDI, '');
    if (options.stripControls) text = text.replace(C0, '');
    if (options.escapeNewlines) text = text.replace(/\r/g, '\\r').replace(/\n/g, '\\n');
    return text;
  }
}

export { ANSI as ANSI_PATTERN, BIDI as BIDI_PATTERN };
export default ControlCharacterSanitizer;

'use strict';

export { default as Redactor, sanitizeString, DEFAULT_KEYS as DEFAULT_REDACTION_KEYS } from './Redactor.js';
export { default as SensitiveFieldRegistry, DEFAULT_PATTERNS as DEFAULT_SENSITIVE_PATTERNS } from './SensitiveFieldRegistry.js';

FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/security/AuditChain.js

'use strict';

import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';

function stableStringify(value) {
  if (value == null || typeof value !== 'object') return JSON.stringify(value);
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map(stableStringify).join(',')}]`;
  const keys = Object.keys(value).sort();
  return `{${keys.map((key) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${stableStringify(value[key])}`).join(',')}}`;
}

function safeEqual(a, b) {
  const left = Buffer.from(String(a));
  const right = Buffer.from(String(b));
  return left.length === right.length && timingSafeEqual(left, right);
}

export class AuditChain {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    const key = options.key ?? options.secret;
    if (!key && !options.keyResolver) throw new TypeError('AuditChain requires a key, secret, or keyResolver.');
    this.key = key;
    this.keyResolver = options.keyResolver;
    this.keyId = options.keyId || 'default';
    this.algorithm = options.algorithm || 'sha256';
    this.previousHash = options.previousHash || 'GENESIS';
    this.sequence = Number(options.sequence) || 0;
  }

  async resolveKey(keyId = this.keyId) {
    if (this.keyResolver) return await this.keyResolver(keyId);
    return this.key;
  }

  async sign(record) {
    const sequence = ++this.sequence;
    const payload = {
      sequence,
      previousHash: this.previousHash,
      record,
    };
    const key = await this.resolveKey(this.keyId);
    const hash = createHmac(this.algorithm, key).update(stableStringify(payload)).digest('hex');
    this.previousHash = hash;
    return {
      ...record,
      audit: {
        version: 1,
        algorithm: `hmac-${this.algorithm}`,
        keyId: this.keyId,
        sequence,
        previousHash: payload.previousHash,
        hash,
      },
    };
  }

  static async verify(records, options = {}) {
    let previousHash = options.previousHash || 'GENESIS';
    let expectedSequence = Number(options.sequence) || 0;
    const keyResolver = options.keyResolver || (async () => options.key);
    const failures = [];

    for (let index = 0; index < records.length; index++) {
      const record = records[index];
      const audit = record?.audit;
      if (!audit) {
        failures.push({ index, reason: 'missing-audit' });
        continue;
      }
      expectedSequence++;
      const unsigned = { ...record };
      delete unsigned.audit;
      const payload = { sequence: audit.sequence, previousHash: audit.previousHash, record: unsigned };
      const key = await keyResolver(audit.keyId);
      const expectedHash = createHmac(String(audit.algorithm || 'hmac-sha256').replace(/^hmac-/, ''), key)
        .update(stableStringify(payload))
        .digest('hex');
      if (audit.sequence !== expectedSequence) failures.push({ index, reason: 'sequence', expected: expectedSequence, actual: audit.sequence });
      if (audit.previousHash !== previousHash) failures.push({ index, reason: 'previous-hash', expected: previousHash, actual: audit.previousHash });
      if (!safeEqual(audit.hash, expectedHash)) failures.push({ index, reason: 'hash', expected: expectedHash, actual: audit.hash });
      previousHash = audit.hash;
    }

    return {
      ok: failures.length === 0,
      valid: failures.length === 0,
      count: records.length,
      lines: records.length,
      failures,
      lastHash: previousHash,
      finalHash: previousHash,
    };
  }
}

export { stableStringify };
export default AuditChain;

'use strict';

const DEFAULT_PATTERNS = Object.freeze([
  /pass(?:word|wd)?/i,
  /secret/i,
  /private[_-]?key/i,
  /api[_-]?key/i,
  /access[_-]?token/i,
  /refresh[_-]?token/i,
  /auth(?:orization)?/i,
  /cookie/i,
  /session(?:id|_id|-id|key|secret)?/i,
  /credential/i,
  /client[_-]?secret/i,
  /signature/i,
  /pepper/i,
  /encryption[_-]?key/i,
  /jwt/i,
  /bearer/i,
  /card(?:number|_number|-number)?/i,
  /cvv|cvc/i,
]);

export class SensitiveFieldRegistry {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.patterns = [...DEFAULT_PATTERNS, ...(options.patterns || [])];
    this.keys = new Set((options.keys || []).map((key) => String(key).toLowerCase()));
  }

  add(patternOrKey) {
    if (patternOrKey instanceof RegExp) this.patterns.push(patternOrKey);
    else this.keys.add(String(patternOrKey).toLowerCase());
    return this;
  }

  has(key) {
    const value = String(key || '');
    if (this.keys.has(value.toLowerCase())) return true;
    return this.patterns.some((pattern) => pattern.test(value));
  }

  snapshot() {
    return { keys: [...this.keys], patterns: this.patterns.map(String) };
  }
}

export { DEFAULT_PATTERNS };
export default SensitiveFieldRegistry;

'use strict';

import http from 'node:http';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import { URL } from 'node:url';

const MAGIC = '258EAFA5-E914-47DA-95CA-C5AB0DC85B11';

function frame(payload) {
  const data = Buffer.isBuffer(payload) ? payload : Buffer.from(String(payload));
  let header;
  if (data.length < 126) {
    header = Buffer.allocUnsafe(2);
    header[1] = data.length;
  } else if (data.length <= 0xffff) {
    header = Buffer.allocUnsafe(4);
    header[1] = 126;
    header.writeUInt16BE(data.length, 2);
  } else {
    header = Buffer.allocUnsafe(10);
    header[1] = 127;
    header.writeBigUInt64BE(BigInt(data.length), 2);
  }
  header[0] = 0x81;
  return Buffer.concat([header, data]);
}

function authorized(req, options) {
  if (!options.token) return true;
  try {
    const url = new URL(req.url || '/', `http://${req.headers.host || 'localhost'}`);
    const supplied = url.searchParams.get('token') || req.headers.authorization?.replace(/^Bearer\s+/i, '');
    if (!supplied) return false;
    const left = Buffer.from(String(supplied));
    const right = Buffer.from(String(options.token));
    return left.length === right.length && crypto.timingSafeEqual(left, right);
  } catch { return false; }
}

function originAllowed(req, origins) {
  if (!origins || origins === '*') return true;
  const origin = req.headers.origin;
  if (!origin) return true;
  return origins.includes(origin);
}

export class InspectorServer {
  constructor(transport, portOrOptions = 9988) {
    if (!transport) throw new TypeError('InspectorServer requires an InspectorTransport.');
    const options = typeof portOrOptions === 'number' ? { port: portOrOptions } : (portOrOptions || {});
    this.transport = transport;
    this.options = {
      host: options.host || '127.0.0.1',
      port: Number.isInteger(Number(options.port)) && Number(options.port) >= 0 ? Number(options.port) : 9988,
      path: options.path || '/',
      token: options.token || null,
      origins: options.origins === '*' ? '*' : (Array.isArray(options.origins) ? options.origins : null),
      maxClientBufferBytes: Math.max(1024, Number(options.maxClientBufferBytes) || 4 * 1024 * 1024),
      maxClients: Math.max(1, Number(options.maxClients) || 100),
    };
    this.clients = new Set();
    this.server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
      res.statusCode = 404;
      res.end('RawNode Logger Inspector');
    });
    this._onRecord = (entry) => this.broadcast(entry);
    this.transport.on(this.transport.channel, this._onRecord);
    this.server.on('upgrade', (req, socket) => this._upgrade(req, socket));
  }

  _upgrade(req, socket) {
    let pathname = '/';
    try { pathname = new URL(req.url || '/', `http://${req.headers.host || 'localhost'}`).pathname; } catch {}
    if (pathname !== this.options.path) return this._reject(socket, 404, 'Not Found');
    if (this.clients.size >= this.options.maxClients) return this._reject(socket, 429, 'Too Many Requests');
    if (String(req.headers.upgrade || '').toLowerCase() !== 'websocket') return this._reject(socket, 400, 'Bad Request');
    if (!authorized(req, this.options)) return this._reject(socket, 401, 'Unauthorized');
    if (!originAllowed(req, this.options.origins)) return this._reject(socket, 403, 'Forbidden');
    const key = req.headers['sec-websocket-key'];
    if (!key) return this._reject(socket, 400, 'Bad Request');
    const accept = crypto.createHash('sha1').update(`${key}${MAGIC}`).digest('base64');
    socket.write([
      'HTTP/1.1 101 Switching Protocols',
      'Upgrade: websocket',
      'Connection: Upgrade',
      `Sec-WebSocket-Accept: ${accept}`,
      '\r\n',
    ].join('\r\n'));
    socket.setNoDelay(true);
    socket._rawnodeInspectorQueuedBytes = 0;
    this.clients.add(socket);
    const cleanup = () => this.clients.delete(socket);
    socket.on('close', cleanup);
    socket.on('error', cleanup);
    socket.on('data', (chunk) => {
      if ((chunk[0] & 0x0f) === 0x8) socket.end();
      if ((chunk[0] & 0x0f) === 0x9) {
        try { socket.write(Buffer.from([0x8a, 0x00])); } catch {}
      }
    });
    const history = this.transport.getBufferedLogs({ limit: 100 });
    for (const record of history) this._send(socket, { type: 'history', record });
  }

  _reject(socket, status, reason) {
    try { socket.end(`HTTP/1.1 ${status} ${reason}\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n`); } catch {}
  }

  _send(socket, value) {
    if (socket.destroyed || !socket.writable) return false;
    const output = frame(JSON.stringify(value));
    const queued = socket.writableLength + output.length;
    if (queued > this.options.maxClientBufferBytes) {
      socket.destroy(new Error('Inspector client exceeded backpressure limit.'));
      return false;
    }
    return socket.write(output);
  }

  broadcast(entry) {
    for (const client of this.clients) this._send(client, { type: 'record', record: entry });
  }

  buildFrame(payload) { return frame(payload); }

  async start() {
    if (this.server.listening) return this.address();
    await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      this.server.once('error', reject);
      this.server.listen(this.options.port, this.options.host, resolve);
    });
    return this.address();
  }

  address() { return this.server.address(); }

  async close() {
    this.transport.off?.(this.transport.channel, this._onRecord);
    for (const client of this.clients) client.destroy();
    this.clients.clear();
    if (this.server.listening) await new Promise((resolve) => this.server.close(resolve));
  }
}

export default InspectorServer;

================================================
FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/inspector/InspectorTransport.js

'use strict';

import BaseTransport from '../transports/BaseTransport.js';

/**
 * Bounded in-memory transport for live inspection.
 * Records entering this transport are expected to have completed the canonical
 * redaction phase. A safe clone is retained so inspector consumers cannot mutate
 * the runtime's delivery record or its compatibility view.
 */
export class InspectorTransport extends BaseTransport {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super({ ...options, name: options.name || 'inspector' });
    this.channel = options.channel || 'inspector-log';
    this.maxBuffer = Math.max(1, Number(options.maxBuffer) || 500);
    this.maxBufferBytes = Math.max(1024, Number(options.maxBufferBytes) || 8 * 1024 * 1024);
    this.cloneRecords = options.cloneRecords !== false;
    this.buffer = [];
    this.bufferBytes = 0;
    this.filter = typeof options.filter === 'function' ? options.filter : null;
    this.stats.dropped = 0;
  }

  async writeRecord(record) {
    if (this.closed || this.silent) return;
    if (this.filter && !this.filter(record)) return;

    const stored = this.cloneRecords ? this.serializer.clone(record) : record;
    const bytes = Buffer.byteLength(this.serializer.stringify(stored));
    if (bytes > this.maxBufferBytes) {
      this.stats.dropped++;
      this.emit('dropped', { reason: 'record-too-large', bytes, maxBufferBytes: this.maxBufferBytes });
      return;
    }

    while (this.buffer.length && (this.buffer.length >= this.maxBuffer || this.bufferBytes + bytes > this.maxBufferBytes)) {
      const removed = this.buffer.shift();
      this.bufferBytes = Math.max(0, this.bufferBytes - removed.bytes);
      this.stats.dropped++;
    }

    this.buffer.push({ record: stored, bytes });
    this.bufferBytes += bytes;
    this._written(bytes);
    this.emit(this.channel, stored);
    this.emit('written', stored);
  }

  getBufferedLogs(options = {}) {
    let records = this.buffer.map((entry) => entry.record);
    if (options.level) records = records.filter((record) => record.level === options.level);
    if (options.channel) records = records.filter((record) => record.channel === options.channel);
    if (options.limit) records = records.slice(-Math.max(0, Number(options.limit) || 0));
    return options.clone === false ? [...records] : records.map((record) => this.serializer.clone(record));
  }

  clear() {
    const count = this.buffer.length;
    this.buffer.length = 0;
    this.bufferBytes = 0;
    return count;
  }

  health() {
    return {
      ...super.health(),
      bufferedItems: this.buffer.length,
      bufferedBytes: this.bufferBytes,
      maxBuffer: this.maxBuffer,
      maxBufferBytes: this.maxBufferBytes,
    };
  }

  async close(options = {}) {
    this.clear();
    await super.close(options);
  }
}

export default InspectorTransport;

'use strict';

function runWithTimeout(factory, timeoutMs, label, signal = null) {
  if (signal?.aborted) return Promise.reject(signal.reason || new Error(`${label} aborted.`));
  if (!timeoutMs || timeoutMs <= 0) return Promise.resolve().then(factory);
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    let settled = false;
    const cleanup = () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      signal?.removeEventListener?.('abort', onAbort);
    };
    const finish = (callback, value) => {
      if (settled) return;
      settled = true;
      cleanup();
      callback(value);
    };
    const onAbort = () => finish(reject, signal.reason || Object.assign(new Error(`${label} aborted.`), { code: 'LOGGER_PROCESSOR_ABORTED' }));
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      finish(reject, Object.assign(new Error(`${label} timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms.`), { code: 'LOGGER_PROCESSOR_TIMEOUT' }));
    }, timeoutMs);
   
    signal?.addEventListener?.('abort', onAbort, { once: true });
    Promise.resolve().then(factory).then(
      (value) => finish(resolve, value),
      (error) => finish(reject, error),
    );
  });
}

function normalizePhase(value) {
  const phase = String(value || 'enrich').toLowerCase();
  if (['pre', 'before-redaction', 'beforeredaction', 'enrich', 'trusted'].includes(phase)) return 'before-redaction';
  if (['post', 'after-redaction', 'afterredaction', 'untrusted'].includes(phase)) return 'after-redaction';
  if (phase === 'before-write' || phase === 'beforewrite') return 'before-write';
  return phase;
}

export class ProcessorRegistry {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.processors = [];
    this.errorPolicy = options.errorPolicy || 'isolate';
    this.diagnostics = options.diagnostics || null;
  }

  use(processor, options = {}) {
    if (typeof processor !== 'function') throw new TypeError('Logger processor must be a function.');
    const entry = {
      name: options.name || processor.name || `processor-${this.processors.length + 1}`,
      processor,
      priority: Number.isFinite(Number(options.priority)) ? Number(options.priority) : 10,
      timeoutMs: Math.max(0, Number(options.timeoutMs) || 0),
      phase: normalizePhase(options.phase),
      trusted: options.trusted !== false,
      enabled: options.enabled !== false,
    };
    if (this.processors.some((item) => item.name === entry.name) && options.replace !== true) {
      throw new Error(`Logger processor [${entry.name}] is already registered.`);
    }
    if (options.replace === true) this.remove(entry.name);
    this.processors.push(entry);
    this.processors.sort((a, b) => a.priority - b.priority);
    return () => this.remove(entry.name);
  }

  remove(name) {
    const before = this.processors.length;
    this.processors = this.processors.filter((entry) => entry.name !== name);
    return this.processors.length !== before;
  }

  hasPhase(phase, filter = {}) {
    const normalized = normalizePhase(phase);
    return this.processors.some((entry) => {
      if (!entry.enabled || entry.phase !== normalized) return false;
      if (filter.trustedOnly === true && entry.trusted !== true) return false;
      if (filter.untrustedOnly === true && entry.trusted === true) return false;
      return true;
    });
  }

  async run(record, context = {}, filter = {}) {
    let current = record;
    const phase = filter.phase ? normalizePhase(filter.phase) : null;
    const trustedOnly = filter.trustedOnly === true;
    const untrustedOnly = filter.untrustedOnly === true;
    const snapshot = [...this.processors];
    for (const entry of snapshot) {
      if (!entry.enabled) continue;
      if (phase && entry.phase !== phase) continue;
      if (trustedOnly && entry.trusted !== true) continue;
      if (untrustedOnly && entry.trusted === true) continue;
      if (context.signal?.aborted) throw context.signal.reason || new Error('Logger processing aborted.');
      try {
        const result = await runWithTimeout(
          () => entry.processor(current, { ...context, processor: entry }),
          entry.timeoutMs,
          `Logger processor [${entry.name}]`,
          context.signal,
        );
        if (result === false || result === null) return null;
        if (result !== undefined) current = result;
      } catch (error) {
        this.diagnostics?.report?.('LOGGER_PROCESSOR_ERROR', error, { processor: entry.name, phase: entry.phase });
        if (this.errorPolicy === 'propagate') throw error;
      }
    }
    return current;
  }

  runPhase(phase, record, context = {}, filter = {}) {
    return this.run(record, context, { ...filter, phase });
  }

  clone(options = {}) {
    const copy = new ProcessorRegistry({
      errorPolicy: options.errorPolicy || this.errorPolicy,
      diagnostics: options.diagnostics || this.diagnostics,
    });
    copy.processors = this.processors.map((entry) => ({ ...entry }));
    return copy;
  }

  snapshot() {
    return this.processors.map(({ name, priority, timeoutMs, phase, trusted, enabled }) => ({
      name,
      priority,
      timeoutMs,
      phase,
      trusted,
      enabled,
    }));
  }
}

export default ProcessorRegistry;

'use strict';

export function createSamplingProcessor(options = {}) {
  const rate = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(options.rate ?? 1)));
  const levels = options.levels ? new Set(options.levels.map(String)) : null;
  const random = options.random || Math.random;
  return function samplingProcessor(record) {
    if (levels && !levels.has(record.level)) return record;
    if (rate >= 1 || random() < rate) return record;
    return null;
  };
}

export default createSamplingProcessor;


================================================
FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/processing/DeduplicationProcessor.js

'use strict';

export function createDeduplicationProcessor(options = {}) {
  const windowMs = Math.max(0, Number(options.windowMs) || 1000);
  const maxEntries = Math.max(1, Number(options.maxEntries) || 10_000);
  const key = options.key || ((record) => `${record.level}:${record.channel}:${record.message}`);
  const seen = new Map();
  return function deduplicationProcessor(record) {
    const now = record.timeUnixMs || Date.now();
    const id = String(key(record));
    const previous = seen.get(id);
    seen.set(id, now);
    if (seen.size > maxEntries) {
      const oldest = seen.keys().next().value;
      seen.delete(oldest);
    }
    if (previous != null && now - previous < windowMs) return null;
    return record;
  };
}

export default createDeduplicationProcessor;

'use strict';

FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/plugins/PluginManager.js

'use strict';

import { AsyncLocalStorage } from 'node:async_hooks';

function runWithTimeout(factory, ms, label, signal = null) {
  if (signal?.aborted) return Promise.reject(signal.reason || new Error(`${label} aborted.`));
  if (!ms || ms <= 0) return Promise.resolve().then(factory);
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    let settled = false;
    const cleanup = () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      signal?.removeEventListener?.('abort', onAbort);
    };
    const finish = (callback, value) => {
      if (settled) return;
      settled = true;
      cleanup();
      callback(value);
    };
    const onAbort = () => finish(reject, signal.reason || Object.assign(new Error(`${label} aborted.`), { code: 'LOGGER_PLUGIN_ABORTED' }));
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      finish(reject, Object.assign(new Error(`${label} timed out after ${ms}ms.`), { code: 'LOGGER_PLUGIN_TIMEOUT' }));
    }, ms);
   
    signal?.addEventListener?.('abort', onAbort, { once: true });
    Promise.resolve().then(factory).then(
      (value) => finish(resolve, value),
      (error) => finish(reject, error),
    );
  });
}

export class PluginManager {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.plugins = [];
    this.duplicate = options.duplicate || 'reject';
    this.errorPolicy = options.errorPolicy || 'isolate';
    this.defaultTimeoutMs = Math.max(0, Number(options.timeoutMs) || 0);
    this.maxDepth = Math.max(1, Number(options.maxDepth) || 16);
    this.diagnostics = options.diagnostics || null;
    this.scope = new AsyncLocalStorage();
    this.stats = {
      hooks: 0,
      errors: 0,
      timeouts: 0,
      recursionSkips: 0,
      removed: 0,
    };
  }

  use(plugin = {}) {
    if (!plugin || typeof plugin !== 'object') throw new TypeError('Logger plugin must be an object.');
    const name = String(plugin.name || `plugin-${this.plugins.length + 1}`);
    const existing = this.plugins.findIndex((entry) => entry.name === name);
    if (existing !== -1) {
      if (this.duplicate === 'ignore') return this;
      if (this.duplicate === 'replace') this.plugins.splice(existing, 1);
      else throw new Error(`Logger plugin [${name}] is already registered.`);
    }
    this.plugins.push({
      name,
      plugin,
      priority: Number.isFinite(Number(plugin.priority)) ? Number(plugin.priority) : 10,
      timeoutMs: Math.max(0, Number(plugin.timeoutMs) || this.defaultTimeoutMs),
      trusted: plugin.trusted === true,
      enabled: plugin.enabled !== false,
    });
    this.plugins.sort((a, b) => a.priority - b.priority);
    return this;
  }

  remove(name) {
    const before = this.plugins.length;
    this.plugins = this.plugins.filter((entry) => entry.name !== name);
    const removed = before - this.plugins.length;
    this.stats.removed += removed;
    return removed > 0;
  }

  hasHook(hookName, filter = {}) {
    return this.plugins.some((entry) => {
      if (!entry.enabled) return false;
      if (filter.trustedOnly === true && entry.trusted !== true) return false;
      if (filter.untrustedOnly === true && entry.trusted === true) return false;
      const hook = entry.plugin?.hooks?.[hookName] || entry.plugin?.[hookName];
      return typeof hook === 'function';
    });
  }

  async runHook(hookName, value, context = {}) {
    if (!this.hasHook(hookName, context)) return value;
    const parent = this.scope.getStore() || { depth: 0, path: [] };
    if (parent.depth >= this.maxDepth) {
      this.stats.recursionSkips++;
      this.diagnostics?.report?.('LOGGER_PLUGIN_RECURSION_LIMIT', new Error(`Logger plugin hook recursion exceeded ${this.maxDepth}.`), {
        hook: hookName,
        path: parent.path,
      });
      return value;
    }

    return await this.scope.run({ depth: parent.depth + 1, path: [...parent.path, hookName] }, async () => {
      let current = value;
      const snapshot = [...this.plugins];
      for (const entry of snapshot) {
        if (!entry.enabled) continue;
        if (context.trustedOnly === true && entry.trusted !== true) continue;
        if (context.untrustedOnly === true && entry.trusted === true) continue;
        const hook = entry.plugin?.hooks?.[hookName] || entry.plugin?.[hookName];
        if (typeof hook !== 'function') continue;
        try {
          this.stats.hooks++;
          const result = await runWithTimeout(
            () => hook.call(entry.plugin, current, { ...context, plugin: entry.name, trusted: entry.trusted }),
            entry.timeoutMs,
            `Logger plugin [${entry.name}] hook [${hookName}]`,
            context.signal,
          );
          if (result === false || result === null) return null;
          if (result !== undefined) current = result;
        } catch (error) {
          this.stats.errors++;
          if (error?.code === 'LOGGER_PLUGIN_TIMEOUT') this.stats.timeouts++;
          this.diagnostics?.report?.('LOGGER_PLUGIN_ERROR', error, { plugin: entry.name, hook: hookName });
          if (this.errorPolicy === 'propagate') throw error;
        }
      }
      return current;
    });
  }

  clone(options = {}) {
    const copy = new PluginManager({
      duplicate: options.duplicate || this.duplicate,
      errorPolicy: options.errorPolicy || this.errorPolicy,
      timeoutMs: options.timeoutMs ?? this.defaultTimeoutMs,
      maxDepth: options.maxDepth ?? this.maxDepth,
      diagnostics: options.diagnostics || this.diagnostics,
    });
    copy.plugins = this.plugins.map((entry) => ({ ...entry }));
    return copy;
  }

  async close(context = {}) {
    await this.runHook('close', undefined, context);
    this.plugins.length = 0;
    this.scope.disable();
  }

  snapshot() {
    return {
      plugins: this.plugins.map(({ name, priority, timeoutMs, trusted, enabled }) => ({
        name,
        priority,
        timeoutMs,
        trusted,
        enabled,
      })),
      stats: { ...this.stats },
    };
  }
}

export default PluginManager;

'use strict';

function dataText(data, serializer) {
  if (data == null) return '';
  if (typeof data === 'string') return data;
  return serializer.stringify(data);
}

export class TextFormatter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.includeTimestamp = options.includeTimestamp !== false;
    this.includeLevel = options.includeLevel !== false;
    this.includeChannel = options.includeChannel === true;
    this.includeContext = options.includeContext === true;
    this.separator = options.separator || ' ';
  }

  format(record, serializer) {
    const parts = [];
    if (this.includeTimestamp) parts.push(`[${record.timestamp}]`);
    if (this.includeLevel) parts.push(`[${String(record.level).toUpperCase()}]`);
    if (this.includeChannel && record.channel) parts.push(`[${record.channel}]`);
    parts.push(String(record.message ?? ''));
    if (record.data != null) parts.push(`=> ${dataText(record.data, serializer)}`);
    if (this.includeContext && record.context && Object.keys(record.context).length) {
      parts.push(`context=${serializer.stringify(record.context)}`);
    }
    if (record.error) parts.push(`error=${serializer.stringify(record.error)}`);
    return parts.join(this.separator);
  }
}

export default TextFormatter;

'use strict';

export { default as SafeSerializer, defaultSerializer, truncateUtf8 } from './SafeSerializer.js';
export { default as TextFormatter } from './TextFormatter.js';

FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/serialization/JsonFormatter.js

'use strict';

export class JsonFormatter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.omit = new Set(options.omit || []);
    this.pick = Array.isArray(options.pick) ? new Set(options.pick) : null;
  }

  format(record, serializer) {
    let value = record;
    if (this.pick || this.omit.size) {
      value = Object.create(null);
      for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(record)) {
        if (this.pick && !this.pick.has(key)) continue;
        if (this.omit.has(key)) continue;
        value[key] = item;
      }
    }
    return serializer.stringify(value);
  }
}

export default JsonFormatter;

FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/serialization/LogfmtFormatter.js
