'use strict';

function quote(value) {
  const text = String(value ?? '');
  return /[\s="\\]/.test(text) ? JSON.stringify(text) : text;
}

export class LogfmtFormatter {
  format(record, serializer) {
    const fields = {
      time: record.timestamp,
      level: record.level,
      message: record.message,
      channel: record.channel,
      service: record.service,
      request_id: record.requestId,
      trace_id: record.traceId,
      span_id: record.spanId,
      tenant_id: record.tenantId,
    };
    const output = [];
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(fields)) if (value != null && value !== '') output.push(`${key}=${quote(value)}`);
    if (record.data != null) output.push(`data=${quote(serializer.stringify(record.data))}`);
    if (record.error != null) output.push(`error=${quote(serializer.stringify(record.error))}`);
    return output.join(' ');
  }
}

export default LogfmtFormatter;


================================================
FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/serialization/SafeSerializer.js
================================================

'use strict';

const BLOCKED_KEYS = new Set(['__proto__', 'prototype', 'constructor']);

function finiteInteger(value, fallback, minimum = 0) {
  const number = Number(value);
  return Number.isSafeInteger(number) && number >= minimum ? number : fallback;
}

function truncateUtf8(value, maxBytes) {
  const text = String(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(maxBytes) || maxBytes <= 0 || Buffer.byteLength(text) <= maxBytes) return text;
  let low = 0;
  let high = text.length;
  while (low < high) {
    const middle = Math.ceil((low + high) / 2);
    if (Buffer.byteLength(text.slice(0, middle)) <= maxBytes) low = middle;
    else high = middle - 1;
  }
  return `${text.slice(0, low)}…[truncated]`;
}

function constructorName(value) {
  try { return value?.constructor?.name || 'Object'; }
  catch { return 'Object'; }
}

export class SafeSerializer {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.options = Object.freeze({
      maxDepth: finiteInteger(options.maxDepth, 24),
      maxKeys: finiteInteger(options.maxKeys, 4096),
      maxArrayItems: finiteInteger(options.maxArrayItems, 10_000),
      maxStringBytes: finiteInteger(options.maxStringBytes, 256 * 1024),
      maxBufferBytes: finiteInteger(options.maxBufferBytes, 256 * 1024),
      maxRecordBytes: finiteInteger(options.maxRecordBytes, 2 * 1024 * 1024),
      getterPolicy: options.getterPolicy || 'describe',
      functionPolicy: options.functionPolicy || 'describe',
      bigintPolicy: options.bigintPolicy || 'string',
      circularValue: options.circularValue || '[Circular]',
      blockedKeyPolicy: options.blockedKeyPolicy || 'drop',
      deterministic: options.deterministic === true,
      includeErrorStack: options.includeErrorStack !== false,
      includeNonEnumerable: options.includeNonEnumerable === true,
    });
  }

  normalize(value, overrides = {}) {
    const options = { ...this.options, ...(overrides || {}) };
    const state = {
      seen: new WeakMap(),
      keys: 0,
      options,
    };
    return this._visit(value, state, 0, '$');
  }

  stringify(value, overrides = {}) {
    const options = { ...this.options, ...(overrides || {}) };
    let normalized;
    try {
      normalized = this.normalize(value, options);
    } catch (error) {
      normalized = { serializerError: error?.message || String(error) };
    }

    let output;
    try {
      output = JSON.stringify(normalized);
    } catch (error) {
      output = JSON.stringify({ serializerError: error?.message || String(error) });
    }

    if (Buffer.byteLength(output) <= options.maxRecordBytes) return output;
    const truncated = {
      truncated: true,
      originalBytes: Buffer.byteLength(output),
      preview: truncateUtf8(output, Math.max(128, options.maxRecordBytes - 128)),
    };
    return JSON.stringify(truncated);
  }

  clone(value, overrides = {}) {
    return this.normalize(value, overrides);
  }

  _visit(value, state, depth, path) {
    const { options } = state;

    if (value === null || value === undefined) return value ?? null;
    if (typeof value === 'string') return truncateUtf8(value, options.maxStringBytes);
    if (typeof value === 'number') return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : String(value);
    if (typeof value === 'boolean') return value;
    if (typeof value === 'bigint') return options.bigintPolicy === 'number' ? Number(value) : `${value}n`;
    if (typeof value === 'symbol') return `[Symbol(${value.description || ''})]`;
    if (typeof value === 'function') {
      if (options.functionPolicy === 'drop') return undefined;
      return `[Function${value.name ? `: ${value.name}` : ''}]`;
    }

    if (depth >= options.maxDepth) return `[MaxDepth:${constructorName(value)}]`;
    if (state.seen.has(value)) return `${options.circularValue} ${state.seen.get(value)}`;
    state.seen.set(value, path);

    if (value instanceof Date) return Number.isNaN(value.getTime()) ? 'Invalid Date' : value.toISOString();
    if (value instanceof RegExp) return value.toString();
    if (value instanceof URL) return value.toString();

    if (value instanceof Error) {
      const output = Object.create(null);
      output.name = value.name || 'Error';
      output.message = truncateUtf8(value.message || '', options.maxStringBytes);
      if (options.includeErrorStack && value.stack) output.stack = truncateUtf8(value.stack, options.maxStringBytes);
      if (value.code !== undefined) output.code = this._visit(value.code, state, depth + 1, `${path}.code`);
      if (value.cause !== undefined) output.cause = this._visit(value.cause, state, depth + 1, `${path}.cause`);
      if (value instanceof AggregateError) {
        output.errors = this._visit(Array.from(value.errors || []), state, depth + 1, `${path}.errors`);
      }
      this._copyProperties(value, output, state, depth, path, new Set(['name', 'message', 'stack', 'code', 'cause', 'errors']));
      return output;
    }

    if (Buffer.isBuffer(value)) {
      const limit = Math.min(value.length, options.maxBufferBytes);
      return {
        type: 'Buffer',
        bytes: value.length,
        truncated: limit < value.length,
        data: value.subarray(0, limit).toString('base64'),
      };
    }

    if (ArrayBuffer.isView(value)) {
      const bytes = Buffer.from(value.buffer, value.byteOffset, value.byteLength);
      const limit = Math.min(bytes.length, options.maxBufferBytes);
      return {
        type: constructorName(value),
        bytes: bytes.length,
        truncated: limit < bytes.length,
        data: bytes.subarray(0, limit).toString('base64'),
      };
    }

    if (value instanceof ArrayBuffer) {
      const bytes = Buffer.from(value);
      const limit = Math.min(bytes.length, options.maxBufferBytes);
      return {
        type: 'ArrayBuffer',
        bytes: bytes.length,
        truncated: limit < bytes.length,
        data: bytes.subarray(0, limit).toString('base64'),
      };
    }

    if (value instanceof Map) {
      const entries = [];
      let index = 0;
      for (const [key, item] of value.entries()) {
        if (index >= options.maxArrayItems || state.keys >= options.maxKeys) break;
        state.keys++;
        entries.push([
          this._visit(key, state, depth + 1, `${path}.mapKey${index}`),
          this._visit(item, state, depth + 1, `${path}.mapValue${index}`),
        ]);
        index++;
      }
      return { type: 'Map', size: value.size, entries, truncated: index < value.size };
    }

    if (value instanceof Set) {
      const values = [];
      let index = 0;
      for (const item of value.values()) {
        if (index >= options.maxArrayItems || state.keys >= options.maxKeys) break;
        state.keys++;
        values.push(this._visit(item, state, depth + 1, `${path}.set${index}`));
        index++;
      }
      return { type: 'Set', size: value.size, values, truncated: index < value.size };
    }

    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      const limit = Math.min(value.length, options.maxArrayItems, Math.max(0, options.maxKeys - state.keys));
      const output = new Array(limit);
      for (let index = 0; index < limit; index++) {
        state.keys++;
        output[index] = this._visit(value[index], state, depth + 1, `${path}[${index}]`);
      }
      if (limit < value.length) output.push(`[${value.length - limit} more items]`);
      return output;
    }

    const output = Object.create(null);
    if (constructorName(value) !== 'Object') output.__type = constructorName(value);
    this._copyProperties(value, output, state, depth, path);
    return output;
  }

  _copyProperties(value, output, state, depth, path, excluded = new Set()) {
    let descriptors;
    try { descriptors = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptors(value); }
    catch { return output; }

    let keys = Object.keys(descriptors).filter((key) => !excluded.has(key) && (state.options.includeNonEnumerable || descriptors[key].enumerable));
    if (state.options.deterministic) keys.sort();

    for (const key of keys) {
      if (state.keys >= state.options.maxKeys) {
        output.__truncatedKeys = true;
        break;
      }
      if (BLOCKED_KEYS.has(key)) {
        if (state.options.blockedKeyPolicy === 'mark') output[`[blocked:${key}]`] = true;
        continue;
      }
      const descriptor = descriptors[key];
      state.keys++;
      if (!('value' in descriptor)) {
        if (state.options.getterPolicy === 'drop') continue;
        output[key] = descriptor.get ? '[Getter]' : '[Accessor]';
        continue;
      }
      const normalized = this._visit(descriptor.value, state, depth + 1, `${path}.${key}`);
      if (normalized !== undefined) output[key] = normalized;
    }
    return output;
  }
}

export { truncateUtf8 };
export const defaultSerializer = new SafeSerializer();
export default SafeSerializer;

FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/transports/HttpBatchTransport.js

'use strict';

import http from 'node:http';
import https from 'node:https';
import { URL } from 'node:url';
import BaseTransport from './BaseTransport.js';

export class HttpBatchTransport extends BaseTransport {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super({ ...options, name: options.name || 'http-batch' });
    if (options.serializer) this.serializer = options.serializer;
    this.url = new URL(options.url || options.endpoint);
    this.headers = { 'content-type': 'application/json', ...(options.headers || {}) };
    this.timeoutMs = Math.max(1, Number(options.timeoutMs) || 10_000);
    this.batchSize = Math.max(1, Number(options.batchSize) || 100);
    this.batchIntervalMs = Math.max(0, Number(options.batchIntervalMs ?? options.flushIntervalMs) || 10);
    this.maxRetries = Math.max(0, Number(options.maxRetries) || 2);
    this.retryDelayMs = Math.max(0, Number(options.retryDelayMs) || 250);
  }

  async writeRecord(record) {
    return await this.writeBatch([record]);
  }

  async writeBatch(records) {
    if (this.closed || this.silent || !records.length) return;
    const payload = this.serializer.stringify({ records });
    let attempt = 0;
    while (true) {
      try {
        const response = await this._request(payload);
        this._written(Buffer.byteLength(payload));
        this.emit('written', { records: records.length, response });
        return response;
      } catch (error) {
        if (attempt++ >= this.maxRetries) throw error;
        if (this.retryDelayMs) await new Promise((resolve) => { const timer = setTimeout(resolve, this.retryDelayMs * attempt); });
      }
    }
  }

  _request(payload) {
    const client = this.url.protocol === 'https:' ? https : http;
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const request = client.request(this.url, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { ...this.headers, 'content-length': Buffer.byteLength(payload) },
      }, (response) => {
        const chunks = [];
        let bytes = 0;
        response.on('data', (chunk) => { if (bytes < 64 * 1024) chunks.push(chunk); bytes += chunk.length; });
        response.on('end', () => {
          const body = Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8');
          if (response.statusCode >= 200 && response.statusCode < 300) resolve({ statusCode: response.statusCode, body });
          else reject(new Error(`HTTP log collector returned ${response.statusCode}: ${body.slice(0, 1000)}`));
        });
      });
      request.setTimeout(this.timeoutMs, () => request.destroy(new Error(`HTTP log request timed out after ${this.timeoutMs}ms.`)));
      request.on('error', reject);
      request.end(payload);
    });
  }
}

export default HttpBatchTransport;


================================================
FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/transports/StreamTransport.js

'use strict';

import { once } from 'node:events';
import BaseTransport from './BaseTransport.js';
import JsonFormatter from '../serialization/JsonFormatter.js';

export class StreamTransport extends BaseTransport {
  constructor(stream, options = {}) {
    if (!stream || typeof stream.write !== 'function') throw new TypeError('StreamTransport requires a writable stream.');
    super({ ...options, name: options.name || 'stream' });
    this.stream = stream;
    if (options.serializer) this.serializer = options.serializer;
    this.formatter = options.formatter || new JsonFormatter();
    this.endOnClose = options.endOnClose === true;
  }

  async writeRecord(record) {
    if (this.closed || this.silent) return;
    let output = typeof this.formatter === 'function' ? await this.formatter(record, this.serializer) : await this.formatter.format(record, this.serializer);
    if (!Buffer.isBuffer(output)) output = String(output);
    if (typeof output === 'string' && !output.endsWith('\n')) output += '\n';
    if (!this.stream.write(output)) await once(this.stream, 'drain');
    this._written(Buffer.isBuffer(output) ? output.length : Buffer.byteLength(output));
    this.emit('written', record);
  }

  async close() {
    if (this.closed) return;
    if (this.endOnClose && typeof this.stream.end === 'function') await new Promise((resolve) => this.stream.end(resolve));
    await super.close();
  }
}

export default StreamTransport;

'use strict';

import dgram from 'node:dgram';
import BaseTransport from './BaseTransport.js';

export class UdpTransport extends BaseTransport {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super({ ...options, name: options.name || 'udp' });
    if (options.serializer) this.serializer = options.serializer;
    this.host = options.host || '127.0.0.1';
    this.port = Number(options.port);
    if (!Number.isInteger(this.port) || this.port <= 0) throw new TypeError('UdpTransport requires a valid port.');
    this.type = options.type || 'udp4';
    this.maxPacketBytes = Math.max(256, Number(options.maxPacketBytes) || 8192);
    this.oversize = options.oversize || 'truncate';
    this.socket = dgram.createSocket(this.type);
    this.socket.unref?.();
    this.socket.on('error', (error) => this._failed(error));
  }

  async writeRecord(record) {
    if (this.closed || this.silent) return;
    let payload = Buffer.from(this.serializer.stringify(record));
    if (payload.length > this.maxPacketBytes) {
      if (this.oversize === 'drop') {
        this.emit('drop', { reason: 'oversize', bytes: payload.length, record });
        return { dropped: true };
      }
      payload = Buffer.from(this.serializer.stringify({
        id: record.id,
        timestamp: record.timestamp,
        level: record.level,
        message: String(record.message).slice(0, Math.max(0, this.maxPacketBytes - 256)),
        truncated: true,
        originalBytes: payload.length,
      }));
      if (payload.length > this.maxPacketBytes) payload = payload.subarray(0, this.maxPacketBytes);
    }
    await new Promise((resolve, reject) => this.socket.send(payload, this.port, this.host, (error) => error ? reject(error) : resolve()));
    this._written(payload.length);
    this.emit('written', record);
  }

  async close() {
    if (this.closed) return;
    await new Promise((resolve) => { try { this.socket.close(resolve); } catch { resolve(); } });
    await super.close();
  }
}

export default UdpTransport;

FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/transports/RedisStreamTransport.js

'use strict';

import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import BaseTransport from './BaseTransport.js';

function method(client, ...names) {
  for (const name of names) if (typeof client?.[name] === 'function') return client[name].bind(client);
  return null;
}

export class RedisStreamTransport extends BaseTransport {
  constructor(client, options = {}) {
    if (!client) throw new TypeError('RedisStreamTransport requires a Redis client.');
    super({ ...options, name: options.name || 'redis-stream' });
    this.client = client;
    if (options.serializer) this.serializer = options.serializer;
    this.stream = options.stream || 'rawnode:logs';
    this.maxLength = Math.max(100, Number(options.maxLength) || 100_000);
    this.approximate = options.approximate !== false;
    this.nodeId = options.nodeId || `${process.pid}`;
  }

  async writeRecord(record) {
    if (this.closed || this.silent) return;
    const xadd = method(this.client, 'xadd', 'xAdd');
    if (!xadd) throw new Error('Redis client does not expose XADD/xAdd.');
    const payload = this.serializer.stringify(record);
    const args = [this.stream, 'MAXLEN'];
    if (this.approximate) args.push('~');
    args.push(this.maxLength, '*',
      'id', record.id || randomUUID(),
      'payload', payload,
      'level', String(record.level || ''),
      'channel', String(record.channel || ''),
      'tenant', String(record.tenantId || ''),
      'node', this.nodeId,
      'createdAt', String(record.timeUnixMs || Date.now()));
    const id = await xadd(...args);
    this._written(Buffer.byteLength(payload));
    this.emit('written', { id, record });
    return id;
  }
}

export default RedisStreamTransport;


================================================
FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/transports/BaseTransport.js
================================================

'use strict';

import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';
import { defaultSerializer } from '../serialization/SafeSerializer.js';

export class BaseTransport extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super();
    this.rawnodeEnterpriseTransport = true;
    this.acceptsRecords = true;
    this.name = options.name || this.constructor.name;
    this.serializer = options.serializer || defaultSerializer;
    this.silent = options.silent === true;
    this.closed = false;
    this.createdAt = Date.now();
    // Error events are observable but never allowed to crash an application merely
    // because no listener has been installed.
    this.on('error', () => {});
    this.stats = {
      writes: 0,
      bytes: 0,
      errors: 0,
      flushes: 0,
      closes: 0,
      lastError: null,
      lastWriteAt: null,
    };
  }

  async writeRecord(_record) {
    throw new Error(`${this.constructor.name}.writeRecord(record) must be implemented.`);
  }

  async writeBatch(records) {
    for (const record of records) await this.writeRecord(record);
  }

  async flush() {
    this.stats.flushes++;
  }

  async reopen() {
    return false;
  }

  async close() {
    if (this.closed) return;
    this.closed = true;
    this.stats.closes++;
    this.removeAllListeners();
  }

  health() {
    return {
      name: this.name,
      status: this.closed ? 'closed' : (this.stats.lastError ? 'degraded' : 'healthy'),
      closed: this.closed,
      uptimeMs: Date.now() - this.createdAt,
      stats: { ...this.stats },
    };
  }

  _written(bytes = 0) {
    this.stats.writes++;
    this.stats.bytes += Number(bytes) || 0;
    this.stats.lastWriteAt = new Date().toISOString();
  }

  _failed(error) {
    this.stats.errors++;
    this.stats.lastError = {
      message: error?.message || String(error),
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    try { this.emit('error', error); } catch {}
  }
}

export default BaseTransport;


================================================
FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/transports/MemoryTransport.js
================================================

'use strict';

import BaseTransport from './BaseTransport.js';

export class MemoryTransport extends BaseTransport {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super({ ...options, name: options.name || 'memory' });
    this.maxItems = Math.max(1, Number(options.maxItems ?? options.limit) || 1000);
    this.limit = this.maxItems;
    this.records = [];
    this.clone = options.clone !== false;
    if (options.serializer) this.serializer = options.serializer;
  }

  async writeRecord(record) {
    if (this.closed || this.silent) return;
    const value = this.clone && this.serializer ? this.serializer.clone(record) : record;
    if (this.records.length >= this.maxItems) this.records.shift();
    this.records.push(value);
    this._written(this.serializer ? Buffer.byteLength(this.serializer.stringify(value)) : 0);
    this.emit('written', value);
  }

  all() { return [...this.records]; }
  latest(count = 1) { return this.records.slice(-Math.max(0, Number(count) || 0)); }
  clear() { const count = this.records.length; this.records.length = 0; return count; }
  find(predicate) { return this.records.find(predicate); }
  filter(predicate) { return this.records.filter(predicate); }
  atLevel(level) { return this.records.filter((record) => record.level === level); }
  snapshot() { return { name: this.name, stored: this.records.length, limit: this.maxItems, closed: this.closed }; }
}

export default MemoryTransport;


================================================
FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/transports/CallbackTransport.js
================================================

'use strict';

import BaseTransport from './BaseTransport.js';

export class CallbackTransport extends BaseTransport {
  constructor(callback, options = {}) {
    if (typeof callback !== 'function') throw new TypeError('CallbackTransport requires a callback.');
    super({ ...options, name: options.name || callback.name || 'callback' });
    this.callback = callback;
  }

  async writeRecord(record) {
    if (this.closed || this.silent) return;
    await this.callback(record, this);
    this._written(0);
    this.emit('written', record);
  }
}

export default CallbackTransport;


================================================
FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/transports/FileTransport.js
================================================

'use strict';

import fs from 'node:fs';
import fsp from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { pipeline } from 'node:stream/promises';
import { createGzip } from 'node:zlib';
import BaseTransport from './BaseTransport.js';
import TextFormatter from '../serialization/TextFormatter.js';
import JsonFormatter from '../serialization/JsonFormatter.js';

function safeInteger(value, fallback, minimum = 0) {
  const number = Number(value);
  return Number.isSafeInteger(number) && number >= minimum ? number : fallback;
}

function timestampForFile(date = new Date()) {
  return date.toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
}

async function pathExists(file) {
  try { await fsp.access(file); return true; }
  catch { return false; }
}

/** Fully asynchronous rotating file transport with retention and durability. */
export class FileTransport extends BaseTransport {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super({ ...options, name: options.name || 'file' });
    if (options.serializer) this.serializer = options.serializer;
    this.logDirectory = path.resolve(options.logDirectory || options.directory || path.join(process.cwd(), 'logs'));
    this.fileName = options.fileName || options.filename || 'application.log';
    this.filePath = path.join(this.logDirectory, this.fileName);
    this.maxSize = safeInteger(options.maxSize ?? options.maxBytes, 10 * 1024 * 1024, 1);
    this.rotateInterval = safeInteger(options.rotateInterval ?? options.rotateIntervalMs, 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000, 0);
    this.compressOnRotate = options.compressOnRotate === true || options.compress === true;
    this.removeUncompressed = options.removeUncompressed !== false;
    this.mode = options.mode == null ? 0o640 : Number(options.mode);
    this.directoryMode = options.directoryMode == null ? 0o750 : Number(options.directoryMode);
    this.durability = options.durability || (options.fsync === true ? 'fsync' : 'none');
    this.format = options.format || (options.json === false ? 'text' : 'json');
    this.formatter = options.formatter || (this.format === 'text'
      ? new TextFormatter(options.textOptions)
      : new JsonFormatter(options.jsonOptions));
    this.retention = {
      maxFiles: safeInteger(options.retention?.maxFiles ?? options.maxFiles, 14, 0),
      maxAgeMs: safeInteger(options.retention?.maxAgeMs ?? options.maxAgeMs, 0, 0),
      maxTotalBytes: safeInteger(options.retention?.maxTotalBytes ?? options.maxTotalBytes, 0, 0),
    };
    this.followSymlinks = options.followSymlinks === true;
    this.fileNamePattern = typeof options.fileNamePattern === 'function' ? options.fileNamePattern : null;
    this.stream = null;
    this.currentBytes = 0;
    this.rotationSequence = 0;
    this.nextRotationAt = this.rotateInterval > 0 ? Date.now() + this.rotateInterval : Infinity;
    this._opening = null;
    this._writeTail = Promise.resolve();
    this._compressionJobs = new Set();
    this._timer = null;
    this.autoUnref = options.autoUnref !== false;
    this.lazy = options.lazy !== false;
    this.awaitCompression = options.awaitCompression === true;
    this.compressionConcurrency = safeInteger(options.compressionConcurrency, 1, 1);
    this.externalRotationCheckMs = safeInteger(options.externalRotationCheckMs, 5_000, 0);
    this._lastExternalCheckAt = 0;
    this._openedIdentity = null;
    this._compressionQueue = [];
    this._compressionActive = 0;

    if (!this.lazy) this._opening = this._open();
    if (this.rotateInterval > 0) {
      this._timer = setInterval(() => {
        this.rotate({ reason: 'interval', force: false }).catch((error) => this._failed(error));
      }, this.rotateInterval);
      if (this.autoUnref) this._timer.unref?.();
    }
  }

  async ready() {
    if (this.closed) throw Object.assign(new Error(`File transport [${this.name}] is closed.`), { code: 'LOGGER_FILE_CLOSED' });
    if (!this._opening) {
      this._opening = this._open().catch((error) => {
        this._opening = null;
        throw error;
      });
    }
    await this._opening;
    return this;
  }

  async _open() {
    await fsp.mkdir(this.logDirectory, { recursive: true, mode: this.directoryMode });
    try { await fsp.chmod(this.logDirectory, this.directoryMode); } catch {}

    let stats = null;
    try {
      const linkStats = await fsp.lstat(this.filePath);
      if (linkStats.isSymbolicLink() && !this.followSymlinks) {
        const error = new Error(`Refusing to open symbolic-link log file [${this.filePath}].`);
        error.code = 'LOGGER_FILE_SYMLINK_DENIED';
        throw error;
      }
      stats = await fsp.stat(this.filePath);
    } catch (error) {
      if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error;
    }

    this.currentBytes = stats?.size || 0;
    this.stream = fs.createWriteStream(this.filePath, {
      flags: 'a',
      mode: this.mode,
      autoClose: true,
      highWaterMark: 64 * 1024,
    });
    const openedStream = this.stream;
    this.stream.on('error', (error) => this._failed(error));
    this.stream.on('close', () => {
      if (this.stream === openedStream && !this.closed) {
        this.stream = null;
        this._opening = null;
      }
    });
    await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      if (this.stream.pending === false) return resolve();
      this.stream.once('open', resolve);
      this.stream.once('error', reject);
    });
    try {
      const opened = await fsp.stat(this.filePath);
      this._openedIdentity = { dev: opened.dev, ino: opened.ino };
    } catch { this._openedIdentity = null; }
    this.emit('open', { file: this.filePath, bytes: this.currentBytes });
  }

  async writeRecord(record) {
    if (this.closed || this.silent) return;
    const task = async () => {
      await this.ready();
      await this._ensureCurrentFile();
      let output = typeof this.formatter === 'function'
        ? await this.formatter(record, this.serializer)
        : await this.formatter.format(record, this.serializer);
      if (!Buffer.isBuffer(output)) output = String(output);
      if (typeof output === 'string' && !output.endsWith('\n')) output += '\n';
      const bytes = Buffer.isBuffer(output) ? output.length : Buffer.byteLength(output);

      const dueBySize = this.currentBytes > 0 && this.currentBytes + bytes > this.maxSize;
      const dueByTime = this.currentBytes > 0 && Date.now() >= this.nextRotationAt;
      if (dueBySize || dueByTime) await this._rotateInternal({ reason: dueBySize ? 'size' : 'interval', force: true });

      await this._write(output);
      this.currentBytes += bytes;
      this._written(bytes);
      this.emit('written', record);
    };
    const next = this._writeTail.then(task, task);
    this._writeTail = next.catch((error) => {
      this._failed(error);
      throw error;
    });
    return await next;
  }

  async _write(output) {
    await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      let callbackDone = false;
      let drainDone = true;
      let settled = false;
      const finish = (error) => {
        if (settled) return;
        if (error) {
          settled = true;
          reject(error);
        } else if (callbackDone && drainDone) {
          settled = true;
          resolve();
        }
      };
      try {
        const accepted = this.stream.write(output, (error) => {
          callbackDone = true;
          finish(error);
        });
        if (accepted === false) {
          drainDone = false;
          this.stream.once('drain', () => {
            drainDone = true;
            finish();
          });
        }
      } catch (error) { finish(error); }
    });
  }

  async rotate(options = {}) {
    if (this.closed) return null;
    const normalized = { reason: 'manual', force: true, ...(options || {}) };
    const task = () => this._rotateInternal(normalized);
    const next = this._writeTail.then(task, task);
    this._writeTail = next.catch((error) => {
      this._failed(error);
      throw error;
    });
    return await next;
  }

  async _rotateInternal(options = {}) {
    await this.ready();
    const reason = options.reason || 'manual';
    const due = this.currentBytes > 0 && (reason === 'interval' ? Date.now() >= this.nextRotationAt : this.currentBytes >= this.maxSize);
    if (!options.force && !due) return null;
    if (this.currentBytes <= 0) {
      this.nextRotationAt = this.rotateInterval > 0 ? Date.now() + this.rotateInterval : Infinity;
      return null;
    }

    this.emit('rotateStart', { file: this.filePath, reason, bytes: this.currentBytes });
    await this._sync();
    await this._endStream();

    const extension = path.extname(this.fileName);
    const base = path.basename(this.fileName, extension);
    const metadata = {
      timestamp: timestampForFile(),
      pid: process.pid,
      sequence: ++this.rotationSequence,
      reason,
      bytes: this.currentBytes,
    };
    const defaultName = `${base}-${metadata.timestamp}-${metadata.pid}-${metadata.sequence}${extension || '.log'}`;
    const archiveName = this.fileNamePattern ? this.fileNamePattern(this.fileName, metadata) : defaultName;
    let archivePath = path.resolve(this.logDirectory, archiveName);
    if (!archivePath.startsWith(`${this.logDirectory}${path.sep}`)) {
      const error = new Error('fileNamePattern attempted to escape the log directory.');
      error.code = 'LOGGER_FILE_ARCHIVE_PATH_DENIED';
      throw error;
    }

    let collision = 0;
    while (await pathExists(archivePath)) {
      collision++;
      const archiveExtension = path.extname(archivePath);
      const archiveBase = path.basename(archivePath, archiveExtension);
      archivePath = path.join(this.logDirectory, `${archiveBase}-${collision}${archiveExtension}`);
    }

    let renameError = null;
    try { await fsp.rename(this.filePath, archivePath); }
    catch (error) { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') renameError = error; }
    finally {
      this.stream = null;
      this._opening = null;
      this._openedIdentity = null;
      this.currentBytes = 0;
      this.nextRotationAt = this.rotateInterval > 0 ? Date.now() + this.rotateInterval : Infinity;
      await this.ready();
    }
    if (renameError) throw renameError;

    let outputPath = archivePath;
    let compressionPending = false;
    if (this.compressOnRotate && await pathExists(archivePath)) {
      const job = this._scheduleCompression(archivePath);
      this._compressionJobs.add(job);
      job.finally(() => this._compressionJobs.delete(job)).catch(() => {});
      const shouldAwaitCompression = options.awaitCompression ?? (reason === 'manual' ? true : this.awaitCompression);
      if (shouldAwaitCompression) {
        outputPath = await job;
        await this._applyRetention();
      } else {
        outputPath = `${archivePath}.gz`;
        compressionPending = true;
        job.then(() => this._applyRetention()).catch((error) => this._failed(error));
      }
    } else {
      await this._applyRetention();
    }
    this.stats.rotations = (this.stats.rotations || 0) + 1;
    const result = { archive: outputPath, source: archivePath, reason, compressionPending };
    this.emit('rotate', result);
    return result;
  }

  async _ensureCurrentFile() {
    if (!this.externalRotationCheckMs || !this.stream) return;
    const now = Date.now();
    if (now - this._lastExternalCheckAt < this.externalRotationCheckMs) return;
    this._lastExternalCheckAt = now;
    let stats = null;
    try { stats = await fsp.stat(this.filePath); } catch (error) { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; }
    const changed = !stats
      || (this._openedIdentity && (stats.dev !== this._openedIdentity.dev || stats.ino !== this._openedIdentity.ino));
    if (!changed) {
      if (stats && Number.isFinite(stats.size)) this.currentBytes = stats.size;
      return;
    }
    await this._sync();
    await this._endStream();
    this._opening = null;
    this._openedIdentity = null;
    await this.ready();
    this.emit('externalRotate', { file: this.filePath });
  }

  _scheduleCompression(file) {
    let resolve;
    let reject;
    const promise = new Promise((res, rej) => { resolve = res; reject = rej; });
    this._compressionQueue.push({ file, resolve, reject });
    this._pumpCompression();
    return promise;
  }

  _pumpCompression() {
    while (this._compressionActive < this.compressionConcurrency && this._compressionQueue.length) {
      const job = this._compressionQueue.shift();
      this._compressionActive++;
      Promise.resolve()
        .then(() => this._compress(job.file))
        .then(job.resolve, job.reject)
        .finally(() => {
          this._compressionActive--;
          this._pumpCompression();
        });
    }
  }

  async _compress(file) {
    const destination = `${file}.gz`;
    await pipeline(
      fs.createReadStream(file),
      createGzip(),
      fs.createWriteStream(destination, { mode: this.mode }),
    );
    // Resolve rotation only after the archive is visible and stat-able. This
    // gives callers a durable postcondition across filesystems and CI runners.
    await fsp.stat(destination);
    if (this.removeUncompressed) await fsp.unlink(file).catch(() => {});
    this.stats.compressed = (this.stats.compressed || 0) + 1;
    this.emit('compress', { source: file, archive: destination });
    return destination;
  }

  async _applyRetention() {
    const { maxFiles, maxAgeMs, maxTotalBytes } = this.retention;
    if (!maxFiles && !maxAgeMs && !maxTotalBytes) return;
    const extension = path.extname(this.fileName);
    const base = path.basename(this.fileName, extension);
    const entries = await fsp.readdir(this.logDirectory, { withFileTypes: true });
    const archives = [];
    for (const entry of entries) {
      if (!entry.isFile() || !entry.name.startsWith(`${base}-`) || entry.name === this.fileName) continue;
      const absolute = path.join(this.logDirectory, entry.name);
      try {
        const stats = await fsp.stat(absolute);
        archives.push({ path: absolute, name: entry.name, size: stats.size, mtimeMs: stats.mtimeMs });
      } catch {}
    }
    archives.sort((a, b) => b.mtimeMs - a.mtimeMs);
    const now = Date.now();
    let total = 0;
    for (let index = 0; index < archives.length; index++) {
      const archive = archives[index];
      total += archive.size;
      const removeByCount = maxFiles > 0 && index >= maxFiles;
      const removeByAge = maxAgeMs > 0 && now - archive.mtimeMs > maxAgeMs;
      const removeByBytes = maxTotalBytes > 0 && total > maxTotalBytes;
      if (removeByCount || removeByAge || removeByBytes) {
        await fsp.unlink(archive.path).catch(() => {});
        this.stats.retentionDeletes = (this.stats.retentionDeletes || 0) + 1;
        this.emit('retentionDelete', archive);
      }
    }
  }

  async _endStream() {
    const stream = this.stream;
    this.stream = null;
    if (!stream || stream.destroyed || stream.writableEnded) return;
    await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const onError = (error) => { cleanup(); reject(error); };
      const onFinish = () => { cleanup(); resolve(); };
      const cleanup = () => {
        stream.off('error', onError);
        stream.off('finish', onFinish);
      };
      stream.once('error', onError);
      stream.once('finish', onFinish);
      stream.end();
    });
  }

  async _sync() {
    if (this.durability === 'none' || !this.stream || !Number.isInteger(this.stream.fd)) return;
    const method = this.durability === 'fdatasync' && typeof fs.fdatasync === 'function' ? fs.fdatasync : fs.fsync;
    await new Promise((resolve, reject) => method(this.stream.fd, (error) => error ? reject(error) : resolve()));
  }

  async flush() {
    await this._writeTail;
    await Promise.allSettled([...this._compressionJobs]);
    await this._sync();
    await super.flush();
  }

  async reopen() {
    if (this.closed) return false;
    const task = async () => {
      await this._sync();
      await this._endStream();
      this._opening = null;
      await this.ready();
      this.emit('reopen', { file: this.filePath });
      return true;
    };
    const next = this._writeTail.then(task, task);
    this._writeTail = next.catch((error) => { this._failed(error); throw error; });
    return await next;
  }

  async close() {
    if (this.closed) return;
    if (this._timer) {
      clearInterval(this._timer);
      this._timer = null;
    }
    await this.flush();
    await this._endStream();
    this._opening = null;
    await super.close();
  }

  health() {
    return {
      ...super.health(),
      file: this.filePath,
      currentBytes: this.currentBytes,
      maxSize: this.maxSize,
      nextRotationAt: Number.isFinite(this.nextRotationAt) ? this.nextRotationAt : null,
      compressionJobs: this._compressionJobs.size,
      compressionQueued: this._compressionQueue.length,
      compressionActive: this._compressionActive,
      durability: this.durability,
    };
  }
}

export default FileTransport;

FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/transports/TcpTransport.js

'use strict';

import net from 'node:net';
import tls from 'node:tls';
import BaseTransport from './BaseTransport.js';

export class TcpTransport extends BaseTransport {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super({ ...options, name: options.name || (options.tls ? 'tls' : 'tcp') });
    if (options.serializer) this.serializer = options.serializer;
    this.host = options.host || '127.0.0.1';
    this.port = Number(options.port);
    if (!Number.isInteger(this.port) || this.port <= 0) throw new TypeError('TcpTransport requires a valid port.');
    this.tls = options.tls === true || options.secure === true;
    this.tlsOptions = { ...(options.tlsOptions || {}) };
    this.connectTimeoutMs = Math.max(1, Number(options.connectTimeoutMs) || 5000);
    this.reconnectDelayMs = Math.max(0, Number(options.reconnectDelayMs) || 250);
    this.socket = null;
    this._connecting = null;
  }

  async connect() {
    if (this.closed) throw new Error(`TCP transport [${this.name}] is closed.`);
    if (this.socket && !this.socket.destroyed && this.socket.writable) return this.socket;
    if (this._connecting) return await this._connecting;
    this._connecting = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const options = { host: this.host, port: this.port, ...this.tlsOptions };
      const socket = this.tls ? tls.connect(options) : net.createConnection(options);
      const timer = setTimeout(() => socket.destroy(new Error(`TCP logger connection timed out after ${this.connectTimeoutMs}ms.`)), this.connectTimeoutMs);
     
      const connectedEvent = this.tls ? 'secureConnect' : 'connect';
      const onError = (error) => { clearTimeout(timer); reject(error); };
      socket.once('error', onError);
      socket.once(connectedEvent, () => {
        clearTimeout(timer);
        socket.off('error', onError);
        socket.setNoDelay(true);
        socket.setKeepAlive(true);
        socket.on('error', (error) => this._failed(error));
        socket.on('close', () => { if (this.socket === socket) this.socket = null; });
        this.socket = socket;
        this.emit('connect', { host: this.host, port: this.port, tls: this.tls });
        resolve(socket);
      });
    }).finally(() => { this._connecting = null; });
    return await this._connecting;
  }

  async writeRecord(record) {
    if (this.closed || this.silent) return;
    let socket;
    try { socket = await this.connect(); }
    catch (error) {
      if (this.reconnectDelayMs) await new Promise((resolve) => { const timer = setTimeout(resolve, this.reconnectDelayMs); });
      throw error;
    }
    const payload = `${this.serializer.stringify(record)}\n`;
    await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      let callbackDone = false;
      let drainDone = true;
      let settled = false;
      const cleanup = () => socket.off('error', onError);
      const finish = (error) => {
        if (settled) return;
        if (error) { settled = true; cleanup(); reject(error); return; }
        if (callbackDone && drainDone) { settled = true; cleanup(); resolve(); }
      };
      const onError = (error) => finish(error);
      socket.once('error', onError);
      try {
        const accepted = socket.write(payload, (error) => { callbackDone = true; finish(error); });
        if (!accepted) {
          drainDone = false;
          socket.once('drain', () => { drainDone = true; finish(); });
        }
      } catch (error) { finish(error); }
    });
    this._written(Buffer.byteLength(payload));
    this.emit('written', record);
  }

  async close() {
    if (this.closed) return;
    const socket = this.socket;
    this.socket = null;
    if (socket && !socket.destroyed) await new Promise((resolve) => { socket.end(resolve); socket.once('close', resolve); });
    await super.close();
  }

  health() {
    return { ...super.health(), host: this.host, port: this.port, connected: Boolean(this.socket && !this.socket.destroyed), tls: this.tls };
  }
}

export default TcpTransport;

FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/transports/AuditFileTransport.js

'use strict';

import fs from 'node:fs';
import fsp from 'node:fs/promises';
import readline from 'node:readline';
import FileTransport from './FileTransport.js';
import AuditChain from '../security/AuditChain.js';

async function readLastAuditState(filePath) {
  let handle;
  try {
    handle = await fsp.open(filePath, 'r');
    const stats = await handle.stat();
    if (!stats.size) return { previousHash: 'GENESIS', sequence: 0 };
    const length = Math.min(stats.size, 1024 * 1024);
    const buffer = Buffer.allocUnsafe(length);
    await handle.read(buffer, 0, length, stats.size - length);
    const lines = buffer.toString('utf8').trim().split('\n');
    for (let index = lines.length - 1; index >= 0; index--) {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(lines[index]);
        if (parsed?.audit?.hash) {
          return {
            previousHash: String(parsed.audit.hash),
            sequence: Number(parsed.audit.sequence) || 0,
          };
        }
      } catch {}
    }
    return { previousHash: 'GENESIS', sequence: 0 };
  } catch (error) {
    if (error?.code === 'ENOENT') return { previousHash: 'GENESIS', sequence: 0 };
    throw error;
  } finally {
    await handle?.close?.().catch(() => {});
  }
}

/**
 * Append-only, tamper-evident NDJSON transport.
 * Each row contains an HMAC chained to the previous row, and the chain resumes
 * from an existing file after process restart.
 */
export class AuditFileTransport extends FileTransport {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    const key = options.key ?? options.secret ?? process.env.LOG_AUDIT_SECRET ?? process.env.APP_KEY;
    if (!key && !options.keyResolver) throw new TypeError('AuditFileTransport requires a key, secret, or keyResolver.');
    super({
      ...options,
      name: options.name || 'audit-file',
      format: 'json',
      json: true,
      durability: options.durability || (options.fsync === false ? 'none' : 'fsync'),
    });
    this.auditChain = options.auditChain || new AuditChain({
      ...(options.audit || {}),
      ...options,
      key,
    });
    this._auditReady = readLastAuditState(this.filePath).then((state) => {
      this.auditChain.previousHash = state.previousHash;
      this.auditChain.sequence = state.sequence;
      return state;
    });
  }

  async ready() {
    await this._auditReady;
    return await super.ready();
  }

  async writeRecord(record) {
    await this._auditReady;
    const signed = await this.auditChain.sign(record);
    return await super.writeRecord(signed);
  }

  health() {
    return {
      ...super.health(),
      audit: {
        keyId: this.auditChain.keyId,
        algorithm: `hmac-${this.auditChain.algorithm}`,
        sequence: this.auditChain.sequence,
        previousHash: this.auditChain.previousHash,
      },
    };
  }

  static async verifyFile(filePath, options = {}) {
    const key = options.key ?? options.secret ?? process.env.LOG_AUDIT_SECRET ?? process.env.APP_KEY;
    if (!key && !options.keyResolver) throw new TypeError('Audit verification requires a key, secret, or keyResolver.');

    const input = fs.createReadStream(filePath, { encoding: 'utf8' });
    const reader = readline.createInterface({ input, crlfDelay: Infinity });
    let previousHash = options.previousHash || 'GENESIS';
    let sequence = Number(options.sequence) || 0;
    let lines = 0;
    const failures = [];

    try {
      for await (const text of reader) {
        if (!text.trim()) continue;
        lines++;
        try {
          const record = JSON.parse(text);
          const result = await AuditChain.verify([record], {
            key,
            keyResolver: options.keyResolver,
            previousHash,
            sequence,
          });
          for (const failure of result.failures) failures.push({ line: lines, ...failure });
          previousHash = result.lastHash;
          sequence = Number(record?.audit?.sequence) || sequence + 1;
        } catch (error) {
          failures.push({ line: lines, reason: 'parse', message: error?.message || String(error) });
        }
      }
    } finally {
      reader.close();
      input.destroy();
    }

    return {
      valid: failures.length === 0,
      ok: failures.length === 0,
      lines,
      count: lines,
      failures,
      finalHash: previousHash,
      lastHash: previousHash,
      sequence,
    };
  }
}

export default AuditFileTransport;

'use strict';

import { once } from 'node:events';
import BaseTransport from './BaseTransport.js';
import TextFormatter from '../serialization/TextFormatter.js';
import JsonFormatter from '../serialization/JsonFormatter.js';

const ANSI = Object.freeze({
  emergency: '\x1b[1;31m',
  alert: '\x1b[1;31m',
  critical: '\x1b[1;31m',
  error: '\x1b[31m',
  urgent: '\x1b[31m',
  priority: '\x1b[33m',
  warn: '\x1b[33m',
  notice: '\x1b[37m',
  success: '\x1b[32m',
  important: '\x1b[1;32m',
  info: '\x1b[34m',
  debug: '\x1b[36m',
  metric: '\x1b[35m',
  metrics: '\x1b[35m',
});
const RESET = '\x1b[0m';

export class ConsoleTransport extends BaseTransport {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super({ ...options, name: options.name || 'console' });
    if (options.serializer) this.serializer = options.serializer;
    this.stdout = options.stdout || process.stdout;
    this.stderr = options.stderr || process.stderr;
    this.format = options.format || (options.json ? 'json' : 'text');
    this.colors = options.colors ?? (this.stdout.isTTY === true && !process.env.NO_COLOR);
    this.formatter = options.formatter || (this.format === 'json' ? new JsonFormatter(options.jsonOptions) : new TextFormatter(options.textOptions));
  }

  async writeRecord(record) {
    if (this.closed || this.silent) return;
    const definition = record.levelDefinition || null;
    const method = definition?.consoleMethod || (['emergency', 'alert', 'critical', 'error', 'urgent'].includes(record.level) ? 'error' : (record.level === 'warn' ? 'warn' : 'log'));
    const destination = method === 'error' || method === 'warn' ? this.stderr : this.stdout;
    let output = typeof this.formatter === 'function'
      ? await this.formatter(record, this.serializer)
      : await this.formatter.format(record, this.serializer);
    output = String(output);
    if (this.colors && this.format !== 'json') output = `${ANSI[record.level] || ''}${output}${ANSI[record.level] ? RESET : ''}`;
    if (!output.endsWith('\n')) output += '\n';
    const writable = destination.write(output);
    if (!writable) await once(destination, 'drain');
    this._written(Buffer.byteLength(output));
    this.emit('written', record);
  }
}

export default ConsoleTransport;

'use strict';

export class LoggerHealth {
  constructor(logger) {
    this.logger = logger;
  }

  snapshot() {
    return this.logger.health();
  }

  isHealthy() {
    return this.snapshot().status === 'healthy';
  }

  assertHealthy() {
    const snapshot = this.snapshot();
    if (snapshot.status !== 'healthy') {
      const error = new Error(`RawNode Logger is ${snapshot.status}.`);
      error.code = 'RAWNODE_LOGGER_UNHEALTHY';
      error.health = snapshot;
      throw error;
    }
    return snapshot;
  }
}

export default LoggerHealth;

FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/observability/LoggerMetrics.js

'use strict';

function metricName(value) {
  return String(value || 'rawnode_logger').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_:]/g, '_');
}

export class LoggerMetrics {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.enabled = options.enabled !== false;
    this.prefix = metricName(options.prefix || 'rawnode_logger');
    this.createdAt = Date.now();
    this.counters = Object.create(null);
    this.gauges = Object.create(null);
    this.histograms = Object.create(null);
  }

  increment(name, amount = 1) {
    if (!this.enabled) return 0;
    const key = metricName(name);
    this.counters[key] = (this.counters[key] || 0) + Number(amount || 0);
    return this.counters[key];
  }

  gauge(name, value) {
    if (!this.enabled) return value;
    this.gauges[metricName(name)] = Number(value) || 0;
    return value;
  }

  observe(name, value) {
    if (!this.enabled) return;
    const key = metricName(name);
    const number = Number(value) || 0;
    const current = this.histograms[key] || { count: 0, sum: 0, min: Infinity, max: -Infinity };
    current.count++;
    current.sum += number;
    current.min = Math.min(current.min, number);
    current.max = Math.max(current.max, number);
    this.histograms[key] = current;
  }

  snapshot() {
    const histograms = Object.create(null);
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(this.histograms)) {
      histograms[key] = {
        ...value,
        min: Number.isFinite(value.min) ? value.min : 0,
        max: Number.isFinite(value.max) ? value.max : 0,
        average: value.count ? value.sum / value.count : 0,
      };
    }
    return {
      enabled: this.enabled,
      uptimeMs: Date.now() - this.createdAt,
      counters: { ...this.counters },
      gauges: { ...this.gauges },
      histograms,
    };
  }

  toPrometheus(labels = {}) {
    const baseLabels = Object.entries(labels).map(([key, value]) => `${metricName(key)}=${JSON.stringify(String(value))}`).join(',');
    const labelText = baseLabels ? `{${baseLabels}}` : '';
    const lines = [];
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(this.counters)) lines.push(`${this.prefix}_${key}_total${labelText} ${value}`);
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(this.gauges)) lines.push(`${this.prefix}_${key}${labelText} ${value}`);
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(this.histograms)) {
      lines.push(`${this.prefix}_${key}_count${labelText} ${value.count}`);
      lines.push(`${this.prefix}_${key}_sum${labelText} ${value.sum}`);
      lines.push(`${this.prefix}_${key}_min${labelText} ${Number.isFinite(value.min) ? value.min : 0}`);
      lines.push(`${this.prefix}_${key}_max${labelText} ${Number.isFinite(value.max) ? value.max : 0}`);
    }
    return `${lines.join('\n')}\n`;
  }
}

export default LoggerMetrics;

'use strict';

import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';

function responseStatus(res) {
  return res?.statusCode ?? res?.raw?.statusCode ?? null;
}

export class HttpLoggingMiddleware {
  constructor(logger, options = {}) {
    if (!logger) throw new TypeError('HttpLoggingMiddleware requires a logger.');
    this.logger = logger;
    this.options = {
      requestLevel: options.requestLevel || 'info',
      responseLevel: options.responseLevel || 'info',
      errorLevel: options.errorLevel || 'error',
      requestIdHeader: String(options.requestIdHeader || 'x-request-id').toLowerCase(),
      logRequest: options.logRequest !== false,
      logResponse: options.logResponse !== false,
      includeHeaders: options.includeHeaders === true,
      requestIdFactory: options.requestIdFactory || randomUUID,
    };
  }

  async handle(req, res, next) {
    const started = process.hrtime.bigint();
    const headers = req?.headers || {};
    const requestId = req?.id || req?.requestId || headers[this.options.requestIdHeader] || this.options.requestIdFactory();
    req.requestId ||= requestId;
    try { res?.setHeader?.('X-Request-Id', requestId); } catch {}
    const context = {
      requestId,
      method: req?.method,
      path: (typeof req?.path === 'function' ? req.path() : req?.path) || req?.url,
      remoteAddress: req?.ip || req?.socket?.remoteAddress,
      protocol: req?.protocol || (req?.socket?.encrypted ? 'https' : 'http'),
      userAgent: headers['user-agent'],
      tenantId: req?.tenantId || req?.tenant?.id,
      userId: req?.user?.id || req?.user?._id || req?.user?.sub,
    };
    if (this.options.includeHeaders) context.headers = headers;
    const requestLogger = this.logger.child(context);

    return await this.logger.runWithContext(context, async () => {
      if (this.options.logRequest) requestLogger.log(this.options.requestLevel, 'HTTP request received');
      let finished = false;
      const finish = () => {
        if (finished) return;
        finished = true;
        const durationMs = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e6;
        if (this.options.logResponse) requestLogger.log(this.options.responseLevel, 'HTTP request completed', { statusCode: responseStatus(res), durationMs });
      };
      res?.once?.('finish', finish);
      res?.once?.('close', finish);
      try {
        const result = await next();
        if (!res?.once) finish();
        return result;
      } catch (error) {
        const durationMs = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e6;
        requestLogger.log(this.options.errorLevel, 'HTTP request failed', { statusCode: responseStatus(res), durationMs }, { error });
        throw error;
      }
    });
  }
}

export function createHttpLoggingMiddleware(logger, options = {}) {
  const middleware = new HttpLoggingMiddleware(logger, options);
  return middleware.handle.bind(middleware);
}

export default HttpLoggingMiddleware;

FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/integrations/LoggerServiceProvider.js

'use strict';

import defaultConfig from '../../../config/logger.js';
import LoggerConfiguration from '../config/LoggerConfiguration.js';
import ChannelManager from '../channels/ChannelManager.js';
import SwappableLogger from './SwappableLogger.js';
import ProcessErrorHandler from './ProcessErrorHandler.js';
import LoggerHealth from '../observability/LoggerHealth.js';

function safeResolve(app, key, fallback = undefined) {
  try { return app?.resolve?.(key) ?? fallback; } catch { return fallback; }
}

function configGet(config, key, fallback = undefined) {
  try {
    if (typeof config?.get === 'function') {
      const value = config.get(key);
      return value === undefined ? fallback : value;
    }
  } catch {}
  const parts = String(key).split('.');
  let current = config;
  for (const part of parts) current = current?.[part];
  return current === undefined ? fallback : current;
}

function bind(app, key, value) {
  if (typeof app?.instance === 'function') return app.instance(key, value);
  if (typeof app?.singleton === 'function') return app.singleton(key, () => value);
  if (typeof app?.bind === 'function') return app.bind(key, () => value);
  if (app?.container && typeof app.container.instance === 'function') return app.container.instance(key, value);
  throw new Error(`RawNode application cannot bind logger service [${key}].`);
}

function tryBind(app, key, value) {
  try { return bind(app, key, value); } catch { return null; }
}

/** RawNode service provider for configuration-driven logging channels. */
export class LoggerServiceProvider {
  static dependencies = ['app'];
  static priority = -1000;

  constructor(app, options = {}) {
    this.app = app;
    this.options = options;
    this.configuration = null;
    this.manager = null;
    this.logger = options.proxy || new SwappableLogger();
    this.processErrors = null;
  }

  async register() {
    const configService = safeResolve(this.app, 'config', {});
    const supplied = this.options.config
      || configGet(configService, 'logger', null)
      || configGet(configService, 'logging', null)
      || defaultConfig;

    this.configuration = supplied instanceof LoggerConfiguration
      ? supplied
      : new LoggerConfiguration(supplied, { defaults: defaultConfig, strict: this.options.strictConfig !== false });

    const snapshot = this.configuration.snapshot();
    this.manager = new ChannelManager({
      default: snapshot.channel || snapshot.default || 'stack',
      channels: snapshot.channels,
      drivers: this.options.drivers,
      loggerFactory: this.options.loggerFactory,
      base: {
        profile: snapshot.profile,
        level: snapshot.level,
        service: this.options.service
          || (!(supplied instanceof LoggerConfiguration) ? supplied?.service : null)
          || configGet(configService, 'app.name', null)
          || snapshot.service
          || process.env.APP_NAME
          || 'rawnode',
        environment: this.options.environment
          || (!(supplied instanceof LoggerConfiguration) ? supplied?.environment : null)
          || configGet(configService, 'app.env', null)
          || snapshot.environment
          || process.env.APP_ENV
          || process.env.NODE_ENV
          || 'development',
        security: {
          enabled: snapshot.security?.redact !== false,
          redact: snapshot.security?.redact !== false,
          sanitizeControls: snapshot.security?.sanitize !== false,
          ...(snapshot.security || {}),
        },
        queue: snapshot.queue,
        observability: snapshot.metrics || snapshot.observability,
      },
    });
    this.manager.setBindingResolver((key) => safeResolve(this.app, key, null));

    const target = this.manager.channel(this.manager.default());
    this.logger.swap(target);

    bind(this.app, 'logger', this.logger);
    tryBind(this.app, 'log', this.logger);
    tryBind(this.app, 'Logger', this.logger);
    tryBind(this.app, 'logger.manager', this.manager);
    tryBind(this.app, 'logger.channels', this.manager);
    tryBind(this.app, 'logger.configuration', this.configuration);
    tryBind(this.app, 'logger.health', new LoggerHealth(this.logger));
    tryBind(this.app, 'logger.channel', (name) => this.manager.channel(name));

    const processOptions = snapshot.processErrors || {};
    if (processOptions.enabled === true) {
      this.processErrors = new ProcessErrorHandler(this.logger, {
        uncaughtException: processOptions.uncaughtException !== false,
        unhandledRejection: processOptions.unhandledRejection !== false,
        warning: processOptions.warning === true,
        exitOnUncaught: processOptions.exitOnUncaught === true,
        flushTimeoutMs: processOptions.flushTimeoutMs,
      });
      tryBind(this.app, 'logger.processErrors', this.processErrors);
    }

    return this.logger;
  }

  async boot() {
    await this.logger.ready?.();
    this.processErrors?.attach();
  }

  async tearDown(options = {}) {
    this.processErrors?.detach();
    await this.manager?.close?.(options);
  }

  async shutdown(options = {}) { return await this.tearDown(options); }
}

export default LoggerServiceProvider;

FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/integrations/ProcessErrorHandler.js
================================================

'use strict';

export class ProcessErrorHandler {
  constructor(logger, options = {}) {
    if (!logger) throw new TypeError('ProcessErrorHandler requires a logger.');
    this.logger = logger;
    this.options = {
      uncaughtException: options.uncaughtException === true,
      unhandledRejection: options.unhandledRejection === true,
      warning: options.warning === true,
      exitOnUncaught: options.exitOnUncaught === true,
      flushTimeoutMs: Math.max(1, Number(options.flushTimeoutMs) || 5000),
    };
    this.handlers = new Map();
  }

  attach() {
    if (this.options.uncaughtException) this._attach('uncaughtException', async (error, origin) => {
      await this._report('critical', 'Uncaught exception', error, { origin });
      if (this.options.exitOnUncaught) process.exitCode = 1;
    });
    if (this.options.unhandledRejection) this._attach('unhandledRejection', async (reason, promise) => {
      const error = reason instanceof Error ? reason : new Error(String(reason));
      await this._report('error', 'Unhandled promise rejection', error, { promise: String(promise) });
    });
    if (this.options.warning) this._attach('warning', async (warning) => {
      await this._report('warn', 'Process warning', warning, { code: warning?.code });
    });
    return this;
  }

  _attach(event, handler) {
    if (this.handlers.has(event)) return;
    const wrapped = (...args) => { Promise.resolve(handler(...args)).catch(() => {}); };
    this.handlers.set(event, wrapped);
    process.on(event, wrapped);
  }

  async _report(level, message, error, data) {
    const work = this.logger.logAsync(level, message, data, { error }).then(() => this.logger.flush());
    await Promise.race([
      work,
      new Promise((resolve) => { const timer = setTimeout(resolve, this.options.flushTimeoutMs); }),
    ]);
  }

  detach() {
    for (const [event, handler] of this.handlers) process.off(event, handler);
    this.handlers.clear();
  }
}

export default ProcessErrorHandler;


================================================
FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/integrations/SwappableLogger.js
================================================

'use strict';

const LEVELS = ['emergency','alert','critical','error','urgent','priority','warn','notice','success','info','debug','metric','metrics','important'];

/** Stable bootstrap proxy whose target may be replaced without invalidating DI references. */
export class SwappableLogger {
  constructor(target = null) { this._target = target; }
  swap(target) {
    if (!target || typeof target.log !== 'function') throw new TypeError('SwappableLogger.swap requires a logger-like target.');
    const previous = this._target;
    this._target = target;
    return previous;
  }
  current() { return this._target; }
  log(...args) { return this._target?.log?.(...args); }
  logAsync(...args) { return this._target?.logAsync?.(...args) ?? Promise.resolve(null); }
  isLevelEnabled(level) { return this._target?.isLevelEnabled?.(level) ?? false; }
  child(...args) { return this._target?.child?.(...args) || this; }
  with(...args) { return this._target?.with?.(...args) || this; }
  withTags(...args) { return this._target?.withTags?.(...args) || this; }
  channel(...args) { return this._target?.channel?.(...args) || this; }
  at(...args) { return this._target?.at?.(...args) || this; }
  withError(...args) { return this._target?.withError?.(...args) || this; }
  when(...args) { return this._target?.when?.(...args) || this; }
  sample(...args) { return this._target?.sample?.(...args) || this; }
  once(...args) { return this._target?.once?.(...args) || this; }
  rateLimit(...args) { return this._target?.rateLimit?.(...args) || this; }
  forRequest(...args) { return this._target?.forRequest?.(...args) || this; }
  forJob(...args) { return this._target?.forJob?.(...args) || this; }
  runWithContext(...args) { return this._target?.runWithContext?.(...args); }
  time(...args) { return this._target?.time?.(...args) || Object.freeze({ end: () => null, stop: () => null, cancel() {} }); }
  measure(...args) { return this._target?.measure?.(...args); }
  setLevel(...args) { this._target?.setLevel?.(...args); return this; }
  registerLevel(...args) { this._target?.registerLevel?.(...args); return this; }
  addTransport(...args) { this._target?.addTransport?.(...args); return this; }
  transport(...args) { this._target?.transport?.(...args); return this; }
  processor(...args) { this._target?.processor?.(...args); return this; }
  use(...args) { this._target?.use?.(...args); return this; }
  plugin(...args) { this._target?.plugin?.(...args); return this; }
  ready(...args) { return Promise.resolve(this._target?.ready?.(...args)).then(() => this); }
  flush(...args) { return this._target?.flush?.(...args) ?? Promise.resolve(); }
  reopen(...args) { return this._target?.reopen?.(...args) ?? Promise.resolve(false); }
  close(...args) { return this._target?.close?.(...args) ?? Promise.resolve(); }
  health() { return this._target?.health?.() || { status: 'bootstrap', state: 'initializing', transports: [] }; }
  stats() { return this._target?.stats?.() || { counters: {}, gauges: {}, histograms: {}, transports: [] }; }
  levels() { return this._target?.levels?.() || []; }
  diagnosticsSnapshot() { return this._target?.diagnosticsSnapshot?.() || []; }
  metricsSnapshot() { return this._target?.metricsSnapshot?.() || {}; }
  prometheus(...args) { return this._target?.prometheus?.(...args) || ''; }
  toPrometheus(...args) { return this._target?.toPrometheus?.(...args) || ''; }
}
for (const level of LEVELS) SwappableLogger.prototype[level] = function levelMethod(...args) { return this._target?.[level]?.(...args); };
export default SwappableLogger;

'use strict';

export class EventLoggingBridge {
  constructor(events, logger, options = {}) {
    this.events = events;
    this.logger = logger;
    this.options = options;
    this.listeners = [];
  }

  listen(event, level = 'info', mapper = null) {
    const handler = (...args) => {
      const mapped = typeof mapper === 'function' ? mapper(...args) : { args };
      this.logger.log(level, `Event dispatched: ${event}`, mapped, { channel: this.options.channel || 'events' });
    };
    this.events.on(event, handler);
    this.listeners.push({ event, handler });
    return this;
  }

  close() {
    for (const { event, handler } of this.listeners) this.events.off?.(event, handler);
    this.listeners.length = 0;
  }
}

export default EventLoggingBridge;

'use strict';

import MemoryTransport from '../transports/MemoryTransport.js';
import { createLogger } from '../core/LoggerRuntime.js';
import SafeSerializer from '../serialization/SafeSerializer.js';

export class LoggerRecorder {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.serializer = options.serializer || new SafeSerializer({ deterministic: true });
    this.transport = options.transport || new MemoryTransport({ serializer: this.serializer, maxItems: options.maxItems || 10_000 });
    this.logger = options.logger || createLogger({
      profile: 'test',
      level: options.level || 'important',
      serializer: this.serializer,
      transports: [this.transport],
      clock: options.clock,
      idFactory: options.idFactory,
    });
  }

  records() { return this.transport.all(); }
  latest(count = 1) { return this.transport.latest(count); }
  clear() { return this.transport.clear(); }
  async flush() { await this.logger.flush(); return this.records(); }
  async close() { await this.logger.close(); }

  assertCount(expected) {
    const actual = this.records().length;
    if (actual !== expected) throw new Error(`Expected ${expected} log records, received ${actual}.`);
    return this;
  }

  assertLogged(level, matcher = null) {
    const records = this.transport.atLevel(level);
    const found = matcher == null ? records.length > 0 : records.some((record) => {
      if (matcher instanceof RegExp) return matcher.test(record.message);
      if (typeof matcher === 'function') return matcher(record);
      return record.message.includes(String(matcher));
    });
    if (!found) throw new Error(`Expected a [${level}] log record matching ${String(matcher)}.`);
    return this;
  }

  assertNotLogged(level, matcher = null) {
    try { this.assertLogged(level, matcher); }
    catch { return this; }
    throw new Error(`Did not expect a [${level}] log record matching ${String(matcher)}.`);
  }
}

export function createLoggerHarness(options = {}) {
  return new LoggerRecorder(options);
}

export default LoggerRecorder;

  tick(ms = 1) { this.now += Number(ms) || 0; return this.now; }
  set(value) { this.now = Number(value); return this.now; }

}

FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/public/LoggerFacade.js
