'use strict';

const LEVEL_METHODS = Object.freeze([
  'emergency', 'alert', 'critical', 'error', 'urgent', 'priority', 'warn',
  'notice', 'success', 'info', 'debug', 'metric', 'metrics', 'important',
]);

function clampRate(value) {
  const number = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return 1;
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, number));
}

function normalizeContext(value) {
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') return Object.freeze({});
  return Object.freeze({ ...value });
}

function messageKey(message) {
  if (typeof message === 'function') return '[lazy]';
  if (message instanceof Error) return message.message;
  if (message && typeof message === 'object') return message.message || '[object]';
  return String(message ?? '');
}

/**
 * Immutable fluent view over a logger runtime.
 *
 * Child loggers do not allocate transports, queues, or context stores. They only
 * carry immutable policy/context and delegate to the parent runtime.
 */
export class LoggerFacade {
  constructor(logger, options = {}) {
    if (!logger || typeof logger.log !== 'function') {
      throw new TypeError('LoggerFacade requires a logger exposing log().');
    }

    this.logger = logger;
    this.context = normalizeContext(options.context);
    this.channelName = options.channel || this.context.channel || null;
    this.fixedLevel = options.level || null;
    this.sampleRate = clampRate(options.sampleRate ?? 1);
    this.random = options.random || Math.random;
    this.disabled = options.disabled === true;
    this.boundError = options.boundError || null;
    this.tags = Object.freeze([...(options.tags || [])]);
    this.onceKey = options.onceKey == null ? null : String(options.onceKey);
    this.ratePolicy = options.ratePolicy || null;
    this.sharedState = options.sharedState || {
      once: new Set(),
      rates: new Map(),
      maxOnceKeys: Math.max(1, Number(options.stateLimits?.maxOnceKeys) || 10_000),
      maxRateBuckets: Math.max(1, Number(options.stateLimits?.maxRateBuckets) || 10_000),
    };
  }

  _isHighSeverity(level) {
    return this.logger.levelRegistry?.isHighSeverity?.(level) === true
      || this.logger.runtime?.levelRegistry?.isHighSeverity?.(level) === true;
  }

  _accept(level, message) {
    if (this.disabled) return false;
    const highSeverity = this._isHighSeverity(level);

    if (!highSeverity && this.sampleRate < 1 && this.random() >= this.sampleRate) {
      this.logger.metricsRegistry?.increment?.('records_sampled_out_total');
      this.logger.runtime?.metricsRegistry?.increment?.('records_sampled_out_total');
      return false;
    }

    if (this.onceKey) {
      const key = String(this.onceKey);
      if (this.sharedState.once.has(key)) return false;
      while (this.sharedState.once.size >= this.sharedState.maxOnceKeys) {
        const oldest = this.sharedState.once.values().next().value;
        if (oldest === undefined) break;
        this.sharedState.once.delete(oldest);
      }
      this.sharedState.once.add(key);
    }

    if (!highSeverity && this.ratePolicy) {
      const now = Date.now();
      const key = `${this.ratePolicy.key}:${level}`;
      let bucket = this.sharedState.rates.get(key);
      if (!bucket || now - bucket.startedAt >= this.ratePolicy.intervalMs) {
        bucket = { startedAt: now, count: 0 };
      }
      if (bucket.count >= this.ratePolicy.limit) return false;
      bucket.count++;
      if (!this.sharedState.rates.has(key) && this.sharedState.rates.size >= this.sharedState.maxRateBuckets) {
        for (const [candidateKey, candidate] of this.sharedState.rates) {
          if (now - candidate.startedAt >= this.ratePolicy.intervalMs) this.sharedState.rates.delete(candidateKey);
        }
        while (this.sharedState.rates.size >= this.sharedState.maxRateBuckets) {
          const oldest = this.sharedState.rates.keys().next().value;
          if (oldest === undefined) break;
          this.sharedState.rates.delete(oldest);
        }
      }
      this.sharedState.rates.set(key, bucket);
    }

    return true;
  }

  _options(options = {}) {
    return {
      ...options,
      channel: options.channel || this.channelName || undefined,
      context: { ...this.context, ...(options.context || {}) },
      tags: [...this.tags, ...(options.tags || [])],
      error: options.error || this.boundError || undefined,
    };
  }

  log(level, message, data = null, options = {}) {
    const actualLevel = this.fixedLevel || level;
    if (!this._accept(actualLevel, message)) return false;
    return this.logger.log(actualLevel, message, data, this._options(options));
  }

  async logAsync(level, message, data = null, options = {}) {
    const actualLevel = this.fixedLevel || level;
    if (!this._accept(actualLevel, message)) return null;
    return await this.logger.logAsync(actualLevel, message, data, this._options(options));
  }

  child(context = {}, options = {}) {
    return new LoggerFacade(this.logger, {
      context: { ...this.context, ...(context || {}) },
      channel: options.channel || this.channelName,
      level: options.level || this.fixedLevel,
      sampleRate: options.sampleRate ?? this.sampleRate,
      random: options.random || this.random,
      disabled: options.disabled ?? this.disabled,
      boundError: options.boundError || this.boundError,
      tags: [...this.tags, ...(options.tags || [])],
      onceKey: options.onceKey ?? this.onceKey,
      ratePolicy: options.ratePolicy || this.ratePolicy,
      sharedState: this.sharedState,
    });
  }

  with(context = {}) { return this.child(context); }
  withTags(...tags) { return this.child({}, { tags: tags.flat().filter((value) => value != null).map(String) }); }
  channel(name) { return this.child({}, { channel: String(name || 'application') }); }
  at(level) { return this.child({}, { level }); }
  sample(rate) { return this.child({}, { sampleRate: rate }); }
  withError(error, context = {}) { return this.child(context, { boundError: error }); }
  when(condition) { return condition ? this : this.child({}, { disabled: true }); }
  once(key = 'default') { return this.child({}, { onceKey: String(key) }); }

  rateLimit(key = 'default', options = {}) {
    return this.child({}, {
      ratePolicy: Object.freeze({
        key: String(key),
        limit: Math.max(1, Number(options.limit) || 1),
        intervalMs: Math.max(1, Number(options.intervalMs ?? options.windowMs) || 1_000),
      }),
    });
  }

  forRequest(req, extra = {}) {
    const manager = this.logger.contextManager || this.logger.runtime?.contextManager;
    const context = manager?.createRequestContext?.(req, extra) || { ...extra };
    return this.child(context);
  }

  forJob(job = {}, extra = {}) {
    const manager = this.logger.contextManager || this.logger.runtime?.contextManager;
    const context = manager?.createJobContext?.(job, extra) || {
      jobId: job.id || job.jobId,
      queue: job.queue || job.queueName,
      attempt: job.attempt || job.attempts,
      tenantId: job.tenantId || job.tenant_id,
      traceId: job.traceId || job.trace_id,
      correlationId: job.correlationId || job.correlation_id,
      ...extra,
    };
    return this.child(context);
  }

  runWithContext(context, callback, ...args) {
    const manager = this.logger.contextManager || this.logger.runtime?.contextManager;
    return manager?.run ? manager.run({ ...this.context, ...(context || {}) }, callback, ...args) : callback(...args);
  }

  getContext() {
    const manager = this.logger.contextManager || this.logger.runtime?.contextManager;
    return manager?.current?.() || manager?.get?.() || {};
  }

  isLevelEnabled(level) {
    return !this.disabled && this.logger.isLevelEnabled(this.fixedLevel || level);
  }

  time(label, context = {}) {
    const started = process.hrtime.bigint();
    let ended = false;
    return Object.freeze({
      end: (first = {}, second = 'metric') => {
        if (ended) return null;
        ended = true;
        const durationMs = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e6;
        const level = typeof first === 'string' ? first : second;
        const data = typeof first === 'string' ? (second && typeof second === 'object' ? second : {}) : (first || {});
        this.log(level || 'metric', label, { ...context, ...data, durationMs });
        return durationMs;
      },
      stop: (first = {}, second = 'metric') => {
        if (ended) return null;
        ended = true;
        const durationMs = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e6;
        const level = typeof first === 'string' ? first : second;
        const data = typeof first === 'string' ? (second && typeof second === 'object' ? second : {}) : (first || {});
        this.log(level || 'metric', label, { ...context, ...data, durationMs });
        return durationMs;
      },
      cancel: () => { ended = true; },
    });
  }

  async measure(label, operation, context = {}, options = {}) {
    if (typeof operation !== 'function') throw new TypeError('logger.measure requires an operation function.');
    const started = process.hrtime.bigint();
    try {
      const result = await operation();
      const durationMs = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e6;
      this.log(options.level || 'metric', label, { ...context, ...(options.data || {}), durationMs, success: true });
      return result;
    } catch (error) {
      const durationMs = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e6;
      this.log(options.errorLevel || 'error', label, { ...context, ...(options.data || {}), durationMs, success: false }, { error });
      throw error;
    }
  }

  setLevel(level) { this.logger.setLevel?.(level); return this; }
  registerLevel(definition, options) { this.logger.registerLevel?.(definition, options); return this; }
  levels() { return this.logger.levels?.() || this.logger.runtime?.levels?.() || []; }
  transport(transport, options) { this.logger.addTransport?.(transport, options); return this; }
  processor(processor, options) { this.logger.processor?.(processor, options); return this; }
  plugin(plugin) { this.logger.plugin?.(plugin); return this; }
  ready() { return typeof this.logger.ready === 'function' ? this.logger.ready().then(() => this) : Promise.resolve(this); }
  reopen(options) { return this.logger.reopen?.(options); }
  flush(options) { return this.logger.flush(options); }
  close(options) { return this.logger.close(options); }
  health() { return this.logger.health(); }
  stats() { return this.logger.stats(); }
  metricsSnapshot() { return this.logger.metricsSnapshot?.() || this.logger.runtime?.metricsSnapshot?.() || {}; }
  prometheus(labels) { return this.toPrometheus(labels); }
  toPrometheus(labels) { return this.logger.toPrometheus?.(labels) || this.logger.prometheus?.(labels) || ''; }
}

for (const level of LEVEL_METHODS) {
  LoggerFacade.prototype[level] = function levelMethod(message, data, options) {
    return this.log(level, message, data, options);
  };
}

export { LEVEL_METHODS };
export default LoggerFacade;

'use strict';

/** A complete no-op logger that is API-compatible with LoggerRuntime. */
export class NullLogger {
  log() { return false; }
  logAsync() { return Promise.resolve(null); }
  submit() { return { accepted: false, promise: Promise.resolve(null) }; }
  isLevelEnabled() { return false; }
  setLevel() { return this; }
  registerLevel() { return this; }
  addTransport() { return this; }
  transport() { return this; }
  removeTransport() { return null; }
  use() { return this; }
  processor() { return this; }
  plugin() { return this; }
  child() { return this; }
  with() { return this; }
  withTags() { return this; }
  channel() { return this; }
  at() { return this; }
  sample() { return this; }
  withError() { return this; }
  when() { return this; }
  once() { return this; }
  rateLimit() { return this; }
  clearOnce() { return this; }
  resetRateLimits() { return this; }
  forRequest() { return this; }
  forJob() { return this; }
  runWithContext(_context, callback, ...args) { return callback(...args); }
  getContext() { return Object.freeze({}); }
  time() { return Object.freeze({ end: () => null, stop: () => null, cancel: () => {} }); }

}

'use strict';

import AdvancedLogger from '../../submodules/advanced-logger/index.js';
import Config from '../../submodules/config/index.js';

/** Fluent, dependency-free logger builder. Every method is additive. */
export class LoggerBuilder {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.options = {
      level: 'info',
      transports: [],
      serialize: false,
      colors: false,
      mode: 'object',
      profile: 'production',
      context: {},
      tags: [],
      ...options,
    };
    this._transports = [];
    this._processors = [];
    this._plugins = [];

    for (const transport of options.transports || []) this.transport(transport);
  }

  level(name = 'info') { this.options.level = name; return this; }
  profile(name = 'v1') { this.options.profile = name; return this; }
  production() { return this.profile('production'); }
  strict() { return this.profile('strict'); }
  testing() { return this.profile('test'); }
  serverless() { return this.profile('serverless'); }
  serialize(enabled = true) { this.options.serialize = enabled; return this; }
  mode(value = 'object') { this.options.mode = value; return this; }
  useColors(enabled = true) { this.options.colors = enabled; return this; }
  colors(enabled = true) { return this.useColors(enabled); }
  withProfile(name) { Config.setProfile(name); this.options.colorProfile = name; return this; }
  service(name) { this.options.service = name; return this; }
  environment(name) { this.options.environment = name; return this; }
  channel(name) { this.options.channel = name; return this; }
  context(values = {}) { this.options.context = { ...(this.options.context || {}), ...values }; return this; }
  tag(...tags) { this.options.tags = [...(this.options.tags || []), ...tags.flat().map(String)]; return this; }
  queue(options = {}) { this.options.queue = { ...(this.options.queue || {}), ...options }; return this; }
  pipelineQueue(options = {}) { this.options.pipelineQueue = { ...(this.options.pipelineQueue || {}), ...options }; return this; }
  transportQueue(options = {}) { this.options.transportQueue = { ...(this.options.transportQueue || {}), ...options }; return this; }
  circuitBreaker(options = {}) { this.options.circuitBreaker = { ...(this.options.circuitBreaker || {}), ...options }; return this; }
  security(options = {}) { this.options.security = { ...(this.options.security || {}), ...options }; return this; }
  redaction(options = true) {
    if (options === false) this.options.security = { ...(this.options.security || {}), redact: false, enabled: false };
    else this.options.security = { ...(this.options.security || {}), redact: true, enabled: true, ...(options === true ? {} : options) };
    return this;
  }
  sanitization(options = true) {
    if (options === false) this.options.security = { ...(this.options.security || {}), sanitizeControls: false };
    else this.options.security = { ...(this.options.security || {}), sanitizeControls: true, ...(options === true ? {} : options) };
    return this;
  }
  metrics(options = true) {
    this.options.observability = options === true
      ? { ...(this.options.observability || {}), enabled: true }
      : options === false
        ? { ...(this.options.observability || {}), enabled: false }
        : { ...(this.options.observability || {}), ...(options || {}) };
    return this;
  }
  formatter(formatter) { this.options.formatter = formatter; return this; }
  awaitDelivery(enabled = true) { this.options.awaitTransportDelivery = enabled; return this; }

  transport(transport, options = {}) {
    if (!transport) throw new TypeError('LoggerBuilder.transport requires a transport.');
    this._transports.push({ transport, options: { ...(options || {}) } });
    return this;
  }

  transports(...transports) {
    for (const transport of transports.flat()) this.transport(transport);
    return this;
  }

  processor(processor, options = {}) {
    if (typeof processor !== 'function') throw new TypeError('LoggerBuilder.processor requires a function.');
    this._processors.push({ processor, ...options });
    return this;
  }

  use(processor, options = {}) { return this.processor(processor, options); }

  plugin(plugin) {
    if (!plugin || typeof plugin !== 'object') throw new TypeError('LoggerBuilder.plugin requires an object.');
    this._plugins.push(plugin);
    return this;
  }

  done() { return this.build(); }

  build() {
    const logger = new AdvancedLogger({
      ...this.options,
      transports: [],
      processorList: this._processors,
      pluginList: this._plugins,
    });
    for (const { transport, options } of this._transports) logger.addTransport(transport, options);
    return logger;
  }
}

export default LoggerBuilder;

export { default as LoggerBuilder } from './LoggerBuilder.js';

FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/console/run.js

'use strict';

import { open, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { monitorEventLoopDelay, performance } from 'node:perf_hooks';
import createLogger from '../public/createLogger.js';
import MemoryTransport from '../transports/MemoryTransport.js';
import FileTransport from '../transports/FileTransport.js';
import AuditFileTransport from '../transports/AuditFileTransport.js';
import LevelRegistry from '../core/LevelRegistry.js';
import ChannelManager from '../channels/ChannelManager.js';
import LoggerConfiguration from '../config/LoggerConfiguration.js';
import defaultConfig from '../../../config/logger.js';
import runDoctor from './LoggerDoctor.js';

function parse(args) {
  const positionals = [];
  const options = Object.create(null);
  for (let index = 0; index < args.length; index++) {
    const value = args[index];
    if (!value.startsWith('--')) { positionals.push(value); continue; }
    const equals = value.indexOf('=');
    if (equals > 2) { options[value.slice(2, equals)] = value.slice(equals + 1); continue; }
    const key = value.slice(2);
    const next = args[index + 1];
    if (next != null && !next.startsWith('--')) { options[key] = next; index++; }
    else options[key] = true;
  }
  return { command: positionals.shift() || 'help', positionals, options };
}

function truthy(value) {
  return value === true || ['1', 'true', 'yes', 'on'].includes(String(value || '').toLowerCase());
}

function mask(value, key = '') {
  if (/(?:secret|token|password|passwd|private[_-]?key|api[_-]?key|authorization|cookie|pepper)/i.test(key) && value != null) return '[REDACTED]';
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((item) => mask(item));
  if (value && typeof value === 'object') return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value).map(([child, item]) => [child, mask(item, child)]));
  return value;
}

function output(value, json = false) {
  if (json || typeof value === 'object') process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)}\n`);
  else process.stdout.write(`${String(value)}\n`);
}

async function benchmark(options) {
  const iterations = Math.max(1, Number(options.iterations || 25_000));
  const transport = new MemoryTransport({ maxItems: Math.min(iterations, 50_000) });
  const logger = createLogger({
    profile: 'production',
    level: 'debug',
    transports: [transport],
    security: { enabled: truthy(options.redaction) },
    pipelineQueue: {
      maxItems: iterations + 64,
      maxBytes: Math.max(64 * 1024 * 1024, iterations * 2048),
      overflow: 'throw',
    },
  });
  const delay = monitorEventLoopDelay({ resolution: 10 });
  delay.enable();
  const cpuStart = process.cpuUsage();
  const startedAt = performance.now();
  for (let index = 0; index < iterations; index++) logger.debug('benchmark', { index, active: true });
  const enqueueMs = performance.now() - startedAt;
  await logger.flush({ throwOnError: true });
  const totalMs = performance.now() - startedAt;
  const cpu = process.cpuUsage(cpuStart);
  delay.disable();
  const result = {
    scenario: 'structured-memory-transport',
    iterations,
    enqueueMs,
    totalMs,
    enqueuePerSecond: iterations / Math.max(enqueueMs, 0.001) * 1000,
    deliveredPerSecond: iterations / Math.max(totalMs, 0.001) * 1000,
    cpuMs: (cpu.user + cpu.system) / 1000,
    eventLoopDelayMs: {
      mean: Number.isFinite(delay.mean) ? delay.mean / 1e6 : 0,
      max: Number.isFinite(delay.max) ? delay.max / 1e6 : 0,
      p99: Number.isFinite(delay.percentile(99)) ? delay.percentile(99) / 1e6 : 0,
    },
    stored: transport.all().length,
    metrics: logger.metricsSnapshot(),
  };
  await logger.close();
  return result;
}

async function tail(file, count = 50, maxBytes = 4 * 1024 * 1024) {
  const target = path.resolve(file);
  const handle = await open(target, 'r');
  try {
    const stats = await handle.stat();
    const length = Math.min(stats.size, Math.max(1024, Number(maxBytes) || maxBytes));
    const buffer = Buffer.alloc(length);
    await handle.read(buffer, 0, length, stats.size - length);
    return buffer.toString('utf8').trimEnd().split('\n').slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count) || 50)).join('\n');
  } finally { await handle.close(); }
}

function normalizeFileDefinition(definition = {}) {
  return {
    ...definition,
    logDirectory: definition.logDirectory || definition.directory,
    fileName: definition.fileName || definition.filename,
    maxSize: definition.maxSize ?? definition.maxBytes,
    rotateInterval: definition.rotateInterval ?? definition.rotateIntervalMs,
    compressOnRotate: definition.compressOnRotate ?? definition.compress,
    retention: definition.retention ? {
      ...definition.retention,
      maxTotalBytes: definition.retention.maxTotalBytes ?? definition.retention.maxBytes,
    } : undefined,
  };
}

async function managerSnapshot(configuration = defaultConfig) {
  const compiled = new LoggerConfiguration(configuration, { defaults: false });
  const snapshot = compiled.snapshot();
  const manager = new ChannelManager({
    default: snapshot.channel || snapshot.default,
    base: {
      profile: snapshot.profile,
      level: snapshot.level,
      service: snapshot.service,
      environment: snapshot.environment,
      security: snapshot.security,
      queue: snapshot.queue,
    },
    channels: snapshot.channels,
  });
  try {
    const logger = manager.channel();
    await logger.ready?.();
    return { configuration: mask(snapshot), manager: manager.snapshot(), health: manager.health() };
  } finally {
    await manager.close();
  }
}

function activeFileDefinition(configuration, requested) {
  const channels = configuration.channels || {};
  if (requested && channels[requested]) return { name: requested, definition: channels[requested] };
  for (const [name, definition] of Object.entries(channels)) {
    if (['file', 'daily', 'audit'].includes(String(definition?.driver))) return { name, definition };
  }
  return null;
}

function help() {
  return `RawNode Logger CLI

Commands:
  doctor [--json]
  health [--json]
  channels [--json]
  transports [--json]
  levels [--json]
  config [--json] [--reveal]
  bench [--iterations 25000] [--redaction] [--json]
  verify-audit <file> [--secret value] [--json]
  rotate [channel] [--json]
  tail <file> [--lines 50] [--max-bytes 4194304]
  version [--json]
`;
}

export async function runCli(args = process.argv.slice(2)) {
  const { command: rawCommand, positionals, options } = parse(args);
  const command = rawCommand.replace(/^logger:/, '');
  const json = truthy(options.json);
  try {
    if (['help', '--help', '-h'].includes(command)) output(help());
    else if (command === 'doctor') {
      const result = await runDoctor({ configuration: defaultConfig });
      output(result, true);
      return result.ok ? 0 : 2;
    } else if (command === 'health') {
      output(await managerSnapshot(defaultConfig), true);
    } else if (command === 'channels') {
      const compiled = new LoggerConfiguration(defaultConfig, { defaults: false }).snapshot();
      output({ default: compiled.channel || compiled.default, channels: mask(compiled.channels || {}) }, true);
    } else if (command === 'transports') {
      const compiled = new LoggerConfiguration(defaultConfig, { defaults: false }).snapshot();
      output(mask(Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(compiled.channels || {}).filter(([, definition]) => definition?.driver !== 'stack'))), true);
    } else if (command === 'levels') {
      output(new LevelRegistry().definitions(), true);
    } else if (command === 'config') {
      const compiled = new LoggerConfiguration(defaultConfig, { defaults: false });
      output(truthy(options.reveal) ? compiled.snapshot() : mask(compiled.snapshot()), true);
    } else if (command === 'bench') {
      output(await benchmark(options), true);
    } else if (command === 'verify-audit') {
      if (!positionals[0]) throw new Error('verify-audit requires a file path.');
      const result = await AuditFileTransport.verifyFile(path.resolve(positionals[0]), {
        secret: options.secret || process.env.LOG_AUDIT_SECRET || process.env.APP_KEY,
      });
      output(result, true);
      return result.valid ? 0 : 3;
    } else if (command === 'rotate') {
      const entry = activeFileDefinition(defaultConfig, positionals[0]);
      if (!entry) throw new Error('No file, daily, or audit channel is configured.');
      const Definition = entry.definition.driver === 'audit' ? AuditFileTransport : FileTransport;
      const transport = new Definition(normalizeFileDefinition(entry.definition));
      try { output({ channel: entry.name, ...(await transport.rotate({ reason: 'cli', force: true })) }, true); }
      finally { await transport.close(); }
    } else if (command === 'tail') {
      if (!positionals[0]) throw new Error('tail requires a file path.');
      output(await tail(positionals[0], options.lines || 50, options['max-bytes']));
    } else if (command === 'version') {
      const packageJson = JSON.parse(await readFile(new URL('../../../package.json', import.meta.url), 'utf8'));
      output(json ? { name: packageJson.name, version: packageJson.version } : packageJson.version, json);
    } else {
      output(help());
      return 1;
    }
    return 0;
  } catch (error) {
    if (json) output({ ok: false, error: error?.message || String(error), code: error?.code }, true);
    else process.stderr.write(`[rawnode-logger] ${error?.stack || error}\n`);
    return 1;
  }
}

export { benchmark as runLoggerBenchmark, tail as tailLogFile };
export default runCli;

'use strict';

export { runDoctor } from './LoggerDoctor.js';
export { runCli, runLoggerBenchmark, tailLogFile } from './run.js';


================================================
FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/console/LoggerDoctor.js

'use strict';

import { access, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { constants } from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import LoggerConfiguration from '../config/LoggerConfiguration.js';
import defaultConfig from '../../../config/logger.js';

const packageRoot = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '../../..');

async function nearestExisting(directory) {
  let current = path.resolve(directory);
  while (true) {
    try { await access(current, constants.F_OK); return current; }
    catch {
      const parent = path.dirname(current);
      if (parent === current) return current;
      current = parent;
    }
  }
}

function activeChannelNames(configuration) {
  const selected = configuration.channel || configuration.default;
  const channels = configuration.channels || {};
  const visited = new Set();
  const output = new Set();
  const visit = (name) => {
    if (!name || visited.has(name)) return;
    visited.add(name);
    const definition = channels[name];
    if (!definition || definition.enabled === false) return;
    if (definition.driver === 'stack') {
      for (const member of definition.channels || []) visit(String(member));
    } else output.add(name);
  };
  visit(selected);
  return [...output];
}

/** Run safe, side-effect-free production readiness checks. */
export async function runDoctor(options = {}) {
  const checks = [];
  const warnings = [];
  const add = (name, ok, details = {}) => checks.push({ name, ok: Boolean(ok), ...details });
  const warn = (name, details = {}) => warnings.push({ name, ...details });

  const nodeMajor = Number(process.versions.node.split('.')[0]);
  add('node-version', nodeMajor >= 20, { actual: process.versions.node, expected: '>=20.0.0' });

  let pkg = {};
  try {
    pkg = JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(packageRoot, 'package.json'), 'utf8'));
    add('package-json', true, { packageName: pkg.name, version: pkg.version });
  } catch (error) {
    add('package-json', false, { error: error.message });
  }

  const dependencyFields = ['dependencies', 'optionalDependencies', 'peerDependencies'];
  const dependencyViolations = dependencyFields.filter((field) => pkg[field] && Object.keys(pkg[field]).length > 0);
  add('zero-runtime-dependencies', dependencyViolations.length === 0, { violations: dependencyViolations });

  const missingExports = [];
  for (const [name, definition] of Object.entries(pkg.exports || {})) {
    if (name === './*' || name === './package.json') continue;
    const target = typeof definition === 'string' ? definition : definition?.import;
    if (!target || !target.startsWith('.')) continue;
    try { await access(path.resolve(packageRoot, target), constants.R_OK); }
    catch { missingExports.push({ name, target }); }
  }
  add('package-exports', missingExports.length === 0, { missing: missingExports });

  let configuration = options.configuration || options.config || defaultConfig;
  let configDiagnostics = [];
  try {
    const compiled = configuration instanceof LoggerConfiguration
      ? configuration
      : new LoggerConfiguration(configuration, { defaults: defaultConfig, strict: false });
    configuration = compiled.snapshot();
    configDiagnostics = compiled.diagnostics;
    add('configuration', !configDiagnostics.some((item) => item.severity === 'error'), { diagnostics: configDiagnostics });
  } catch (error) {
    add('configuration', false, { error: error.message, diagnostics: error.diagnostics || [] });
  }

  const queue = configuration.queue || {};
  add('bounded-pipeline-queue', Number(queue.maxItems) > 0 && Number(queue.maxBytes) > 0, {
    maxItems: queue.maxItems,
    maxBytes: queue.maxBytes,
    overflow: queue.overflow,
  });

  const channels = configuration.channels || {};
  const active = activeChannelNames(configuration);
  for (const name of active) {
    const definition = channels[name] || {};
    const driver = String(definition.driver || 'console');
    if (driver === 'file' || driver === 'daily' || driver === 'audit') {
      const directory = path.resolve(definition.logDirectory || definition.directory || 'storage/logs');
      const ancestor = await nearestExisting(directory);
      try {
        await access(ancestor, constants.W_OK);
        add(`channel:${name}:writable`, true, { driver, directory, existingAncestor: ancestor });
      } catch (error) {
        add(`channel:${name}:writable`, false, { driver, directory, existingAncestor: ancestor, error: error.message });
      }
    }
    if (driver === 'audit') {
      const hasKey = Boolean(definition.key || definition.secret || definition.keyResolver || process.env.LOG_AUDIT_SECRET || process.env.APP_KEY);
      add(`channel:${name}:audit-key`, hasKey, { keyId: definition.keyId || 'primary' });
    }
    if ((driver === 'tcp' || driver === 'tls' || driver === 'udp') && !definition.port) {
      add(`channel:${name}:network-port`, false, { driver, error: 'port is required' });
    }
    if ((driver === 'http' || driver === 'http-batch') && !(definition.url || definition.endpoint)) {
      add(`channel:${name}:endpoint`, false, { driver, error: 'url or endpoint is required' });
    }
  }

  const inspector = configuration.inspector || {};
  if (inspector.enabled === true) {
    const loopback = ['127.0.0.1', '::1', 'localhost'].includes(String(inspector.host || '127.0.0.1'));
    add('inspector-access-control', loopback || Boolean(inspector.token), { host: inspector.host || '127.0.0.1', tokenConfigured: Boolean(inspector.token) });
    if (inspector.origins === '*') warn('inspector-origin-wildcard', { message: 'A wildcard inspector origin is not recommended in production.' });
  }

  for (const diagnostic of configDiagnostics.filter((item) => item.severity === 'warning')) warn(diagnostic.code, diagnostic);
  const failures = checks.filter((check) => !check.ok);
  return {
    ok: failures.length === 0,
    package: { name: pkg.name, version: pkg.version },
    activeChannels: active,
    checks,
    failures: failures.length,
    warnings,
  };
}

export default runDoctor;

FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/channels/Channel.js
================================================

'use strict';

const LEVELS = ['emergency','alert','critical','error','urgent','priority','warn','notice','success','info','debug','metric','metrics','important'];

export class Channel {
  constructor(name, logger) {
    if (!logger || typeof logger.log !== 'function') throw new TypeError('Channel requires a logger-like instance.');
    this.name = String(name);
    this.logger = logger.channel ? logger.channel(this.name) : logger;
  }
  log(level, message, data, options) { return this.logger.log(level, message, data, options); }
  logAsync(level, message, data, options) { return this.logger.logAsync(level, message, data, options); }
  isLevelEnabled(level) { return this.logger.isLevelEnabled?.(level) ?? true; }
  child(context, options) { return this.logger.child?.(context, options) || this; }
  with(context) { return this.logger.with?.(context) || this; }
  withTags(...tags) { return this.logger.withTags?.(...tags) || this; }
  withError(error, context) { return this.logger.withError?.(error, context) || this; }
  at(level) { return this.logger.at?.(level) || this; }
  forRequest(req, extra) { return this.logger.forRequest?.(req, extra) || this; }
  forJob(job, extra) { return this.logger.forJob?.(job, extra) || this; }
  ready(options) { return this.logger.ready?.(options) ?? Promise.resolve(this); }
  flush(options) { return this.logger.flush?.(options) ?? Promise.resolve(); }
  reopen(options) { return this.logger.reopen?.(options) ?? Promise.resolve(false); }
  close(options) { return this.logger.close?.(options) ?? Promise.resolve(); }
  health() { return this.logger.health?.() || { status: 'unknown' }; }
  stats() { return this.logger.stats?.() || {}; }
  prometheus(labels) { return this.logger.prometheus?.(labels) || ''; }
}
for (const level of LEVELS) Channel.prototype[level] = function levelMethod(message, data, options) { return this.log(level, message, data, options); };
export default Channel;


================================================
FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/channels/ChannelManager.js
================================================

'use strict';

import createLogger from '../public/createLogger.js';
import ConsoleTransport from '../transports/ConsoleTransport.js';
import FileTransport from '../transports/FileTransport.js';
import AuditFileTransport from '../transports/AuditFileTransport.js';
import MemoryTransport from '../transports/MemoryTransport.js';
import NullTransport from '../transports/NullTransport.js';
import RedisStreamTransport from '../transports/RedisStreamTransport.js';
import TcpTransport from '../transports/TcpTransport.js';
import UdpTransport from '../transports/UdpTransport.js';
import HttpBatchTransport from '../transports/HttpBatchTransport.js';

function normalizeFileOptions(definition = {}) {
  return {
    ...definition,
    logDirectory: definition.logDirectory || definition.directory,
    fileName: definition.fileName || definition.filename,
    maxSize: definition.maxSize ?? definition.maxBytes,
    rotateInterval: definition.rotateInterval ?? definition.rotateIntervalMs,
    compressOnRotate: definition.compressOnRotate ?? definition.compress,
    retention: definition.retention ? {
      ...definition.retention,
      maxTotalBytes: definition.retention.maxTotalBytes ?? definition.retention.maxBytes,
    } : undefined,
  };
}

function channelError(code, message, details = null) {
  const error = new Error(message);
  error.name = 'LoggerChannelError';
  error.code = code;
  error.details = details;
  return error;
}

/** Configuration-driven, Laravel-style logger channel manager. */
export class ChannelManager {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.loggerFactory = options.loggerFactory || createLogger;
    this.base = { ...(options.base || {}) };
    this.definitions = new Map(Object.entries(options.channels || {}));
    this.instances = new Map();
    this.defaultChannel = options.default || 'app';
    this.bindingResolver = typeof options.bindingResolver === 'function' ? options.bindingResolver : null;
    this.drivers = new Map();
    this.closed = false;

    const customDrivers = options.drivers instanceof Map
      ? options.drivers
      : new Map(Object.entries(options.drivers || {}));
    for (const [name, factory] of customDrivers) this.extend(name, factory, { replace: true });
  }

  setBindingResolver(resolver) {
    if (resolver != null && typeof resolver !== 'function') throw new TypeError('Channel binding resolver must be a function.');
    this.bindingResolver = resolver || null;
    return this;
  }

  resolveBinding(key, fallback = null) {
    if (!key || !this.bindingResolver) return fallback;
    try { return this.bindingResolver(key) ?? fallback; } catch { return fallback; }
  }

  default(name) {
    if (name === undefined) return this.defaultChannel;
    this.defaultChannel = String(name);
    return this;
  }

  extend(driver, factory, options = {}) {
    const name = String(driver || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!name || typeof factory !== 'function') throw new TypeError('ChannelManager.extend requires a driver name and factory.');
    if (this.drivers.has(name) && options.replace !== true) throw new Error(`Logger channel driver [${name}] is already registered.`);
    this.drivers.set(name, factory);
    return this;
  }

  define(name, definition, options = {}) {
    if (this.closed) throw new Error('Cannot define a channel on a closed ChannelManager.');
    const key = String(name || '').trim();
    if (!key) throw new TypeError('Channel name is required.');
    if (this.definitions.has(key) && options.replace !== true) throw new Error(`Logger channel [${key}] is already defined.`);
    if (options.replace === true) this.instances.delete(key);
    this.definitions.set(key, definition);
    return this;
  }

  forget(name, options = {}) {
    const key = String(name);
    const logger = this.instances.get(key);
    this.instances.delete(key);
    if (options.close === true) Promise.resolve(logger?.close?.()).catch(() => {});
    return this.definitions.delete(key) || Boolean(logger);
  }

  has(name) {
    const key = String(name);
    return this.definitions.has(key) || this.instances.has(key);
  }

  _assertAcyclic(name, ancestry = []) {
    const key = String(name);
    const cycleAt = ancestry.indexOf(key);
    if (cycleAt !== -1) {
      const cycle = [...ancestry.slice(cycleAt), key];
      throw channelError(
        'LOGGER_CHANNEL_CYCLE',
        `Logger channel cycle detected: ${cycle.join(' -> ')}.`,
        { cycle },
      );
    }

    const definition = this.definitions.get(key);
    if (!definition || typeof definition === 'function' || typeof definition?.log === 'function') return;
    if (String(definition.driver || '').toLowerCase() !== 'stack') return;

    const children = Array.isArray(definition.channels)
      ? definition.channels.map(String).filter(Boolean)
      : String(definition.channels || '').split(',').map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    const next = [...ancestry, key];
    for (const child of children) {
      if (!this.definitions.has(child) && !this.instances.has(child)) {
        throw channelError(
          'LOGGER_CHANNEL_NOT_FOUND',
          `Logger stack channel [${key}] references undefined channel [${child}].`,
          { channel: key, missing: child },
        );
      }
      this._assertAcyclic(child, next);
    }
  }

  _assertNoStackCycle(start) {
    const visiting = new Set();
    const visited = new Set();
    const path = [];

    const visit = (name) => {
      const key = String(name);
      if (visiting.has(key)) {
        const from = path.indexOf(key);
        const cycle = [...path.slice(from), key];
        const error = new Error(`Circular logger channel stack detected: ${cycle.join(' -> ')}`);
        error.code = 'LOGGER_CHANNEL_CYCLE';
        error.details = { cycle };
        throw error;
      }
      if (visited.has(key)) return;

      const definition = this.definitions.get(key);
      if (!definition || String(definition?.driver || '').toLowerCase() !== 'stack') {
        visited.add(key);
        return;
      }

      visiting.add(key);
      path.push(key);
      const children = Array.isArray(definition.channels)
        ? definition.channels
        : String(definition.channels || '').split(',').map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      for (const child of children) visit(child);
      path.pop();
      visiting.delete(key);
      visited.add(key);
    };

    visit(start);
  }

  channel(name = this.defaultChannel) {
    if (this.closed) throw new Error('ChannelManager is closed.');
    const key = String(name || this.defaultChannel);
    if (this.instances.has(key)) return this.instances.get(key);
    const definition = this.definitions.get(key);
    if (!definition) throw channelError('LOGGER_CHANNEL_NOT_FOUND', `Logger channel [${key}] is not defined.`, { channel: key });
    if (definition?.enabled === false) throw channelError('LOGGER_CHANNEL_DISABLED', `Logger channel [${key}] is disabled.`, { channel: key });
    this._assertAcyclic(key);

    let logger;
    if (typeof definition === 'function') logger = definition({ name: key, manager: this, base: { ...this.base } });
    else if (definition && typeof definition.log === 'function') logger = definition;
    else if (String(definition?.driver || '').toLowerCase() === 'stack') {
      this._assertNoStackCycle(key);
      logger = this.stack(definition.channels || [], { ...definition, name: key, channel: definition.channel || key });
    }
    else logger = this._buildDriverLogger(key, definition || {});

    if (!logger || typeof logger.log !== 'function') throw new TypeError(`Logger channel [${key}] did not resolve to a logger.`);
    this.instances.set(key, logger);
    return logger;
  }

  _buildDriverLogger(name, definition) {
    const driver = String(definition.driver || 'console').toLowerCase();
    const custom = this.drivers.get(driver);
    if (custom) {
      const value = custom({ name, definition, manager: this, base: { ...this.base }, resolve: this.resolveBinding.bind(this) });
      if (value && typeof value.log === 'function') return value;
      if (value) return this.loggerFactory({ ...this.base, ...definition, channel: definition.channel || name, transports: [value] });
      throw new Error(`Custom logger driver [${driver}] returned no logger or transport.`);
    }

    if (Array.isArray(definition.transports)) {
      return this.loggerFactory({ ...this.base, ...definition, channel: definition.channel || name, transports: definition.transports });
    }

    const transport = this._createTransport(name, driver, definition);
    return this.loggerFactory({
      ...this.base,
      ...definition,
      channel: definition.channel || name,
      transports: [{ transport, options: definition.transportOptions || definition.queue || {} }],
    });
  }

  _createTransport(name, driver, definition) {
    if (definition.transport) return definition.transport;
    if (typeof definition.factory === 'function') return definition.factory({ name, definition, manager: this });
    if (driver === 'console' || driver === 'stdout' || driver === 'stderr') return new ConsoleTransport({ ...definition, name });
    if (driver === 'file' || driver === 'daily') return new FileTransport({ ...normalizeFileOptions(definition), name });
    if (driver === 'audit') return new AuditFileTransport({ ...normalizeFileOptions(definition), name });
    if (driver === 'memory') return new MemoryTransport({ ...definition, name });
    if (driver === 'null' || driver === 'silent') return new NullTransport({ ...definition, name });
    if (driver === 'redis' || driver === 'redis-stream') {
      const client = definition.client || this.resolveBinding(definition.binding || 'redis') || this.resolveBinding('redis.client');
      if (!client) throw new Error(`Logger channel [${name}] requires a Redis client binding.`);
      return new RedisStreamTransport(client, { ...definition, name });
    }
    if (driver === 'tcp' || driver === 'tls') return new TcpTransport({ ...definition, name, tls: driver === 'tls' || definition.tls });
    if (driver === 'udp') return new UdpTransport({ ...definition, name });
    if (driver === 'http' || driver === 'http-batch') return new HttpBatchTransport({ ...definition, name });
    throw new Error(`Unsupported logger channel driver [${driver}] for channel [${name}].`);
  }

  stack(names, options = {}) {
    const list = Array.isArray(names)
      ? names.map(String).filter(Boolean)
      : String(names || '').split(',').map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if (!list.length) throw new TypeError('A stack channel requires at least one channel.');

    const transports = list.map((name) => {
      const manager = this;
      return {
        name: `channel:${name}`,
        rawnodeEnterpriseTransport: true,
        owned: false,
        async writeRecord(record) {
          await manager.channel(name).logAsync(record.level, record.message, record.data, {
            channel: record.channel,
            context: record.context,
            error: record.error,
            tags: record.tags,
            awaitDelivery: true,
          });
        },
        async flush(flushOptions) { await manager.channel(name).flush?.(flushOptions); },
        async reopen(reopenOptions) { return await manager.channel(name).reopen?.(reopenOptions); },
        health() { return manager.channel(name).health?.() || { status: 'unknown' }; },
      };
    });

    return this.loggerFactory({
      ...this.base,
      ...options,
      channel: options.channel || options.name || `stack:${list.join(',')}`,
      transports,
    });
  }

  async ready() { await Promise.allSettled([...this.instances.values()].map((logger) => logger.ready?.())); return this; }
  async flush(options = {}) { return await Promise.allSettled([...this.instances.values()].map((logger) => logger.flush?.(options))); }
  async reopen(options = {}) { return await Promise.allSettled([...this.instances.values()].map((logger) => logger.reopen?.(options))); }

  health() {
    return {
      status: this.closed ? 'closed' : 'healthy',
      default: this.defaultChannel,
      channels: Object.fromEntries([...this.instances].map(([name, logger]) => [name, logger.health?.() || { status: 'unknown' }])),
    };
  }
  stats() { return Object.fromEntries([...this.instances].map(([name, logger]) => [name, logger.stats?.() || null])); }

  async close(options = {}) {
    if (this.closed) return;
    this.closed = true;
    const unique = [...new Set(this.instances.values())];
    await Promise.allSettled(unique.map((logger) => logger.close?.(options)));
    this.instances.clear();
  }

  snapshot() {
    return {
      closed: this.closed,
      default: this.defaultChannel,
      definitions: [...this.definitions.keys()],
      active: [...this.instances.keys()],
      drivers: [...this.drivers.keys()],
    };
  }
}

export default ChannelManager;

FILE PATH: ./src/enterprise/channels/StackChannel.js
================================================

'use strict';

const LEVELS = ['emergency','alert','critical','error','urgent','priority','warn','notice','success','info','debug','metric','metrics','important'];

export class StackChannel {
  constructor(name, channels = [], options = {}) {
    this.name = String(name || 'stack');
    this.channels = [...channels];
    this.ignoreExceptions = options.ignoreExceptions === true;
  }
  log(level, message, data, options) {
    const results = [];
    for (const channel of this.channels) {
      try { results.push(channel.log(level, message, data, options)); }
      catch (error) { if (!this.ignoreExceptions) throw error; }
    }
    return results.some((value) => value !== false && value !== null);
  }
  async logAsync(level, message, data, options) {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(this.channels.map((channel) => channel.logAsync(level, message, data, options)));
    const failed = results.filter((entry) => entry.status === 'rejected');
    if (failed.length && !this.ignoreExceptions) throw new AggregateError(failed.map((entry) => entry.reason), `Stack channel [${this.name}] failed.`);
    return results;
  }
  isLevelEnabled(level) { return this.channels.some((channel) => channel.isLevelEnabled?.(level) ?? true); }
  child(context, options) { return new StackChannel(this.name, this.channels.map((channel) => channel.child?.(context, options) || channel), { ignoreExceptions: this.ignoreExceptions }); }
  with(context) { return this.child(context); }
  withTags(...tags) { return new StackChannel(this.name, this.channels.map((channel) => channel.withTags?.(...tags) || channel), { ignoreExceptions: this.ignoreExceptions }); }
  withError(error, context) { return new StackChannel(this.name, this.channels.map((channel) => channel.withError?.(error, context) || channel), { ignoreExceptions: this.ignoreExceptions }); }
  at(level) { return new StackChannel(this.name, this.channels.map((channel) => channel.at?.(level) || channel), { ignoreExceptions: this.ignoreExceptions }); }
  ready(options) { return Promise.allSettled(this.channels.map((channel) => channel.ready?.(options))); }
  flush(options) { return Promise.allSettled(this.channels.map((channel) => channel.flush?.(options))); }
  reopen(options) { return Promise.allSettled(this.channels.map((channel) => channel.reopen?.(options))); }
  // The manager owns member-channel lifecycle; direct stacks intentionally do not close members by default.
  close(options = {}) { return options.closeMembers === true ? Promise.allSettled(this.channels.map((channel) => channel.close?.(options))) : Promise.resolve(); }
  health() {
    const channels = this.channels.map((channel) => channel.health?.() || { status: 'unknown' });
    return { status: channels.some((item) => item.status === 'degraded') ? 'degraded' : 'healthy', name: this.name, channels };
  }
  stats() { return { name: this.name, channels: this.channels.map((channel) => channel.stats?.() || {}) }; }
}
for (const level of LEVELS) StackChannel.prototype[level] = function levelMethod(message, data, options) { return this.log(level, message, data, options); };
export default StackChannel;

FILE PATH: ./src/plugins/container.js

// src/submodules/plugins/container.js
"use strict";

/**
 * PluginContainer manages logger plugins and their lifecycle hooks.
 *
 * Hook points:
 *  - beforeBuild(entry)
 *  - afterBuild(entry)
 *  - beforeWrite(entry, transport)
 *  - afterWrite(entry, transport)
 */
class PluginContainer {
    constructor() {
        this.plugins = [];
    }

    use(plugin = {}) {
        if (plugin && typeof plugin === "object") {
            this.plugins.push(plugin);
        }
        return this;
    }

    runHook(hookName, ...args) {
        for (const plugin of this.plugins) {
            if (plugin.hooks && typeof plugin.hooks[hookName] === "function") {
                try {
                    plugin.hooks[hookName](...args);
                } catch (err) {
                    // plugin errors should NOT break the logger
                    console.error(`Plugin hook error (${hookName}):`, err);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

export default PluginContainer;

'use strict';

import SimpleLogger from '../simple-logger/index.js';
import ConsoleTransport from '../console-transport/index.js';
import FileTransport from '../file-transport/index.js';

/** Compatibility factory for the historical app/core logger. */
export default function createLogger(options = {}) {
  const transports = Array.isArray(options.transports) ? [...options.transports] : [];
  if (!options.transports) {
    if (options.consoleOutput !== false) transports.push(new ConsoleTransport());
    transports.push(new FileTransport({
      logDirectory: options.logDirectory || 'logs',
      fileName: options.accessLogName || 'application.log',
      maxSize: options.maxFileSize,
      rotateInterval: options.rotationInterval,
    }));
  }
  return new SimpleLogger({ level: options.logLevel || options.level || 'info', transports, ...options });
}

FILE PATH: ./src/submodules/observable-console-transport/index.js

'use strict';

import BaseTransport from '../base/BaseTransport.js';
import colors from '../colors/index.js';

const { red, yellow, cyan, blue } = colors;

class ObservableConsoleTransport extends BaseTransport {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super(options);
    this.prefix = options.prefix || '';
    this.suffix = options.suffix || '';
    this.colors = { error: red, warn: yellow, info: blue, debug: cyan };
  }

  _transform(chunk, _encoding, callback) {
    if (this.silent) return callback(null, chunk);
    const entry = this._normalize(chunk);
    this._safeEmit('beforeWrite', entry);
    const colorFn = this.colors[entry.level] || ((text) => text);
    const output = colorFn(`${this.prefix}${entry.formatted ?? entry.message ?? ''}${this.suffix}`);
    if (entry.level === 'error') console.error(output);
    else if (entry.level === 'warn') console.warn(output);
    else console.log(output);
    this.stats.written++;
    this._safeEmit('afterWrite', entry);
    callback(null, chunk);
  }
}

export default ObservableConsoleTransport;


================================================
FILE PATH: ./src/submodules/config/index.js
================================================

'use strict';

import colors from '../colors/index.js';

const { rgb, bold, underline } = colors;
const defaultData = rgb(112, 162, 216);
const defaultLogEntry = rgb(24, 48, 72);

let currentProfile = 'default';
const profiles = Object.create(null);

profiles.default = {
  emergency: [139, 0, 0], alert: [255, 165, 0], critical: [255, 0, 0], error: [244, 15, 8],
  urgent: [255, 69, 0], priority: [255, 140, 0], warn: [255, 215, 0], notice: [128, 128, 128],
  success: [10, 220, 10], important: [10, 220, 10], info: [2, 121, 249], debug: [0, 200, 200],
  metrics: [0, 200, 200], metric: [0, 200, 200],
};
profiles.dark = {
  emergency: [200, 0, 0], alert: [255, 200, 0], critical: [255, 50, 50], error: [255, 100, 100],
  urgent: [255, 120, 0], priority: [255, 160, 0], warn: [255, 220, 0], notice: [160, 160, 160],
  success: [0, 255, 0], important: [0, 255, 0], info: [0, 150, 255], debug: [0, 255, 255],
  metrics: [0, 255, 255], metric: [0, 255, 255],
};

function ensureRGB(value, name) {
  if (!Array.isArray(value) || value.length !== 3 || value.some((entry) => !Number.isFinite(Number(entry)))) {
    throw new Error(`${name} must be an array of three numbers.`);
  }
}

function normalize(value) {
  if (value === null) return 'null';
  if (value === undefined) return 'undefined';
  if (typeof value === 'object') {
    try { return JSON.stringify(value); }
    catch { return '[Unserializable]'; }
  }
  return String(value);
}

function compose(first, second) {
  if (!first) return second;
  if (!second) return first;
  return (text) => second(first(text));
}

export function createLevelConfig({ level, formattedRGB, bgRGB = null, isBold = false, isUnderlined = false, context = {} }) {
  ensureRGB(formattedRGB, 'formattedRGB');
  let formatted = rgb(...formattedRGB);
  if (isBold) formatted = compose(formatted, bold);
  if (isUnderlined) formatted = compose(formatted, underline);
  if (bgRGB) { ensureRGB(bgRGB, 'bgRGB'); formatted = compose(formatted, rgb(...bgRGB)); }
  const contextEntries = Object.entries(context || {});
  const safeFormatted = (value) => {
    let output = formatted(normalize(value));
    if (contextEntries.length) output += ` | ${contextEntries.map(([key, val]) => `${key}: ${val}`).join(', ')}`;
    return output;
  };
  return { level, formatted: safeFormatted, data: (value) => defaultData(normalize(value)), logEntry: (value) => defaultLogEntry(normalize(value)) };
}

function buildLevelConfigs() {
  const p = profiles[currentProfile];
  const out = Object.create(null);
  for (const level of Object.keys(p)) {
    out[level] = createLevelConfig({ level, formattedRGB: p[level], isBold: ['emergency','alert','critical','important'].includes(level) });
  }
  return out;
}

let levelConfigurations = buildLevelConfigs();

function setProfile(name) {
  if (!profiles[name]) throw new Error(`Unknown profile: ${name}`);
  currentProfile = name;
  levelConfigurations = buildLevelConfigs();
  return api;
}
function getProfile() { return currentProfile; }
function registerProfile(name, definition, options = {}) {
  const key = String(name || '').trim();
  if (!key) throw new TypeError('Profile name is required.');
  if (profiles[key] && options.replace !== true) throw new Error(`Profile already exists: ${key}`);
  const base = options.extend ? profiles[options.extend] : profiles.default;
  profiles[key] = { ...base, ...(definition || {}) };
  for (const [level, value] of Object.entries(profiles[key])) ensureRGB(value, `${key}.${level}`);
  return api;
}
function getLevelConfig(level) { return levelConfigurations[level] || null; }
function updateLevelConfig(level, newConfig) {
  if (!levelConfigurations[level]) throw new Error(`Log level ${level} does not exist.`);
  levelConfigurations[level] = { ...levelConfigurations[level], ...(newConfig || {}) };
  return api;
}
function resetLevelConfigs() { levelConfigurations = buildLevelConfigs(); return api; }
function listAvailableLevels() { return Object.keys(levelConfigurations); }
function listProfiles() { return Object.keys(profiles); }
function snapshot() { return { profile: currentProfile, levels: listAvailableLevels(), profiles: listProfiles() }; }

const api = {
  get levelConfigurations() { return levelConfigurations; },
  get profiles() { return { ...profiles }; },
  getLevelConfig,
  updateLevelConfig,
  resetLevelConfigs,
  setProfile,
  getProfile,
  registerProfile,
  listAvailableLevels,
  listProfiles,
  snapshot,
  createLevelConfig,
};

export default api;

// laboratorio entry point
