'use strict';

// --- 256-color mapping ---
function _toAnsi256(r, g, b) {
  // Compute nearest in 6x6x6 cube
  const to6 = (v) => Math.round((v / 255) * 5);
  const ir = to6(r), ig = to6(g), ib = to6(b);
  const cubeIndex = 16 + 36*ir + 6*ig + ib;

  // Grayscale range [232..255]
  const avg = (r + g + b) / 3;
  const grayIndex = 232 + Math.round(((avg / 255) * 23));

  // pick closer by Euclidean distance
  const cr = [0,95,135,175,215,255][ir];
  const cg = [0,95,135,175,215,255][ig];
  const cb = [0,95,135,175,215,255][ib];
  const dc = (cr-r)*(cr-r) + (cg-g)*(cg-g) + (cb-b)*(cb-b);
  const gv = 8 + 10 * (grayIndex - 232);
  const dg = 3*(gv - avg)*(gv - avg);
  return (dg < dc) ? grayIndex : cubeIndex;
}

// --- 16-color mapping ---
const ANSI16 = [
  [0,0,0], [128,0,0], [0,128,0], [128,128,0], [0,0,128], [128,0,128], [0,128,128], [192,192,192],
  [128,128,128], [255,0,0], [0,255,0], [255,255,0], [0,0,255], [255,0,255], [0,255,255], [255,255,255]
];

function _nearestAnsi16(r, g, b) {
  let best = 0, bestD = Infinity;
  for (let i = 0; i < 16; i += 1) {
    const c = ANSI16[i];
    const d = (c[0]-r)*(c[0]-r) + (c[1]-g)*(c[1]-g) + (c[2]-b)*(c[2]-b);
    if (d < bestD) { bestD = d; best = i; }
  }
  // Map 0..15 -> SGR 30..37 (0..7) and 90..97 (8..15)
  if (best < 8) { return { base: 30 + best, bright: false }; }
  return { base: 90 + (best - 8), bright: true };
}

// Returns "38;2;r;g;b" | "38;5;idx" | "30..37/90..97"
function _sgrFG(level, r, g, b, metrics) {
  if (level === 3) { return '38;2;' + r + ';' + g + ';' + b; }
  if (level === 2) { return '38;5;' + _toAnsi256(r, g, b); }
  const m = _nearestAnsi16(r, g, b);
  return String(m.base);
}
function _sgrBG(level, r, g, b, metrics) {
  if (level === 3) { return '48;2;' + r + ';' + g + ';' + b; }
  if (level === 2) { return '48;5;' + _toAnsi256(r, g, b); }
  const m = _nearestAnsi16(r, g, b);
  // BG: 40..47/100..107
  const idx = parseInt(String(m.base), 10);
  if (idx >= 90) { return String(100 + (idx - 90)); }
  return String(40 + (idx - 30));
}

function sgrForFG(level, rgb, metrics) {
  const s = _sgrFG(level, rgb.r|0, rgb.g|0, rgb.b|0, metrics);
  return { open: '\x1B[' + s + 'm', close: '\x1B[39m', describe: () => ({ kind: 'fg', level, s }) };
}

function sgrForBG(level, rgb, metrics) {
  const s = _sgrBG(level, rgb.r|0, rgb.g|0, rgb.b|0, metrics);
  return { open: '\x1B[' + s + 'm', close: '\x1B[49m', describe: () => ({ kind: 'bg', level, s }) };
}

export default { sgrForFG, sgrForBG };
export { sgrForFG, sgrForBG };

'use strict';

// Build an OKLCH-based gradient that applies to grapheme segments
function _lerp(a, b, t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }

function makeGradient(stops, opts, helpers) {
  const mode = (opts && opts.mode) ? String(opts.mode).toLowerCase() : 'oklch';
  const parsed = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < stops.length; i += 1) {
    parsed.push(helpers.to(mode, helpers.parse(stops[i])));
  }

  function _colorAt(t) {
    if (mode === 'oklch') {
      const seg = Math.max(0, Math.min(parsed.length - 2, Math.floor(t * (parsed.length - 1))));
      const localT = (t * (parsed.length - 1)) - seg;
      const a = parsed[seg], b = parsed[seg + 1];
      const L = _lerp(a.L, b.L, localT);
      const C = _lerp(a.C, b.C, localT);
      // Shortest hue interpolation
      let dH = b.H - a.H;
      if (dH > 180) { dH -= 360; }
      if (dH < -180) { dH += 360; }
      const H = (a.H + dH * localT + 360) % 360;
      const rgb = helpers.to('rgb', { space: 'oklch', L, C, H, alpha: 1 });
      return rgb;
    }
    // Fallback: linear sRGB mix
    const seg = Math.max(0, Math.min(parsed.length - 2, Math.floor(t * (parsed.length - 1))));
    const localT = (t * (parsed.length - 1)) - seg;
    const A = helpers.to('rgb', parsed[seg]);
    const B = helpers.to('rgb', parsed[seg + 1]);
    return helpers.env ? helpers.env.mixLinearRGB(A, B, localT) : helpers.mixLinearRGB(A, B, localT);
  }

  return {
    apply(text) {
      const gs = helpers.segmentGraphemes(String(text));
      const n = gs.length === 0 ? 1 : gs.length;
      let out = '';
      for (let i = 0; i < gs.length; i += 1) {
        const t = i / Math.max(1, (n - 1));
        const rgb = _colorAt(t);
        out += helpers.applyFG(rgb, gs[i]);
      }
      return out;
    }
  };
}

export default { makeGradient };
export { makeGradient };

'use strict';

// sRGB <-> linear
function _toLinear(c) { const v = c / 255; return v <= 0.04045 ? (v / 12.92) : Math.pow((v + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4); }
function _toGamma(v) { return v <= 0.0031308 ? (v * 12.92) : (1.055 * Math.pow(v, 1 / 2.4) - 0.055); }

function rgbToHsl({ r, g, b, a }) {
  const R = r / 255, G = g / 255, B = b / 255;
  const max = Math.max(R, G, B), min = Math.min(R, G, B);
  let h = 0, s = 0, l = (max + min) / 2;
  if (max !== min) {
    const d = max - min;
    s = l > 0.5 ? d / (2 - max - min) : d / (max + min);
    switch (max) {
      case R: h = (G - B) / d + (G < B ? 6 : 0); break;
      case G: h = (B - R) / d + 2; break;
      default: h = (R - G) / d + 4; break;
    }
    h *= 60;
  }
  return { space: 'hsl', h, s, l, a: a === undefined ? 1 : a };
}

function hslToRgb({ h, s, l, a }) {
  const C = (1 - Math.abs(2*l - 1)) * s;
  const Hp = (h % 360) / 60;
  const X = C * (1 - Math.abs((Hp % 2) - 1));
  let R = 0, G = 0, B = 0;
  if (Hp >= 0 && Hp < 1) { R=C; G=X; B=0; }
  else if (Hp < 2) { R=X; G=C; B=0; }
  else if (Hp < 3) { R=0; G=C; B=X; }
  else if (Hp < 4) { R=0; G=X; B=C; }
  else if (Hp < 5) { R=X; G=0; B=C; }
  else { R=C; G=0; B=X; }
  const m = l - C/2;
  const r = Math.round((R + m) * 255);
  const g = Math.round((G + m) * 255);
  const b = Math.round((B + m) * 255);
  return { space: 'rgb', r, g, b, a: a === undefined ? 1 : a };
}

// OKLab/OKLCH (D65) based on published spec constants
function rgbToOklab({ r, g, b, a }) {
  // to linear sRGB
  const rl = _toLinear(r), gl = _toLinear(g), bl = _toLinear(b);
  // linear RGB to LMS
  const l = 0.4122214708*rl + 0.5363325363*gl + 0.0514459929*bl;
  const m = 0.2119034982*rl + 0.6806995451*gl + 0.1073969566*bl;
  const s = 0.0883024619*rl + 0.2817188376*gl + 0.6299787005*bl;
  const l_ = Math.cbrt(l), m_ = Math.cbrt(m), s_ = Math.cbrt(s);
  const L = 0.2104542553*l_ + 0.7936177850*m_ - 0.0040720468*s_;
  const a_ = 1.9779984951*l_ - 2.4285922050*m_ + 0.4505937099*s_;
  const b_ = 0.0259040371*l_ + 0.7827717662*m_ - 0.8086757660*s_;
  return { space: 'oklab', L, a: a_, b: b_, alpha: a === undefined ? 1 : a };
}

function oklabToRgb({ L, a, b, alpha }) {
  const l_ = Math.pow(L + 0.3963377774*a + 0.2158037573*b, 3);
  const m_ = Math.pow(L - 0.1055613458*a - 0.0638541728*b, 3);
  const s_ = Math.pow(L - 0.0894841775*a - 1.2914855480*b, 3);

  let rl = +4.0767416621*l_ - 3.3077115913*m_ + 0.2309699292*s_;
  let gl = -1.2684380046*l_ + 2.6097574011*m_ - 0.3413193965*s_;
  let bl = -0.0041960863*l_ - 0.7034186147*m_ + 1.7076147010*s_;

  rl = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, rl));
  gl = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, gl));
  bl = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, bl));

  return {
    space: 'rgb',
    r: Math.round(_toGamma(rl) * 255),
    g: Math.round(_toGamma(gl) * 255),
    b: Math.round(_toGamma(bl) * 255),
    a: alpha === undefined ? 1 : alpha
  };
}

function oklabToOklch({ L, a, b, alpha }) {
  const C = Math.sqrt(a*a + b*b);
  let H = Math.atan2(b, a) * 180 / Math.PI;
  if (H < 0) { H += 360; }
  return { space: 'oklch', L, C, H, alpha: alpha === undefined ? 1 : alpha };
}

function oklchToOklab({ L, C, H, alpha }) {
  const hrad = (H * Math.PI) / 180;
  return { space: 'oklab', L, a: C * Math.cos(hrad), b: C * Math.sin(hrad), alpha: alpha === undefined ? 1 : alpha };
}

function to(space, color) {
  const target = String(space).toLowerCase();
  if (color.space === target) { return color; }
  if (target === 'rgb') {
    if (color.space === 'rgb') { return color; }
    if (color.space === 'hsl') { return hslToRgb(color); }
    if (color.space === 'oklab') { return oklabToRgb(color); }
    if (color.space === 'oklch') { return oklabToRgb(oklchToOklab(color)); }
  }
  if (target === 'hsl') {
    const rgb = to('rgb', color);
    return rgbToHsl(rgb);
  }
  if (target === 'oklab') {
    const rgb = to('rgb', color);
    return rgbToOklab(rgb);
  }
  if (target === 'oklch') {
    const ol = to('oklab', color);
    return oklabToOklch(ol);
  }
  return color;
}

function _clamp01(x) { return x < 0 ? 0 : (x > 1 ? 1 : x); }

function mix(a, b, t) {
  // gamma-correct mix in linear sRGB
  const A = to('rgb', a), B = to('rgb', b);
  const ar = _toLinear(A.r), ag = _toLinear(A.g), ab = _toLinear(A.b);
  const br = _toLinear(B.r), bg = _toLinear(B.g), bb = _toLinear(B.b);
  const tr = ar + (br - ar) * t;
  const tg = ag + (bg - ag) * t;
  const tb = ab + (bb - ab) * t;
  return { space: 'rgb', r: Math.round(_toGamma(_clamp01(tr)) * 255), g: Math.round(_toGamma(_clamp01(tg)) * 255), b: Math.round(_toGamma(_clamp01(tb)) * 255), a: 1 };
}

function luminance(color) {
  const c = to('rgb', color);
  const rl = _toLinear(c.r), gl = _toLinear(c.g), bl = _toLinear(c.b);
  return 0.2126*rl + 0.7152*gl + 0.0722*bl;
}

function contrast(a, b) {
  const L1 = luminance(a);
  const L2 = luminance(b);
  const hi = Math.max(L1, L2), lo = Math.min(L1, L2);
  return (hi + 0.05) / (lo + 0.05);
}

function chooseOn(bg, opts) {
  const min = opts && typeof opts.min === 'number' ? opts.min : 4.5;
  const black = { space: 'rgb', r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, a: 1 };
  const white = { space: 'rgb', r: 255, g: 255, b: 255, a: 1 };
  const cB = contrast(bg, black);
  const cW = contrast(bg, white);
  if (cB >= min && cB >= cW) { return black; }
  if (cW >= min && cW >= cB) { return white; }
  // pick better anyway
  return cW >= cB ? white : black;
}

export default { to, mix, contrast, chooseOn, rgbToHsl, hslToRgb };
export { to, mix, contrast, chooseOn, rgbToHsl, hslToRgb };

'use strict';

import BaseTransport from '../base/BaseTransport.js';
import AsyncFileTransport from '../../transports/AsyncFileTransport.js';

class ObservableFileTransport extends BaseTransport {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super(options);
    this.delegate = new AsyncFileTransport({
      ...options,
      directory: options.directory || options.logDirectory,
      filename: options.filename || options.fileName,
      maxBytes: options.maxBytes || options.maxSize,
      rotateIntervalMs: options.rotateIntervalMs || options.rotateInterval,
      formatter: options.formatter || { format: (record) => String(record.formatted ?? record.message ?? '') },
    });
    this.logDirectory = this.delegate.logDirectory;
    this.fileName = this.delegate.fileName;
    this.filePath = this.delegate.filePath;
    for (const event of ['open','written','rotateStart','rotate','compress','retentionDelete','reopen','error']) {
      this.delegate.on(event, (...args) => this._safeEmit(event, ...args));
    }
  }

  _transform(chunk, _encoding, callback) {
    const entry = this._normalize(chunk);
    this._safeEmit('beforeWrite', entry);
    this.delegate.writeRecord(entry).then(() => {
      this.stats.written++;
      this._safeEmit('written', entry);
      this._safeEmit('afterWrite', entry);
      callback(null, chunk);
    }, (error) => {
      this.stats.errors++;
      this._safeEmit('transportError', error);
      callback(error);
    });
  }

  rotate(options) { return this.delegate.rotate(options); }
  flush(options) { return this.delegate.flush(options); }
  reopen() { return this.delegate.reopen(); }
  health() { return this.delegate.health(); }
  async close() { await this.delegate.close(); await super.close(); }
}

export default ObservableFileTransport;

'use strict';

import BaseTransport from '../base/BaseTransport.js';
import colors from '../colors/index.js';

const { red, yellow, green, white, blue, cyan, magenta } = colors;

class ObservableConsoleTransportEnhanced extends BaseTransport {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super(options);
    this.prefix = typeof options.prefix === 'function' ? options.prefix('') : typeof options.prefix === 'string' ? options.prefix : '';
    this.suffix = typeof options.suffix === 'function' ? options.suffix('') : typeof options.suffix === 'string' ? options.suffix : '';
    this.levelColors = {
      emergency: red, alert: red, critical: red, error: red, urgent: red,
      priority: yellow, warn: yellow, notice: white, success: green,
      info: blue, debug: cyan, metric: magenta || cyan, metrics: magenta || cyan, important: green,
      ...(options.levelColors || {}),
    };
    this.formatter = options.formatter || ((message) => message);
  }

  _transform(chunk, _encoding, callback) {
    if (this.silent) return callback(null, chunk);
    const entry = this._normalize(chunk);
    this._safeEmit('beforeFormat', entry);
    let message = `${this.prefix}${entry.formatted ?? entry.message ?? ''}${this.suffix}`;
    try { message = this.formatter(message, entry, chunk); }
    catch (error) {
      this._safeEmit('formatError', error, entry);
      message = `${this.prefix}${entry.formatted ?? entry.message ?? ''}${this.suffix}`;
    }
    this._safeEmit('afterFormat', message, entry);
    const colorFn = this.levelColors[entry.level] || ((text) => text);
    let output;
    try { output = colorFn(message); } catch { output = message; }
    this._safeEmit('beforeWrite', output, entry);
    if (['error','critical','alert','emergency','urgent'].includes(entry.level)) console.error(output);
    else if (['warn','priority'].includes(entry.level)) console.warn(output);
    else console.log(output);
    this.stats.written++;
    this._safeEmit('afterWrite', output, entry);
    callback(null, chunk);
  }
}

export default ObservableConsoleTransportEnhanced;

FILE PATH: ./src/submodules/observable-console-transport-enhanced/errors/index.js

'use strict';
import Transformer from "../../Transformer/index.js";
import { blue, red, yellow, green, magenta, reset } from "../../colors/index.js";


// Require your color module (adjust the path as needed)// moved import for { blue, red, yellow, green, magenta, reset }// moved import for Transformer

/**
 * ObservableConsoleTransportEnhanced extends Transformer to provide an enterprise-grade,
 * fully event-driven console transport with configurable formatting using your color module.
 */
class ObservableConsoleTransportEnhanced extends Transformer {
  /**
   * Creates a new ObservableConsoleTransportEnhanced instance.
   * @param {Object} options - Configuration options.
   * @param {string} [options.prefix=''] - Optional prefix to prepend to each log message.
   * @param {string} [options.suffix=''] - Optional suffix to append to each log message.
   * @param {Function} [options.formatter] - Custom formatter function; receives (message, logEntry).
   */
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super({ objectMode: true });
    this.prefix = options.prefix || '';
    this.suffix = options.suffix || '';
    // Use the color module to set default colors for each log level.
    this.levelColors = {
      error: red,
      warn: yellow,
      info: blue,
      debug: magenta
    };
    // Allow custom formatter; defaults to identity function.
    this.formatter = options.formatter || ((msg) => msg);
  }

  /**
   * _transform processes each log entry. It emits events at various stages:
   * - 'beforeFormat': right before applying formatting.
   * - 'afterFormat': after formatting is applied.
   * - 'beforeWrite': just before outputting to the console.
   * - 'afterWrite': immediately after the log entry is output.
   *
   * @param {Object} logEntry - The log entry object (expects a 'formatted' property).
   * @param {string} encoding - Not used in object mode.
   * @param {Function} callback - Callback to signal completion.
   * @private
   */

  // In AdvancedLogger.js
// _transform(logEntry, encoding, callback) {
//   this.transports.forEach((transport) => {
//     try {
//       // Pass the full log entry object rather than separate arguments.
//       transport.write(logEntry);
//     } catch (err) {
//       console.error('Transport error in AdvancedLogger:', err);
//     }
//   });
//   callback(null, logEntry);
// }

  _transform(logEntry, encoding, callback) {
    // Emit event before formatting.
    this.emit('beforeFormat', logEntry);

    // Build the message with prefix and suffix.
    let message = `${this.prefix}${logEntry.formatted}${this.suffix}`;
    // Apply the custom formatter.
    message = this.formatter(message, logEntry);

    // Emit event after formatting.
    this.emit('afterFormat', message, logEntry);

    // Use the color module for colorizing based on log level.
    const colorFn = this.levelColors[logEntry.level] || ((text) => text);
    const output = colorFn(message) + reset();

    // Emit event before writing to console.
    this.emit('beforeWrite', output, logEntry);

    // Write to console using appropriate method.
    switch (logEntry.level) {
      case 'error':
        console.error(output);
        break;
      case 'warn':
        console.warn(output);
        break;
      default:
        console.log(output);
    }

    // Emit event after writing.
    this.emit('afterWrite', output, logEntry);

    // Signal that transformation is complete.
    callback(null, logEntry);
  }
}

export default ObservableConsoleTransportEnhanced;

'use strict';

import path from 'node:path';
import AsyncFileTransport from '../../transports/AsyncFileTransport.js';

/**
 * Legacy two-argument file transport backed by the non-blocking file runtime.
 * write(level, formatted) remains unchanged.
 */
class FileTransport {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.name = options.name || 'file-legacy';
    this.logDirectory = path.resolve(options.logDirectory || options.directory || path.join(process.cwd(), 'logs'));
    this.fileName = options.fileName || options.filename || 'application.log';
    this.maxSize = options.maxSize || options.maxBytes || 10 * 1024 * 1024;
    this.rotateInterval = options.rotateInterval || options.rotateIntervalMs || 24 * 60 * 60 * 1_000;
    this.delegate = new AsyncFileTransport({
      ...options,
      name: `${this.name}:delegate`,
      directory: this.logDirectory,
      filename: this.fileName,
      maxBytes: this.maxSize,
      rotateIntervalMs: this.rotateInterval,
      formatter: { format: (record) => String(record.formatted ?? record.message ?? '') },
    });
    this.filePath = this.delegate.filePath;
    this.closed = false;
  }

  write(level, logEntry) {
    if (this.closed) return false;
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
    const promise = this.delegate.writeRecord({ timestamp, level, message: String(logEntry), formatted: String(logEntry), data: null });
    promise.catch(() => {});
    return promise;
  }

  _rotateLogs() { return this.delegate.rotate({ force: false }); }
  rotate(options) { return this.delegate.rotate(options); }
  flush(options) { return this.delegate.flush(options); }
  reopen() { return this.delegate.reopen(); }
  health() { return this.delegate.health(); }
  async close() { if (this.closed) return; this.closed = true; await this.delegate.close(); }
}

export default FileTransport;

'use strict';

import AdvancedLogger from '../advanced-logger/index.js';
import Config from '../config/index.js';

class LoggerBuilder {
  constructor() {
    this.options = {
      profile: 'v1',
      level: 'info',
      transports: [],
      serialize: false,
      colors: true,
      mode: 'json',
      context: {},
      tags: [],
    };
    this.plugins = [];
    this.processors = [];
  }

  profile(name = 'v1') { this.options.profile = name; return this; }
  level(name = 'info') { this.options.level = name; return this; }
  transport(instance, options = {}) {
    if (!instance) throw new TypeError('LoggerBuilder.transport requires a transport.');
    this.options.transports.push(instance);
    if (Object.keys(options).length) {
      this.options.transportOptions ||= new Map();
      this.options.transportOptions.set(instance, options);
    }
    return this;
  }
  transports(instances = []) { for (const instance of instances) this.transport(instance); return this; }
  serialize(enable = true) { this.options.serialize = enable; return this; }
  mode(mode = 'object') { this.options.mode = mode; return this; }
  useColors(enable = true) { this.options.colors = enable; return this; }
  withProfile(name) { Config.setProfile(name); this.options.profile = name === 'default' || name === 'dark' ? 'v1' : name; return this; }
  context(values = {}) { Object.assign(this.options.context, values); return this; }
  tag(...tags) { this.options.tags.push(...tags.flat().map(String)); return this; }
  channel(name) { this.options.channel = name; return this; }
  service(name) { this.options.service = name; return this; }
  environment(name) { this.options.environment = name; return this; }
  queue(options = {}) { this.options.queue = { ...(this.options.queue || {}), ...options }; return this; }
  security(options = {}) { this.options.security = { ...(this.options.security || {}), ...options }; return this; }
  formatter(formatter) { this.options.formatter = formatter; return this; }
  processor(processor, options = {}) { this.processors.push({ processor, ...options }); return this; }
  use(processor, options = {}) { return this.processor(processor, options); }
  plugin(plugin) { this.plugins.push(plugin); return this; }

  done() {
    const logger = new AdvancedLogger({ ...this.options, processorList: this.processors, pluginList: this.plugins });
    const map = this.options.transportOptions;
    if (map) {
      for (const [transport, options] of map) {
        const adapter = logger.runtime.dispatcher.adapters.find((item) => item.transport === transport);
        if (adapter) Object.assign(adapter, options);
      }
    }
    return logger;
  }

  build() { return this.done(); }
}

export default LoggerBuilder;

    }

'use strict';

import Transformer from '../Transformer/index.js';
import formatLog from '../formatter/formatLog.js';
import buildLogEntry from '../formatter/buildLogEntry.js';
import colors from '../colors/index.js';
import LoggerRuntime from '../../enterprise/core/LoggerRuntime.js';
import { COMPATIBILITY_VIEW } from '../../enterprise/core/TransportAdapter.js';

const { rgb, green } = colors;
const endTimestamp = rgb(184, 197, 209);

const MUTATING_ARRAY_METHODS = new Set(['push', 'pop', 'shift', 'unshift', 'splice', 'sort', 'reverse', 'copyWithin', 'fill']);

function createTransportCollection(owner, initial = []) {
  const target = [...initial];
  return new Proxy(target, {
    get(array, property, receiver) {
      if (MUTATING_ARRAY_METHODS.has(property)) {
        return (...args) => {
          const result = Array.prototype[property].apply(array, args);
          owner._syncTransports?.();
          return result;
        };
      }
      return Reflect.get(array, property, receiver);
    },
    set(array, property, value, receiver) {
      const result = Reflect.set(array, property, value, receiver);
      owner._syncTransports?.();
      return result;
    },
    deleteProperty(array, property) {
      const result = Reflect.deleteProperty(array, property);
      owner._syncTransports?.();
      return result;
    },
  });
}

function safeDataText(value, serializer) {
  if (value === null) return 'null';
  if (value === undefined) return 'undefined';
  if (typeof value === 'string') return value;
  return serializer.stringify(value);
}

/**
 * Backward-compatible AdvancedLogger facade powered by the enterprise runtime.
 * It remains a Transform stream and preserves the existing v1 methods, output
 * mode, middleware semantics, event name, level order, and transport list.
 */
class AdvancedLogger extends Transformer {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super({ objectMode: true, autoDestroy: true });
    this.level = options.level || 'info';
    this._transportOptions = new Map();
    const initialTransports = [];
    for (const entry of Array.isArray(options.transports) ? options.transports : []) {
      const transport = entry?.transport || entry;
      if (!transport) continue;
      initialTransports.push(transport);
      if (entry?.transport) this._transportOptions.set(transport, { ...(entry.options || {}) });
    }
    this.transports = createTransportCollection(this, initialTransports);
    this.serialize = options.serialize || false;
    const profileName = typeof options.profile === 'string' ? options.profile : options.profile?.name || 'v1';
    this.mode = options.mode || (profileName === 'v1' ? 'json' : 'object');
    this.colors = options.colors ?? (profileName === 'v1');
    this._closing = false;

    const compatibilityViewFactory = async (record) => this._buildCompatibilityView(record);
    compatibilityViewFactory.shouldBuild = () => this._needsCompatibilityView();

    this.runtime = new LoggerRuntime({
      ...options,
      profile: options.profile || 'v1',
      level: this._level,
      transports: this.transports.map((transport) => ({ transport, options: this._transportOptions.get(transport) || {} })),
      transportPayload: this.mode === 'object' ? 'object' : 'json',
      compatibilityViewFactory,
    });

    this.runtime.on('record', (record) => {
      const compatibility = record[COMPATIBILITY_VIEW] || record;
      try { this.emit('record', record); } catch {}
      try { this.emit('log', compatibility); } catch {}
      if (this.readableFlowing || this.listenerCount('data') > 0 || this.listenerCount('readable') > 0) {
        const payload = this.mode === 'object' ? compatibility : this.runtime.serializer.stringify(compatibility);
        this.push(payload);
      }
    });
  }

  get level() { return this.runtime?.level || this._level || 'info'; }
  set level(value) {
    this._level = String(value || 'info');
    if (this.runtime) this.runtime.setLevel(this._level);
  }

  _syncTransports() {
    if (!this.runtime?.dispatcher) return;
    const desired = new Set(this.transports);
    for (const adapter of [...this.runtime.dispatcher.adapters]) {
      if (!desired.has(adapter.transport)) this.runtime.dispatcher.remove(adapter.transport);
    }
    for (const transport of desired) {
      if (!this.runtime.dispatcher.adapters.some((adapter) => adapter.transport === transport)) {
        this.runtime.addTransport(transport, this._transportOptions.get(transport) || {});
      }
    }
  }

  _needsCompatibilityView() {
    if (this.runtime?.profile?.name === 'v1') return true;
    if (this.colors || this.serialize || this.middlewareStack?.length) return true;
    if (this.listenerCount('log') > 0 || this.listenerCount('data') > 0 || this.listenerCount('readable') > 0 || this.readableFlowing) return true;
    return this.transports.some((transport) => !(
      transport?.rawnodeEnterpriseTransport === true
      && (typeof transport.writeRecord === 'function' || typeof transport.writeBatch === 'function')
    ));
  }

  async _buildCompatibilityView(record) {
    const plainFormatted = `[${record.timestamp}] [${String(record.level).toUpperCase()}] ${record.message}${record.data == null ? '' : ` => ${safeDataText(record.data, this.runtime.serializer)}`}`;
    const legacyFormatted = this.colors
      ? formatLog({ timestamp: record.timestamp, level: record.level, message: record.message, data: record.data })
      : plainFormatted;
    const formatted = this.serialize ? this.runtime.serializer.stringify(legacyFormatted) : legacyFormatted;
    const base = this.colors
      ? buildLogEntry({
          timestamp: endTimestamp(record.timestamp),
          level: record.level,
          message: record.message,
          formatted,
          data: green(safeDataText(record.data, this.runtime.serializer)),
        })
      : {
          timestamp: record.timestamp,
          level: record.level,
          message: record.message,
          formatted,
          data: record.data,
          color: (value) => String(value),
        };
    const entry = await this.applyMiddleware(base);
    return entry ?? base;
  }

  _shouldLog(level) {
    return this.runtime.isLevelEnabled(level);
  }

  log(level, message, data = null, options = {}) {
    return this.runtime.log(level, message, data, options);
  }

  async logAsync(level, message, data = null, options = {}) {
    return await this.runtime.logAsync(level, message, data, options);
  }

  emergency(message, data, options) { return this.log('emergency', message, data, options); }
  alert(message, data, options) { return this.log('alert', message, data, options); }
  critical(message, data, options) { return this.log('critical', message, data, options); }
  error(message, data, options) { return this.log('error', message, data, options); }
  urgent(message, data, options) { return this.log('urgent', message, data, options); }
  priority(message, data, options) { return this.log('priority', message, data, options); }
  warn(message, data, options) { return this.log('warn', message, data, options); }
  notice(message, data, options) { return this.log('notice', message, data, options); }
  success(message, data, options) { return this.log('success', message, data, options); }
  info(message, data, options) { return this.log('info', message, data, options); }
  debug(message, data, options) { return this.log('debug', message, data, options); }
  metric(message, data, options) { return this.log('metric', message, data, options); }
  metrics(message, data, options) { return this.log('metrics', message, data, options); }
  important(message, data, options) { return this.log('important', message, data, options); }

  use(middlewareFn, options = {}) {
    if (typeof middlewareFn !== 'function') throw new TypeError('Logger middleware must be a function.');
    // V1 keeps the exact historic compatibility-entry middleware contract.
    // Modern profiles use the canonical structured-record processor pipeline.
    if (this.runtime.profile.name === 'v1' || options.legacy === true) super.use(middlewareFn);
    else this.runtime.use(middlewareFn, options);
    return this;
  }

  middleware(middlewareFn) {
    if (typeof middlewareFn !== 'function') throw new TypeError('Logger middleware must be a function.');
    super.use(middlewareFn);
    return this;
  }

  processor(processor, options = {}) { this.runtime.use(processor, options); return this; }
  plugin(plugin) { this.runtime.plugin(plugin); return this; }

  addTransport(transport, options = {}) {
    if (!transport) throw new TypeError('AdvancedLogger.addTransport requires a transport.');
    this._transportOptions.set(transport, { ...(options || {}) });
    if (!this.transports.includes(transport)) this.transports.push(transport);
    else if (!this.runtime.dispatcher.adapters.some((item) => item.transport === transport)) this._syncTransports();
    return this;
  }

  transport(transport, options = {}) { return this.addTransport(transport, options); }

  removeTransport(nameOrTransport, options = {}) {
    const index = this.transports.findIndex((transport) => transport === nameOrTransport || transport?.name === nameOrTransport);
    if (index === -1) return null;
    const [transport] = this.transports.splice(index, 1);
    this._transportOptions.delete(transport);
    const adapter = this.runtime.dispatcher.remove(transport);
    if (options.close === true) adapter?.close?.(options).catch?.(() => {});
    return transport;
  }

  setLevel(level) { this.level = level; return this; }
  isLevelEnabled(level) { return this.runtime.isLevelEnabled(level); }
  registerLevel(definition, options) { this.runtime.registerLevel(definition, options); return this; }
  child(context, options) { return this.runtime.child(context, options); }
  with(context) { return this.runtime.with(context); }
  withTags(...tags) { return this.runtime.withTags(...tags); }
  channel(name) { return this.runtime.channel(name); }
  withError(error, context) { return this.runtime.withError(error, context); }
  when(condition) { return this.runtime.when(condition); }
  sample(rate) { return this.runtime.sample(rate); }
  once(key) { return this.runtime.once(key); }
  rateLimit(key, options) { return this.runtime.rateLimit(key, options); }
  at(level) { return this.runtime.at(level); }
  forRequest(req, extra) { return this.runtime.forRequest(req, extra); }
  forJob(job, extra) { return this.runtime.forJob(job, extra); }
  runWithContext(context, callback, ...args) { return this.runtime.runWithContext(context, callback, ...args); }
  getContext() { return this.runtime.getContext(); }
  time(label, context) { return this.runtime.time(label, context); }
  measure(label, operation, context, options) { return this.runtime.measure(label, operation, context, options); }
  health() { return this.runtime.health(); }
  stats() { return this.runtime.stats(); }
  metricsSnapshot() { return this.runtime.metricsSnapshot(); }
  diagnosticsSnapshot() { return this.runtime.diagnosticsSnapshot(); }
  levels() { return this.runtime.levels(); }
  prometheus(labels) { return this.runtime.prometheus(labels); }
  toPrometheus(labels) { return this.runtime.toPrometheus(labels); }

  async ready() { await this.runtime.ready(); return this; }
  async flush(options) { await this.runtime.flush(options); }
  async reopen() { return await this.runtime.reopen(); }

  async close(options) {
    if (this._closing) return;
    this._closing = true;
    await this.runtime.close(options);
    if (!this.readableEnded) this.push(null);
  }

  _transform(chunk, encoding, callback) {
    let entry = chunk;
    if (Buffer.isBuffer(entry)) entry = entry.toString(encoding || 'utf8');
    if (typeof entry === 'string') {
      try { entry = JSON.parse(entry); }
      catch { entry = { level: 'info', message: entry, formatted: entry }; }
    }
    const level = entry?.level && this.runtime.levelRegistry.has(entry.level) ? entry.level : 'info';
    this.runtime.logAsync(level, entry?.message ?? entry?.formatted ?? '', entry?.data ?? null, {
      context: entry?.context,
      error: entry?.error instanceof Error ? entry.error : undefined,
    }).then(() => callback(null, chunk), callback);
  }

  _flush(callback) { this.flush().then(() => callback(), callback); }
  _final(callback) { this.flush().then(() => callback(), callback); }
  _destroy(error, callback) { this.runtime.close().then(() => callback(error), () => callback(error)); }
}

export default AdvancedLogger;

'use strict';
import Transformer from "../../Transformer/index.js";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

// moved import for Transformer // Adjust the path as needed// moved import for fs// moved import for path

/**
 * Logging levels and their numeric severities.
 */
const LEVELS = {
  error: 0,
  warn: 1,
  info: 2,
  debug: 3
};

/**
 * AdvancedLogger class that extends the Transformer.
 * Log entries are processed via middleware before being forwarded to transports.
 *
 * @extends Transformer
 */
class AdvancedLogger extends Transformer {
  /**
   * Creates a new AdvancedLogger instance.
   * @param {Object} options - Configuration options.
   * @param {string} [options.level='info'] - Minimum log level.
   * @param {Array<Object>} [options.transports=[]] - List of transport instances.
   */
  constructor(options = {}) {
    // Initialize the Transformer in object mode
    super({ objectMode: true });
    this.level = options.level || 'info';
    this.transports = options.transports || [];
  }

  /**
   * Logs a message at the specified level.
   * The log entry is passed through any middleware before being written.
   *
   * @param {string} level - Log level ('error', 'warn', 'info', 'debug').
   * @param {string} message - The log message.
   */
  log(level, message) {
    if (LEVELS[level] > LEVELS[this.level]) return;
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
    const formatted = `[${timestamp}] [${level.toUpperCase()}] ${message}`;
    const logEntry = { timestamp, level, message, formatted };

    // Apply middleware transformation (if any)
    this.applyMiddleware(logEntry)
      .then((transformedEntry) => {
        // Write the transformed log entry into the stream.
        // This triggers the _transform method.
        this.write(transformedEntry);
        // Emit a 'log' event for observers.
        this.emit('log', transformedEntry);
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        console.error('Middleware error in AdvancedLogger:', err);
      });
  }

  /**
   * Convenience method for logging error messages.
   * @param {string} message - The error message.
   */
  error(message) {
    this.log('error', message);
  }

  /**
   * Convenience method for logging warning messages.
   * @param {string} message - The warning message.
   */
  warn(message) {
    this.log('warn', message);
  }

  /**
   * Convenience method for logging informational messages.
   * @param {string} message - The informational message.
   */
  info(message) {
    this.log('info', message);
  }

  /**
   * Convenience method for logging debug messages.
   * @param {string} message - The debug message.
   */
  debug(message) {
    this.log('debug', message);
  }

  /**
   * The _transform method is called when data is written to the stream.
   * It forwards the (transformed) log entry to all configured transports.
   *
   * @param {Object} logEntry - The transformed log entry.
   * @param {string} encoding - Not used in object mode.
   * @param {Function} callback - Callback to signal completion.
   * @private
   */
  _transform(logEntry, encoding, callback) {
    this.transports.forEach((transport) => {
      try {
        transport.write(logEntry.level, logEntry.formatted);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Transport error in AdvancedLogger:', err);
      }
    });
    // Optionally pass the log entry downstream.
    callback(null, logEntry);
  }
}

export default AdvancedLogger

'use strict';

import { Transform } from 'node:stream';

/**
 * Compatibility transport base. Existing Transform semantics are retained,
 * while flush/close/writeRecord make it usable by the enterprise dispatcher.
 */
class BaseTransport extends Transform {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super({ objectMode: true, autoDestroy: true, highWaterMark: options.highWaterMark || 16 });
    this.name = options.name || this.constructor.name;
    this.rawnodeTransportMode = 'legacy';
    this.rawnodeCompatibilityTransport = true;
    this.payloadMode = 'legacy';
    this.silent = options.silent === true;
    this._rnClosed = false;
    this.stats = { written: 0, errors: 0, lastError: null };
    // Prevent an unhandled EventEmitter "error" event from terminating an app.
    this.on('error', () => {});
  }

  _normalize(entry) {
    if (entry && typeof entry === 'object') return entry;
    if (typeof entry === 'string') {
      try { return JSON.parse(entry); }
      catch { return { timestamp: '', level: '', message: entry, formatted: entry, data: null }; }
    }
    const text = String(entry ?? '');
    return { timestamp: '', level: '', message: text, formatted: text, data: null };
  }

  _safeEmit(event, ...args) {
    try { this.emit(event, ...args); }
    catch (error) {
      this.stats.errors++;
      this.stats.lastError = { message: error?.message || String(error), at: Date.now() };
      try { process.stderr.write(`[RawNodeLogger:TRANSPORT] ${event}: ${error?.message || error}\n`); } catch {}
    }
  }

  writeRecord(record) {
    if (this._rnClosed) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`Transport [${this.name}] is closed.`), { code: 'LOGGER_TRANSPORT_CLOSED' }));
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      try {
        const accepted = this.write(record, (error) => error ? reject(error) : resolve(true));
        if (accepted === false) this.once('drain', () => resolve(true));
      } catch (error) { reject(error); }
    });
  }

  async flush() {
    if (this.writableNeedDrain) await new Promise((resolve) => this.once('drain', resolve));
  }

  async close() {
    if (this._rnClosed) return;
    await this.flush();
    this._rnClosed = true;
    if (!this.writableEnded && !this.destroyed) await new Promise((resolve, reject) => this.end((error) => error ? reject(error) : resolve()));
  }

  health() {
    return { name: this.name, status: this._rnClosed ? 'closed' : this.stats.lastError ? 'degraded' : 'healthy', stats: { ...this.stats } };
  }
}

export default BaseTransport;
